TL;DR: 论文提出 QuAKE（Quantization-Aware Kalman Estimator），将量化去噪器的采样过程建模为在线估计问题，利用量化输出的历史信息恢复全精度去噪输出。该方法基于卡尔曼估计器，融合平滑轨迹先验与条件高斯观测模型，以闭式递推方式更新输出窗口后验，并将后验均值反馈给采样器，是一种无需修改量化网络的轻量即插即用校正器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型在部署时面临显著的内存与计算开销，量化是降低开销的实用途径，但激进量化（如 W4A4）会使量化模型的输出严重偏离全精度对应输出，导致采样轨迹退化。已有的采样阶段校正方法主要依赖局部信息，未能充分利用轨迹历史，因而难以纠正跨时间步传播的累积误差。论文的动机即在于利用去噪器输出的历史信息来抑制这种随采样步传播的误差，从而缩小量化采样与全精度采样之间的分布差距。

Method: 论文将"使用量化去噪器进行采样"形式化为在线估计问题：目标是依据量化去噪器输出的历史序列，估计采样器所需的全精度去噪器输出。为此提出 QuAKE（Quantization-Aware Kalman Estimator），其核心由两部分构成：（1）平滑轨迹先验，用以刻画去噪输出沿采样轨迹的时序平滑性；（2）条件高斯观测模型，用以建模量化输出与全精度输出之间的观测关系。在每个采样步，QuAKE 对输出窗口的后验分布进行闭式递归更新（卡尔曼滤波式的预测—更新），并将后验均值送入采样器，以替代直接使用量化输出。QuAKE 作为即插即用校正模块，无需对量化网络做任何修改，且天然兼容任意高阶多步 ODE 采样器（即不限于一阶或单步采样）。

Result: 论文在 W4A4 量化的文本到图像扩散模型上开展实验，评价指标为量化采样相对于全精度采样在分布上的偏差（distributional discrepancy）。实验结果表明，QuAKE 在减小该分布偏差方面持续优于现有采样阶段校正方法。摘要未给出具体数据集名称、模型架构、数值指标或消融细节，因此无法进一步量化其性能增益。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于：将量化扩散采样重新表述为在线状态估计问题，并提出基于卡尔曼估计的 QuAKE 校正器，通过利用输出窗口历史与轨迹先验实现闭式递推校正；其即插即用、免网络修改、兼容高阶多步 ODE 采样器的特性使其具备良好的通用性与实用性；在 W4A4 文生图模型上验证了其在降低分布偏差上的有效性。局限方面：摘要仅报告了 W4A4 文生图场景，未涉及其它量化配置（如 W8A8）、其它任务（如视频生成）或其它模态；所依赖的高斯观测与平滑轨迹先验假设在多大范围内成立尚不明确；同时卡尔曼递推带来的额外采样开销与实时性影响也未在摘要中说明。

Abstract: Quantization offers a practical path to deploying diffusion models with reduced memory and computation, but aggressive compression can cause quantized outputs to deviate substantially from their full-precision counterparts. Sampling-stage correction methods seek to compensate for such deviations during sampling, but existing approaches rely primarily on local information and underexploit trajectory history, limiting their ability to correct errors that propagate across timesteps. In this work, we formulate sampling with a quantized denoiser as an online estimation problem, using the history of quantized denoiser outputs to recover the underlying full-precision outputs required by the sampler. We propose QuAKE, a Quantization-Aware Kalman Estimator that combines a smooth trajectory prior with a conditional Gaussian observation model. At each sampling step, QuAKE recursively updates the posterior over the output window in closed form and feeds its posterior mean to the sampler. QuAKE is a lightweight plug-and-play corrector that requires no modification to the quantized network and naturally supports arbitrary high-order multistep ODE samplers. Experiments across W4A4-quantized text-to-image diffusion models show that QuAKE consistently outperforms existing methods in reducing the distributional discrepancy from full-precision sampling.

</details>


### [30] [When Online Adaptation Hurts: Parameter-Frozen Test-Time Ensembling for Continual Medical Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21412)
*Ruijie Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出"参数冻结的推理增强"(Parameter-frozen Inference Enhancement, PIE),作为医学图像持续测试时适应(CTTA)的替代方案:不更新模型权重与归一化统计量,而是对源域训练好的分割器施加保解剖结构的缩放与翻转视图,将各视图预测映射回原位置后对概率求平均。在M&Ms心脏MRI跨厂商(vendor A训练,顺序评估B/C/D)流式场景下,PIE取得0.7786平均Dice,优于源域推理0.7680及五种在线适应基线(0.7388–0.7416)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学图像分割器在站点、扫描仪厂商或成像协议变化时性能会显著下降。持续测试时适应(CTTA)试图在无目标标签条件下缓解该问题,但在非平稳数据流上在线更新模型既可能不可行,也容易累积错误。作者因此重新审视问题设定,提出更务实、更合理的"参数冻结推理增强"范式,探索在不改动模型参数与归一化统计量的前提下提升跨域鲁棒性,为医学CTTA提供稳定可复现的基线。

Method: 方法核心为测试时多视图集成推理:(1)使用源域训练得到的分割器,保持权重与归一化统计量完全冻结;(2)应用一组保解剖结构(anatomy-preserving)的几何变换,包括缩放(scale)与翻转(flip)构造多视图输入;(3)对各视图预测经逆变换映射回原始(native)空间;(4)在概率层面进行平均融合得到最终预测。作者未引入任何在线梯度更新、伪标签自训练或手工后处理。

Result: 在M&Ms心脏MRI数据集上构建跨厂商持续流:训练于厂商A,依次在B、C、D上评估。PIE平均Dice为0.7786,优于仅源域推理(0.7680)与五个在线适应基线(0.7388–0.7416)。消融实验表明:性能在约28个视图时饱和;置信度加权、类别先验校正、连通域滤波、形态学细化以及层间平滑均无明显增益,甚至造成负迁移。在心脏MRI与眼底图像上的定性结果一致显示,冻结集成能更好地保留更细及嵌套的解剖结构。

Conclusion: 论文主要贡献:提出parameter-frozen inference enhancement设定与多视图概率融合方法,为医学图像CTTA提供强有力的稳定基线;并通过系统消融揭示一个重要失效模式——在线适应与手工精修可能比精心设计的推理更不可靠。意义在于质疑复杂在线适应的必要性,强调推理阶段设计价值。局限:仅在心脏MRI(与少量定性眼底)上验证;多视图推理带来约28倍计算开销,未讨论实时性;缩放/翻转变换的适用性依赖解剖结构假设,可能不适用于任意模态或非刚性形变场景;未覆盖参数更新确实必要的情形。

Abstract: Medical image segmenters often get worse when sites, scanner vendors, or protocols change. Continual test-time adaptation (CTTA) addresses this problem without target labels, but it can be impossible to update a model on a non-stationary stream and can lead to a lot of errors. We examine a more reasonable and meaningful alternative: parameter-frozen inference enhancement(PIE). We use a source-trained segmenter that learns about anatomy-preserving scale and flip views, maps their predictions back to the native location, and averages the probabilities. We do not modify the weights of the model or the normalization statistics. On a cardiac MRI stream from M\&Ms, which is trained on vendor A and evaluated sequentially on vendors B, C, and D, PIE has 0.7786 mean Dice, compared to 0.7680 for source-only inference and 0.7388--0.7416 for five other online-adaptation baselines. The controlled ablations show that performance saturates at 28 views, and confidence weighting, class-prior correction, connected-component filtering, morphological refinement, and inter-slice smoothing have no effect or cause negative transfer. Qualitative results on cardiac MRI and fundus images are also consistent with the frozen ensemble keeping thinner and nested anatomical structures. These results provide a strong, stable baseline for medical CTTA and expose an important failure mode: adaptation and handcrafted refinement can be less reliable than carefully designed inference.

</details>


### [31] [P$^3$-SAM: SAM with Perceptual Parallel Prompt for Few-Shot Strip Steel Surface Defect Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21424)
*Qian Xu,Hang Xiong,Anpeng Wang,Sam Kwong,Cong Zhang,Runmin Cong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: "论文提出P³-SAM框架，通过感知优化编码（POE）与并行提示生成器（PPG）两大策略适配Segment Anything Model（SAM），以解决条钢表面缺陷（S³D）小样本语义分割中低对比度、光照不均和细粒度纹理等工业场景难题，在三个基准上取得SOTA，Surface Defects-4i上mIoU提升12.00%。"


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: "小样本语义分割（FSS）在条钢表面缺陷（S³D）场景中面临与自然图像截然不同的挑战：缺陷区域局部对比度低、光照分布不均、纹理模式呈现复杂细粒度特征。尽管基于SAM的方法在自然图像FSS任务中借助其强大的预训练表征取得进展，但直接迁移至工业缺陷场景时，由于上述工业图像特性与自然图像分布差异显著，SAM的通用表征难以捕捉细粒度缺陷线索，导致性能明显下降。因此，如何在不依赖大量标注的前提下，使SAM有效适配工业缺陷的细粒度、低对比度分割需求，是一个有实际价值且尚未解决的问题。"

Method: "论文提出P³-SAM（Perceptual Parallel Prompt-SAM）框架，核心是在SAM基础上引入两项策略。其一是感知优化编码（Perceptual-Optimized Encoding, POE）：通过对输入进行感知层面的增强，提升缺陷区域的局部对比度并保留对分割至关重要的纹理细节，从而弥补工业图像低对比度与细粒度纹理带来的表征损失，使SAM的编码器能获得更有利于缺陷辨识的特征。其二是并行提示生成器（Parallel Prompt Generator, PPG）：针对不同输入图像，同时生成语义提示（semantic prompts）与空间提示（spatial prompts）两类并行提示，为SAM解码器提供更全面、互补的引导，克服单一提示形式难以适应图像间差异的问题。整体上，P³将SAM从自然图像范式适配到工业FSS任务，通过输入感知增强与多类型提示生成联合驱动分割解码。"

Result: "论文在三个小样本S³D基准数据集上进行了实验，评估指标为mIoU。结果显示P³-SAM取得state-of-the-art性能，其中在Surface Defects-4i数据集上mIoU提升达12.00%，表明所提POE与PPG策略能够有效提升SAM在工业缺陷小样本分割任务上的表现。摘要中未披露更详细的数据集规模、主干配置、对比方法与消融实验细节。"

Conclusion: "论文的主要贡献是识别并针对S³D工业缺陷小样本分割的独特难点（低局部对比度、光照不均、细粒度纹理），提出P³-SAM框架，通过POE增强感知编码、通过PPG并行生成语义与空间提示，使SAM适配工业缺陷场景，并在三个基准上取得SOTA（Surface Defects-4i mIoU +12.00%）。其意义在于为工业表面缺陷的小样本分割提供了一条基于大模型提示调优的有效路径。潜在局限包括：方法依赖SAM的预训练表征，可能带来计算开销；实验仅覆盖条钢表面缺陷，向其他工业缺陷类型的泛化性尚待验证；摘要亦未说明推理效率与实时性。"

Abstract: Few-shot semantic segmentation (FSS) of strip steel surface defects (S$^3$D) has posed significant challenges distinct from natural scenes. Unlike natural images, S$^3$D task exhibits unique characteristics including low local contrast, uneven illumination, and complex fine-grained texture patterns. Although recent methods based on Segment Anything Model (SAM) have shown promise in FSS on natural images by leveraging SAM's powerful pre-trained representations, these unique industrial characteristics of S$^3$D images lead to performance drop when directly applying SAM to industrial defect scenarios. In this paper, we propose a novel Perceptual Parallel Prompt (P$^3$) framework that empowers SAM, creating the P$^3$-SAM model to address these challenges through two core strategies. First, we develop a Perceptual-Optimized Encoding (POE) strategy that enhances local contrast and preserves critical texture details for S$^3$D segmentation. Second, we introduce the Parallel Prompt Generator (PPG) strategy that simultaneously generates both semantic and spatial prompts, enabling comprehensive guidance for SAM's decoder across varying images. Extensive experiments on three few-shot S$^3$D benchmarks demonstrate that P$^3$-SAM achieves state-of-the-art performance, with particularly notable improvements of 12.00% in mIoU on Surface Defects-4i dataset.

</details>


### [32] [Think Locally, Refine Globally for Memory-Efficient 3D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21437)
*Jingke Zhou,Chenhang Ma,Zhizhou Zhong,Mingkai Liu,Zhuang Zhou,Yicheng ji,Binghua Su,Bo Cai,Xianliang Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LoG-VGGT 是一个面向长序列三维重建的内存高效框架，通过仅在少量 Transformer 模块引入跨窗口注意力，并结合基于寄存器（register）token 的全局相机一致性精化模块，在受限显存下实现局部时序建模与全局相机一致性的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长序列三维重建（如流式/增量式场景重建）需要在长时间跨度上同时保证深度精度与相机位姿一致性。然而，现有方法依赖全序列全局注意力来维持全局一致性，其显存开销随序列长度呈二次增长，难以扩展到长序列；而仅使用局部窗口注意力虽然显存可控，却会导致跨窗口信息传递受限，并引发长期位姿漂移（pose drift）。因此，如何在有界显存下兼顾局部时序建模与全局相机一致性，是该工作的核心动机。

Method: LoG-VGGT 的核心设计包含两部分。其一为跨窗口注意力（cross-window attention）机制：并不在所有 Transformer 模块中做全局注意力，而只在少量（a small subset of）Transformer 模块中引入跨窗口注意力，使信息能够在相邻时序窗口之间传播，同时将显存占用控制在有界范围内，从而在局部时序建模与跨窗口信息流动之间取得折中。其二为全局相机一致性精化模块（global camera consistency refinement module）：相机 token（camera tokens）通过交叉注意力与紧凑的寄存器 token（register tokens）交互，利用紧凑的寄存器表示在整个序列上施加场景级约束，从而在避免序列级全局注意力高开销的前提下，实现相机表示与场景约束的联合优化（joint optimization），缓解长期位姿漂移并提升长时序位姿稳定性。

Result: 论文在多个长序列基准（long-sequence benchmarks）上进行了大量实验，评估指标涵盖深度精度与相机位姿估计。结果表明：LoG-VGGT 在多个长序列基准上取得了更优的深度精度以及稳健的相机位姿估计，同时在流式（streaming）重建任务上具有竞争力的性能表现。摘要未给出具体数据集名称、序列长度、数值指标或消融细节。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于提出一种兼顾内存效率与全局一致性的长序列三维重建框架：通过稀疏化跨窗口注意力的引入位置来限制显存增长，并通过寄存器 token 中介的交叉注意力实现全序列场景级约束与相机表示的联合优化，从而改善长时序位姿稳定性。其意义在于为流式/长序列重建提供了一条在有限显存下维持全局一致性的可行路径。潜在局限包括：摘要未披露具体量化结果与消融分析；寄存器 token 与跨窗口模块的放置策略、以及序列长度的可扩展上限仍需实证验证；长序列位姿漂移是否被完全抑制亦有待更长时间跨度的评估。

Abstract: We propose LoG-VGGT, a memory-efficient framework for long-sequence 3D reconstruction that balances local temporal modeling with global camera consistency. Instead of relying on full global attention, our method introduces cross-window attention at a small subset of transformer blocks, enabling effective information propagation across adjacent temporal windows while keeping memory usage bounded. To mitigate long-term pose drift, we further design a global camera consistency refinement module, where camera tokens interact with compact register tokens via cross-attention to enforce scene-level constraints across the entire sequence. This design enables joint optimization of camera representations and significantly improves long-horizon pose stability without incurring the high cost of sequence-wide attention. Extensive experiments demonstrate that LoG-VGGT achieves improved depth accuracy and robust camera pose estimation across multiple long-sequence benchmarks, while delivering competitive streaming reconstruction performance.

</details>


### [33] [ME-Dex 1.0: Bringing Heterogeneous Tactile Sensing into World Action Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21449)
*Xuancheng Zhang,Xuetao Liu,Qianying Tang,Jizhe Wang,Zhijing Cheng,Bochen Lin,Haoran Wen,Ming Li,Kun Zhan,Yu Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ME-Dex-1.0 提出统一的 World Action Tactile Model，将触觉信号与视频一样视为未来世界状态的观测，通过 Mixture-of-Transformers 架构统一联合建模视觉、触觉与动作，实现机器人操作中的多模态联合去噪与动作生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: World Action Models 借助视频预测能力为机器人动作生成提供未来视觉状态基础，而触觉传感可直接测量物理交互。现有方法多将触觉特征作为条件输入，而未将未来触觉状态与视觉观测、动作进行联合预测。作者的核心洞察是：触觉信号与视频类似，都是对演化中世界状态的观测，因此应作为未来观测与视频一并建模，从而更充分地利用物理交互信息。此外，多源异构触觉输入以及配对视觉-触觉-动作数据稀缺也是该方向的关键障碍。

Method: ME-Dex-1.0（MachEmbodied-Dex-1.0）采用 Mixture-of-Transformers 架构，包含 Video Expert、Tactile Expert 与 Action Expert 三个专家模块，均以 flow matching 训练。专家之间通过中间层的共享注意力（shared attention）连接，使动作生成在联合去噪过程中能够利用学习到的视觉与触觉动态表征。针对多源异构触觉输入，方法引入 Canonical Hand Model 与 Unified Tactile Autoencoder，将不同本体与传感布局的触觉观测映射到共享的空间与潜在空间。为缓解配对数据不足，作者构建了 Agentic Tactile Data Engine，这是一个基于 agent 的数据生成平台，在仿真轨迹回放过程中直接采集力传感器触觉数据，为 RoboTwin 与 DexJoCo 补充触觉信息。

Result: 实验在 RoboTwin、DexJoCo 与 ManiFeel 三个仿真平台以及真实机器人上进行评估，涵盖配备触觉传感的夹爪与灵巧手。结果表明，使用触觉传感后操作性能得到提升，验证了将触觉作为未来观测进行联合建模的有效性，以及 Canonical Hand Model 与 Unified Tactile Autoencoder 在跨本体、跨传感布局上的泛化能力，同时体现了 Agentic Tactile Data Engine 在补充配对数据方面的作用。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于提出统一的视觉-触觉-动作 World Action Tactile Model、基于共享注意力的 Mixture-of-Transformers 联合 flow matching 框架、跨本体统一触觉编码方案，以及 agent 驱动的触觉数据生成引擎。其意义在于将触觉提升为与视频对等的未来观测模态，推动多模态世界模型驱动的机器人操作。潜在局限可能包括对仿真触觉数据与真实传感器分布的差异依赖，以及多专家架构与统一触觉自编码器带来的训练与部署复杂度。

Abstract: World Action Models bring the predictive capabilities of video models into robot action generation, providing a rich foundation for modeling future visual states. Tactile sensing complements this foundation with direct measurements of physical interaction. Some existing methods use tactile features as conditioning inputs without jointly predicting future tactile states, visual observations, and actions. Our key insight is that tactile signals, like video, provide observations of the evolving world state and should be modeled as future observations alongside video. We present ME-Dex-1.0 (MachEmbodied-Dex-1.0), a unified World Action Tactile Model for joint visual, tactile, and action learning. ME-Dex-1.0 adopts a Mixture-of-Transformers architecture comprising a Video Expert, a Tactile Expert, and an Action Expert, all trained with flow matching. We use shared attention connects the experts in intermediate layers, allowing action generation to draw on learned representations of visual and tactile dynamics during joint denoising. To support multi-source heterogeneous tactile inputs, a Canonical Hand Model and a Unified Tactile Autoencoder map tactile observations from different embodiments and sensing layouts into shared spatial and latent spaces. To address the limited availability of paired visual, tactile, and action data, we develop the Agentic Tactile Data Engine, an agent-based data production platform. It supplements RoboTwin and DexJoCo with tactile data recorded directly from force sensors during trajectory replay in simulation. Experiments on the RoboTwin, DexJoCo, and ManiFeel simulation platforms, together with real robot evaluations, demonstrate improved manipulation performance using both grippers and dexterous hands equipped with tactile sensing.

</details>


### [34] [CompAdapt: Adaptable Composite Motion Modeling for Physics-Consistent Text-to-Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21455)
*Haoran Qin,Renlong Wu,Tianyu Huang,Yukang Ding,Hui Li,Wangmeng Zuo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CompAdapt 是一个面向物理一致性文本到视频（T2V）生成的框架，将神经动力学建模从单一运动类型扩展到复合物理行为（耦合运动、多阶段转换、多物体碰撞），并通过语言提示实现运动类型、时序关系与初始物理参数的结构化端到端指定，同时支持无需重训练的 one-shot 物理环境适配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散式 T2V 模型虽能生成真实且时序连贯的视频，但常常违反基本物理动力学规律。近期引入显式动力学先验的物理约束方法虽提升了物理合理性，但存在三方面局限：(1) 仅适用于单一类型的简单运动；(2) 依赖人工指定的参数；(3) 难以泛化到未见过的物理规律。因此，在复杂真实场景下实现可适配、物理一致的 T2V 生成仍是一个未解决的问题。

Method: CompAdapt 的核心设计包含四个部分：(1) 将神经动力学建模从单一运动类型扩展到复合物理行为，涵盖耦合运动、多阶段转换与多物体碰撞；(2) 通过将自然语言提示翻译为结构化的物理语义表示，实现对运动类型、时序关系和初始物理参数的端到端指定；(3) 引入 dynamics-aware prior matching（动力学感知先验匹配）机制，可在不重训练核心动力学模块的前提下实现 one-shot 适配，从而泛化到新颖物理环境；(4) 提出 physics-aware latent feature fusion（物理感知隐特征融合）模块，提升快速与复杂运动下的视觉保真度。

Result: 论文在面向物理的 T2V 基准上进行实验评估，结果表明 CompAdapt 在物理一致性上优于通用 T2V 模型和物理约束类基线方法，同时在视觉质量和针对未见动力学的适配能力上保持较高水平。摘要未给出具体量化指标、数据集名称或消融细节，相关数值需参考原文及项目页面。

Conclusion: CompAdapt 的主要贡献在于：把物理约束的 T2V 生成从单一运动类型推进到复合物理行为，实现了语言驱动的结构化物理语义指定，并通过先验匹配支持无需重训练的 one-shot 动力学适配，同时兼顾视觉保真度。其意义在于提升生成视频的物理可信度与跨场景可迁移性。潜在局限包括：摘要未披露复合物理行为的覆盖范围与复杂度上限、one-shot 适配对先验库规模的依赖、以及物理一致性评估指标的客观性与可扩展性仍有待原文验证。

Abstract: While diffusion-based text-to-video (T2V) models have demonstrated impressive capability in generating realistic and temporally coherent videos, they often fail to respect fundamental physical dynamics. Although recent physics-constrained methods incorporate explicit dynamics priors to improve physical plausibility, they remain limited to simple single-type motions, depend on manually specified parameters, and struggle to generalize to unseen physical laws. In this work, we propose CompAdapt, a physics-consistent T2V framework for adaptable generation across complex real-world scenarios. It extends neural dynamics modeling beyond single-type motions to encompass composite physical behaviors, including coupled motions, multi-stage transitions, and multi-object collisions. Furthermore, CompAdapt translates natural language prompts into structured physical semantics, enabling end-to-end specification of motion types, temporal relations, and initial physical parameters. To generalize to novel physical environments, CompAdapt introduces dynamics-aware prior matching, achieving one-shot adaptation without retraining the core dynamics module. In addition, a physics-aware latent feature fusion module improves visual fidelity under fast and complex motion. Experiments on physics-focused T2V benchmarks demonstrate that CompAdapt improves physical consistency over both general T2V models and physics-constrained baselines, while preserving high visual quality and adaptability to unseen dynamics. The project page is available at https://makapic.github.io/CompAdapt/ .

</details>


### [35] [PSEE: Progressive Sensor Event Expansion for Point-Supervised Temporal Action Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21462)
*Jiaxi Yin,Ge Wang,Han Ding,Fei Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PSEE（Progressive Sensor Event Expansion）面向点监督下的可穿戴传感器时间动作定位（TAL），仅用每个动作实例的单一时间戳与类别标签，通过融合语义激活、传感器特异的转换证据与自适应时间归属，逐步扩展生成伪片段边界，从而以标准 TAL 检测器完成训练，且不改变其推理流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可穿戴传感器流的时序动作定位比传统动作识别提供更细粒度的活动理解（同时给出动作类别与起止时间），但常规训练依赖逐实例的起止点标注，代价高昂且主观性强。点监督（每个实例仅一个时间戳+类别）可大幅降低标注成本，但如何从单点标签恢复出可靠的伪起止边界、从而有效监督 TAL 检测器，是该设定下的核心难题。论文即针对点监督 TAL 中的伪边界质量问题展开研究。

Method: 论文提出 PSEE 框架，用于在点监督下恢复伪片段（pseudo segments），主要包含三个互补机制：(1) 语义激活（semantic activations）——利用类别相关的响应信号定位实例可能覆盖的时间范围；(2) 传感器特异的转换证据（sensor-specific transition evidence）——借助惯性等传感器信号在动作转换处的物理/统计变化特征来推断边界位置；(3) 自适应时间归属（adaptive temporal ownership）——对时间点归属于哪个动作实例进行动态分配。三者以"渐进扩展（progressive expansion）"的方式组合，从单点标签出发逐步扩展出伪起止区间。生成的伪片段直接用于监督标准 TAL 检测器，检测器的网络结构与推理过程均不做修改，因而具备与现有 TAL 方法解耦的通用性。

Result: 在四个惯性传感（inertial-sensing）基准上进行了跨被试（cross-subject）实验。结果显示：相比经过适配的点监督基线，PSEE 生成的伪边界质量更高；方法可与不同 TAL 检测器兼容（即作为通用伪标签生成模块提升多种检测器）；同时对点采样方式（point sampling）具有鲁棒性。评测围绕伪边界质量、与不同检测器的兼容性以及采样鲁棒性展开。代码已开源。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于提出 PSEE，一种面向可穿戴传感器点监督 TAL 的伪片段生成框架，通过语义激活、传感器转换证据与自适应时间归属的渐进式融合，缓解了点监督下边界信息缺失的问题，并在不改变检测器推理流程的前提下提升多种 TAL 检测器性能，且对点采样稳健。其意义在于降低细粒度时序动作定位的标注成本，并可即插即用地适配现有 TAL 检测器。局限方面：论文摘要未给出具体数值结果与消融细节，方法依赖传感器转换证据的可用性，目前验证局限于惯性传感基准，向其他模态（如事件相机、视频流）的迁移性尚未说明。

Abstract: Temporal action localization (TAL) in wearable sensor streams identifies action classes and temporal boundaries, enabling finer-grained activity understanding than conventional action recognition. However, training typically requires costly start--end annotations for every action instance. To reduce this burden, we study point-supervised TAL, where each instance is labeled with only one timestamp and its class. We propose Progressive Sensor Event Expansion (PSEE), which combines semantic activations, sensor-specific transition evidence, and adaptive temporal ownership to recover point-supervised pseudo segments. These segments supervise standard TAL detectors without modifying their inference procedures. Cross-subject experiments on four inertial-sensing benchmarks demonstrate improved pseudo-boundary quality over adapted point-supervised baselines, compatibility with different TAL detectors, and robustness to point sampling. Code is available at https://github.com/joeeeeyin/PSEE.

</details>


### [36] [SkillIR: Evolving Scene-Aware Skills for Agentic Image Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21468)
*Jie Shao,Shengkai Hu,Xu Zhang,Beihang Song,Yongcheng Jing,Xu Wu,Jun Wan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出SkillIR，一种技能引导的智能体图像复原框架。它不将复原经验表示为完整的工具使用轨迹，而是表示为"以退化为中心的动作证据"，并将其整合为场景感知的复原技能，在经验证的残差状态循环中逐步引导单步有界动作，从而提升复杂多重退化的复原质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂退化图像需要多模态智能体协调调用多种专用复原工具。现有复原智能体通常从原始退化图像一次性推导完整工具使用计划，或检索此前成功的完整轨迹，难以根据不断演化的中间复原状态调整单个动作。作者发现：一次被接受的工具执行会改变残差退化状态，进而影响后续工具的适用性，因此静态的全局计划或轨迹级检索无法应对这种状态依赖的动态变化。该问题对于真实场景中退化类型复杂、相互叠加的图像复原尤为重要。

Method: SkillIR将复原经验表示为"以退化为中心的动作证据"而非完整轨迹。方法首先把上下文相关的动作结果整合为场景感知的复原技能，每个技能刻画适用条件、预期效果以及可归因的失败情形。推理时并不预先制定完整复原计划，而是由检索到的技能在"经验证的残差状态循环"中一次只引导一个有界动作：每个工具的输出被视为候选，只有通过转移验证（transition verification）后才被提交，随后重新评估当前活跃的残差退化。每一次rollout产生的证据被用于创建、细化或修补动态技能，使积累的复原经验能持续改进针对后续输入的决策，形成自演化的技能库。

Result: 论文在合成的和真实世界的多重退化数据集上进行实验，结果表明SkillIR提升了复原质量，并实现了更可靠、更有效的工具使用。摘要未给出具体的定量指标、基线与数值结果细节。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于提出以退化为中心的动作证据表示替代轨迹级经验，通过技能层级的单步引导、转移验证与残差状态再评估，以及动态技能的自演化，缓解了多工具复原中状态依赖的适应性问题，并在多重退化数据上验证了有效性。潜在局限在于：摘要未报告具体量化结果与效率开销；技能库的创建、验证与维护依赖转移验证机制，其可靠性可能影响整体鲁棒性；方法的泛化能力与在实时/边缘部署场景下的适用性也未在摘要中说明。

Abstract: This paper studies agentic image restoration, in which multimodal agents coordinate specialized restoration tools to recover images affected by complex degradations. Existing restoration agents often derive complete tool-use plans from the original degraded image or retrieve previously successful trajectories, providing limited support for adapting individual actions to evolving intermediate restoration states. We find that accepted tool executions can change the residual degradation state and, consequently, the applicability of subsequent tools. To address this issue, we propose SkillIR, a skill-guided framework that represents restoration experience as degradation-centered action evidence rather than complete tool-use trajectories. SkillIR consolidates context-dependent action outcomes into scene-aware restoration skills that characterize applicable conditions, expected effects, and attributable failure cases. Instead of prescribing a complete restoration plan, the retrieved skills guide one bounded action at a time within a verified residual-state loop: each tool output is treated as a candidate, committed only after transition verification, and followed by reassessment of the active residual degradations. After each rollout, the resulting evidence is used to create, refine, or patch dynamic skills, enabling accumulated restoration experience to improve decision-making for subsequent inputs. Experiments on synthetic and real-world multi-degradation datasets demonstrate that SkillIR improves restoration quality and enables more reliable and effective tool use.

</details>


### [37] [MT-WAM: Reorienting the One-Pass Predictive Representation Toward Action Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21474)
*Yiguang Yang,Jiankun Peng,Xiaoming Wang,Yiran Zhang,Zhibo Fang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MT-WAM在Fast-WAM（视频-动作协同训练但推理时不生成未来视频）基础上，保留原训练目标并额外引入未来二维点轨迹与未来视觉特征的监督信号，用轻量双流分支对视频骨干末段做目标特化处理，从而在不增加推理开销的前提下提升动作生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Fast-WAM证明视频-动作协同训练可仅凭单次视频扩散Transformer前向的表示来驱动动作生成，无需在推理时生成未来视频。但论文指出：未来观测预测目标并未显式强调控制所需的未来动态与视觉结构，因此从该表示中提取的信息对动作生成并非最优。目标是让视频骨干学到更具控制价值、对视觉条件变化更鲁棒的视觉上下文表示，同时保持推理阶段的高效率（跳过未来视频预测）。

Method: 核心思路是保留Fast-WAM原本的训练目标，额外增加两类互补监督：（1）未来二维点轨迹预测，显式建模运动动态；（2）未来视觉特征预测，提供捕获物体与空间结构的特征空间监督。实现上，从视频骨干最后若干block复制出一个轻量双流分支，对两类目标分别做特化处理；通过结构化注意力掩码阻止跨流注意力，保证分支间互不干扰。运动流token被用作动作专家（action expert）的额外动态条件；而未来视觉特征预测的监督仅用于训练视频骨干、提升其提供给动作生成的视觉上下文信息量，并不会把视觉特征流token加入动作条件，因此不引入额外条件开销。推理阶段，MT-WAM每个replan仅计算一次视频缓存与运动缓存，并跳过未来视频预测，保持与Fast-WAM相同的低推理成本。

Result: 在无需额外具身策略预训练的前提下：LIBERO成功率98.2%；LIBERO-Plus成功率73.7%，相对Fast-WAM提升23.8个百分点；RoboTwin 2.0 Clean2Rand上Random设置成功率由6.30%升至19.40%；四个真实世界任务平均成功率由67.0%升至77.8%。实验表明轨迹与视觉特征监督在不同视觉条件变化下均带来一致性增益，尤其在分布偏移/OOD（如Clean2Rand、LIBERO-Plus）场景下提升显著。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于证明了对未来动态（点轨迹）与未来视觉结构（特征）的显式互补监督能提升视频扩散骨干所提供表示的“控制可用性”，并在不增加推理时未来视频生成、不引入额外策略预训练的情况下取得大幅性能提升。局限方面：方法依赖视频扩散Transformer骨干与双流改动，训练复杂度与显存需增加（训练时多目标+分支），2D点轨迹监督对三维精细操作的表征能力有限；推理效率与Fast-WAM相当但并未降低；评估集中在操作类任务（LIBERO/RoboTwin及四类真机任务），对长时程、多模态或动态相机场景的泛化性有待进一步验证。

Abstract: Fast-WAM shows that video-action co-training improves control without generating future video at inference, making the representation from a single video diffusion Transformer forward central to action generation. However, future-observation prediction does not explicitly prioritize the future dynamics and visual structure needed for control. We present MT-WAM, which retains the original training objectives and adds complementary supervision for future two-dimensional point trajectories and visual features. A lightweight dual-stream branch copied from the video backbone's final blocks provides target-specific processing, while a structured attention mask prevents cross-stream attention. Motion-stream tokens supply additional dynamics conditions to the action expert. Future visual-feature prediction provides supervision in a feature space that captures object and spatial structure. This supervision trains the video backbone to provide more informative visual context for action generation under changing visual conditions, without adding visual-feature-stream tokens to action conditioning. At inference, MT-WAM uses video and motion caches computed once per replan and skips future-video prediction. Without additional embodied policy pretraining, MT-WAM achieves 98.2% success on LIBERO and 73.7% on LIBERO-Plus, exceeding Fast-WAM by 23.8 percentage points on the latter. On RoboTwin 2.0 Clean2Rand, Random success increases from 6.30% to 19.40%; across four real-world tasks, average success increases from 67.0% to 77.8%.

</details>


### [38] [OpenSAL360: Open-Source Crowdsourcing Platform for Omnidirectional Video Saliency Collection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21480)
*Alexey Bryncev,Andrey Moskalenko,Kira Shilovskaya,Ivan Kosmynin,Dmitriy Vatolin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 OpenSAL360，一个开源、低成本、可扩展的 360° 视频显著性数据采集平台，仅需普通屏幕、鼠标和网络连接即可进行众包标注，无需 VR 头显与眼动仪。作者据此采集并公开了涵盖 500 段全景视频、由 2000+ 众包标注者参与的数据集，据称是该领域最大规模的数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全向（360°）视频显著性预测对视口自适应流媒体与压缩、注视点渲染、网格简化、感知质量评估等沉浸式多媒体应用至关重要。然而，该领域进展受限于数据瓶颈：传统协议依赖 VR 头显与眼动追踪设备采集注视数据，成本高、流程复杂，难以大规模扩展，导致数据集规模有限，限制了显著性预测模型的训练与评估。因此，亟需一种低成本、可并行、可规模化的显著性数据采集方案。

Method: 核心贡献是 OpenSAL360 采集平台：用标准屏幕 + 鼠标 + 互联网连接替代 VR 眼动追踪，使普通众包标注者可在无专用硬件条件下并行贡献显著性数据，从而大幅降低单样本采集成本并提升吞吐。方法上包含完整的采集协议设计（界面交互方式、前处理与后处理参数），并通过消融实验系统分析关键接口参数、预处理与后处理参数对采集质量的影响。作者将所提协议与七个成熟的 VR 眼动追踪数据集进行对比验证，以证明众包标注结果与传统 VR 眼动数据的一致性和有效性。平台与数据开源发布于 GitHub。

Result: 实验验证分两部分：一是与七个已有 VR 眼动追踪数据集进行协议对比验证；二是对界面、前处理与后处理关键参数开展消融研究。基于该协议，作者采集并公开了一个包含 500 段全向视频、由 2000+ 众包标注者标注的显著性数据集，声称是 360° 视频显著性领域目前规模最大的数据集，以此证明该方法在有效性与可扩展性上的优势。论文未在摘要中给出具体量化指标（如相似度、AUC、CC 等）与数值结果。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于：(1) 提出首个开源、低成本、可扩展的 360° 视频显著性采集平台，摆脱 VR 眼动硬件依赖；(2) 通过跨七个 VR 数据集的验证与消融研究支撑协议有效性；(3) 发布 500 视频 / 2000+ 标注者的大规模显著性数据集，缓解该领域的数据稀缺问题，对全向视频显著性预测、视口自适应流媒体等应用具有推动作用。潜在局限：众包鼠标标注与真实 VR 眼动注视之间存在模态与精度差异（摘要仅以协议验证回应）；摘要中未提供定量评估指标与模型基准结果，下游显著性预测性能提升幅度尚不明确。

Abstract: Omnidirectional video saliency prediction plays an important role in many immersive multimedia applications, including viewport-adaptive streaming and compression, foveated rendering, mesh simplification, perceptual quality assessment. Yet progress in this area remains constrained by the cost and complexity of collecting eye-tracking data with VR headsets, which makes large-scale dataset creation difficult to extend. We present OpenSAL360, the first open-source platform for scalable, low-cost 360° video saliency collection. Unlike conventional VR-based protocols, it requires only a standard screen, mouse, and internet connection, enabling parallel saliency data collection from common crowdsourcing assessors without specialized hardware. We validate our collection protocol against seven well-established VR eye-tracking datasets and conduct ablation studies on key interface, pre-, and post-processing parameters. To demonstrate the effectiveness and scalability of the proposed methodology, we collect and publicly release a saliency dataset covering 500 omnidirectional videos annotated by 2,000+ crowdsourcing assessors, making it, to the best of our knowledge, the largest dataset in this field. We make OpenSAL360 publicly available at https://github.com/msu-video-group/OpenSAL360.

</details>


### [39] [VoxelTTO: Voxel-Aligned Feed-Forward 3D Gaussian Splatting with Test-Time Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21498)
*Yibin Zhao,Yihan Pan,Yangwen Li,Jun Nan,Jianjun Yi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VoxelTTO 是一个前馈式 3D 高斯泼溅(3DGS)重建框架:它将稠密图像特征聚合到全局体素表示中,由体素特征解码高斯原语,从而打破"像素—高斯"一一对应关系;同时引入测试时优化(TTO)通过 LoRA 适配冻结视觉基础模型,并用随机实体体渲染替代传统 3DGS 光栅化,在少量图片与可选相机参数下实现几何精确的 NVS 与位姿估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前馈式 3DGS 方法通常回归与像素对齐的高斯基元,导致高斯间过度重叠与伪影;其根本问题在于将重建单元绑定到像素网格上,缺乏全局几何一致性。此外,当输入相机位姿不准确时,新视角合成(NVS)会出现明显错位。论文的动机是:在不重新训练大型视觉基础模型(VFM)的前提下,利用全局表示消除像素—高斯绑定,并借助已知/可监督的相机参数在测试时修正位姿与几何,从而提升重建精度与位姿估计质量。

Method: 方法核心包含三部分:1) 全局体素表示——将多视角稠密图像特征聚合进统一体素网格,再由体素特征解码高斯原语(位置、协方差、颜色等),使高斯不再与单个像素绑定,缓解重叠与伪影;2) 测试时优化(TTO)——在冻结的预训练 VFM 上插入轻量级 LoRA 模块,利用相机位姿监督信号在测试时适配,从而在保持 VFM 参数不变的情况下利用已知相机参数;3) 渲染替换——训练与推理阶段均用随机实体体积渲染(stochastic solid volume rendering)取代标准 3DGS 光栅化,以提升几何保真度。训练时仅更新体素对齐的高斯重建模块,计算开销约 80 GPU 小时。

Result: 在 Replica、Tanks and Temples、DTU 三个数据集上进行实验,评估 RGB-D 新视角合成与相机位姿估计。结果显示,相比现有方法,VoxelTTO 在 RGB-D NVS 质量和位姿估计精度上均有提升。训练成本约 80 GPU 小时,仅优化体素对齐的高斯重建模块与 LoRA 模块。

Conclusion: 主要贡献:(1) 提出基于全局体素特征解码高斯的架构,解耦像素与高斯基元;(2) 通过 TTO + LoRA 在冻结 VFM 上实现位姿监督适配;(3) 采用随机实体体积渲染提升几何保真度。意义在于证明前馈 3DGS 可通过全局表示与轻量测试时适配同时改善几何精度与位姿估计。潜在局限:摘要未给出与 SOTA 的具体数值差距及推理时延,80 GPU 小时训练成本仍较高,TTO 依赖位姿监督信号的可获得性,且未涉及动态场景或极端稀疏视图下的表现。

Abstract: Recent feed-forward 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) methods typically regress pixel-aligned Gaussian primitives, often causing excessive overlap and artifacts, while inaccuracies in predicted camera poses can lead to misalignment in novel-view synthesis (NVS). We present VoxelTTO, a feed-forward framework for reconstructing geometrically accurate 3DGS scenes from an arbitrary number of images and optional camera parameters. VoxelTTO aggregates dense image features into a global voxel representation and decodes Gaussians from voxel features, breaking the pixel-to-Gaussian correspondence. To exploit known camera parameters while keeping the pretrained visual foundation model (VFM) parameters frozen, we introduce test-time optimization (TTO) that adapts lightweight LoRA modules using pose supervision. We further replace vanilla 3DGS rasterization with stochastic solid volume rendering during training and inference, improving geometric fidelity. Training updates only the voxel-aligned Gaussian reconstruction modules, requiring 80 GPU hours. Experiments on Replica, Tanks and Temples, and DTU demonstrate improved RGB-D NVS and camera-pose estimation relative to prior methods.

</details>


### [40] [Adaptive World Memory 3D Foundation Model for Scalable 3D Mapping, Localization, and Rendering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21502)
*Tianchen Deng,Guole Shen,Yilin Shen,Wenhua Wu,Yilin Fang,Ziqi Ma,Tianjun Zhang,Shenghai Yuan,Wolfram Burgard,Hesheng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ${\color{red}\textbf{TL;DR}}$ 本文提出一种以记忆为中心的3D基础模型（AWM-3DFM），核心是"自适应世界记忆"机制，通过基于Transformer的门控更新与测试时时空调节，在统一框架内完成可扩展的机器人定位、稠密重建与高斯渲染。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ${\color{red}\textbf{动机与问题}}$ 近期3D基础模型虽能从RGB图像进行可泛化的几何推理，但存在三方面局限：一是缺乏持久记忆能力，难以在长序列中维持稳定的世界表征；二是可扩展性不足，难以支撑大规模场景建图；三是无法建模可渲染（renderable）的场景，导致定位、重建与渲染通常割裂于不同模型或流水线中。对机器人而言，长期运行下的持久世界建模、轨迹精度与全局一致性至关重要，因此需要一种兼具记忆、可扩展性与可渲染性的统一3D基础模型。

Method: ${\color{red}\textbf{方法与创新}}$ 核心是自适应世界记忆（adaptive world memory）机制：采用Transformer结构的门控更新（gated updates）控制循环记忆传播，学习到的门（learned gates）决定记忆token的更新与遗忘；同时引入测试时（test-time）时空调节，通过时序状态演化（temporal state evolution）与空间"观测-状态"一致性约束，对token级更新与长序列遗忘进行正则化。为支持大规模建图，将记忆组织为局部子图（local submaps），并结合渐进式建图与跟踪、回环检测以及基于SL(4)的全局优化以兼顾局部精度与全局一致性。模型附加高斯重建头，将记忆增强特征解码为可渲染的高斯基元，从而在单一模型内统一相机位姿估计、稠密点云重建与照片级渲染。

Result: ${\color{red}\textbf{实验与结果}}$ 在公开基准与多种机器人平台自采数据集上进行评测，涵盖轨迹精度、重建完整性与渲染质量三类指标。结果显示相比现有3D基础重建方法与SLAM基线，本方法在三个方面均有提升。数据与代码将开源（https://github.com/dtc111111/AWM-3DFM）。摘要未给出具体数值、数据集名称与量化指标。

Conclusion: ${\color{red}\textbf{结论与意义}}$ 该工作将"自适应记忆"确立为持久机器人世界建模的基础，通过门控循环记忆与时空一致性调节，把定位、重建与渲染统一到一个可扩展的3D基础模型中，并通过子图组织与SL(4)全局优化缓解长期漂移。潜在局限在于：记忆门控与时空调节在长序列（遗忘与更新权衡）中的稳定性、子图划分与SL(4)精化的计算与内存开销、以及对事件/多模态（如事件相机）数据的适配性尚不明确；摘要亦未披露定量结果与消融。

Abstract: Recent 3D foundation models enable generalizable geometric reasoning from RGB images but remain limited in persistent memory, scalability, and renderable scene modeling. We present a memory-centric 3D foundation model for scalable robotic localization, reconstruction, and Gaussian rendering. Its core is an adaptive world memory mechanism that combines transformer-based gated updates with test-time temporal-spatial regulation. Learned gates control recurrent memory propagation, while temporal state evolution and spatial observation-state consistency regulate token-wise updates and forgetting over long image sequences. To support large-scale mapping, we organize memory into local submaps and integrate progressive mapping and tracking, loop closure, and SL(4)-based global refinement to maintain local accuracy and global consistency. A Gaussian reconstruction head decodes memory-enhanced features into renderable primitives, unifying camera pose estimation, dense point-cloud reconstruction, and photorealistic rendering within a single model. Experiments on public benchmarks and self-collected datasets from diverse robotic platforms demonstrate improved trajectory accuracy, reconstruction completeness, and rendering quality over existing 3D foundation reconstruction and SLAM baselines. These results support adaptive memory as a foundation for persistent robotic world modeling. The dataset and code will be made publicly available at \href{https://github.com/dtc111111/AWM-3DFM}{https://github.com/dtc111111/AWM-3DFM}.

</details>


### [41] [2D GauSS-MI: Efficient Active Scene Reconstruction with Balanced Visual and Geometric Quality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21516)
*Yuhan Xie,Jia Pan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于2D Gaussian Splatting（2DGS）的主动重建框架，通过构建面向增量RGB-D观测的在线2DGS建图管线，并设计概率可靠性模型与2D GauSS-MI互信息度量，实现兼顾视觉与几何重建质量的主动视角选择，在有限机载算力下取得质量与效率的较好平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主动重建（如主动式SLAM/Next-Best-View）需要在有限的机载计算资源下高效选择观测视角，以在尽可能少的观测中实现高质量重建。现有方法难以同时兼顾视觉质量、几何质量与实时运行所需的计算效率。已有基于NeRF或3DGS的主动重建方案往往计算开销大、存储成本高，且多数度量未能同时刻画视角相关的视觉与几何重建不确定性，导致视角选择次优。因此，设计一种既轻量又能同时评估视觉与几何信息增益的主动视角选择度量，对实时机器人/无人机感知与在线重建具有重要意义。

Method: 该工作以2D Gaussian Splatting（2DGS）作为场景表示，2DGS使用显式的2D高斯面元（surface-oriented splats）表达表面，天然携带表面朝向信息。方法包含三部分：（1）面向增量式RGB-D观测的在线2DGS建图管线，支持随新帧到达进行增量式优化与地图更新，适配实时在线场景；（2）概率可靠性模型，对各2D高斯面元的视角相关重建质量（视觉与几何可靠性）进行概率化刻画；（3）在此模型基础上推导出2D Gaussian Splatting Shannon Mutual Information（2D GauSS-MI）度量，一种基于香农互信息的信息增益指标，利用2DGS显式的表面朝向计算候选视角的期望信息增益。该度量使主动视角选择同时考虑视觉与几何重建质量，从而在候选视角集合中挑选信息量最大者。

Result: 在8个Replica场景上，与三种当前最优（state-of-the-art）基线方法进行对比评估。实验结果表明，所提方法在视觉与几何重建质量之间取得了良好的平衡，同时计算开销显著降低，模型存储规模亦具有竞争力。论文摘要未给出具体量化指标（如PSNR、深度误差、运行时间数值等）与详细协议细节。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于：将2DGS引入主动重建，构建支持增量RGB-D观测的在线建图管线；提出刻画视角相关重建质量的概率可靠性模型；并提出基于显式表面朝向与香农互信息的2D GauSS-MI度量，使主动视角选择同时兼顾视觉与几何质量。其意义在于以较低计算代价与竞争性存储实现质量均衡，契合有限机载算力下的实时重建需求。潜在局限在于：评估仅基于8个Replica合成场景，未涉及真实世界或机载平台实验；缺乏具体量化结果与实时性（帧率/延迟）数据；摘要中未讨论与运动规划、动态场景或退化视角条件下的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Active reconstruction requires efficient active view selection to achieve high-quality reconstruction within limited onboard computational resources. Existing methods face challenges in adequately balancing visual and geometric quality with the computational efficiency required for real-time operation. In this work, we present an active reconstruction framework based on 2D Gaussian Splatting (2DGS). We develop an efficient online 2DGS mapping pipeline for incremental RGB-D observations and introduce a probabilistic reliability model that characterizes the view-dependent reconstruction quality of individual 2D Gaussian splats. Building on this model, we formulate 2D Gaussian Splatting Shannon Mutual Information (2D GauSS-MI), a mutual-information-based metric that exploits the explicit surface orientation of 2DGS to evaluate the expected information gain of candidate views. The proposed metric enables active view selection to account for both visual and geometric reconstruction quality. We evaluate the proposed system against three state-of-the-art baselines on eight Replica scenes. Experimental results demonstrate that our method achieves a favorable balance between visual and geometric reconstruction quality with substantially lower computational cost and competitive model storage.

</details>


### [42] [VidOmni-Bench: A Benchmark for Fine-Grained Video Understanding via Spatio-Temporal Event Verification across Complexity and Duration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21521)
*Changbeen Kim,Junwon Chang,Kipyo Kim,Risa Shinoda,Kuniaki Saito,Donghyun Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出 VidOmni-Bench，一个面向视频大语言模型（Video-LLMs）细粒度视频理解能力的诊断型基准。其核心任务是让模型逐句验证密集视频描述中的每个事件是否能被视频内容所支持，从而避免传统问答或字幕匹配评测中因表层线索和标注不全而产生的虚高表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 Video-LLM 评测主要依赖问答（QA）或与真值字幕进行匹配，这存在两个问题：一是模型可能通过语言先验或表层线索"蒙对"答案，而非真正理解视频；二是标注往往不完整，无法覆盖视频中密集的事件细节。这使得评测无法可靠反映模型对细粒度时序事件的理解能力，也难以暴露幻觉等关键缺陷。因此需要一个能够严格检验"每个事件是否被视频证据支持"的评测范式。

Method: VidOmni-Bench 采用"事件验证"而非"事件生成/问答"的评测范式：给定视频及其密集描述，模型需逐句判断描述中的事件是否被视频内容支持。构建流程上，作者沿视频复杂度与时长两个轴收集视频，覆盖五种复杂度类型，时长从 4 秒到 90 分钟，共 500 段视频；随后使用多种 Video-LLM 生成密集字幕，再由人工进行句子级验证标注，将包含错误事件的句子作为困难负样本（hard negatives）用于评测。这种设计使错误描述在语言上"看似合理"却在视觉上不成立，从而能有效区分真正理解与语言先验。

Result: 在 VidOmni-Bench 上的实验揭示了三点关键发现：(i) Video-LLM 在密集视频描述任务中频繁产生幻觉内容；(ii) 作为验证器时表现同样不佳，难以可靠识别那些看似合理但实际错误的事件描述；(iii) 模型的薄弱环节随视频复杂度与时长而变化，不同模型呈现出各自特有的瓶颈。数据集包含 500 段视频，跨越五种复杂度类型，时长跨度 4 秒至 90 分钟，句子级人工标注提供细粒度评估信号。

Conclusion: 该工作的主要贡献是提出了一个以"事件验证"为核心的 Video-LLM 细粒度评测基准，通过句子级人工标注与困难负样本设计，绕开了传统 QA/字幕匹配评测的捷径问题，系统性地量化了 Video-LLM 的幻觉与验证能力缺陷。其意义在于为视频理解模型提供了更严格、更具诊断性的评测工具，并揭示了模型瓶颈对视频复杂度和时长的依赖性。潜在局限在于基准规模相对有限（500 段视频），评测聚焦于描述验证而非完整视频理解能力，且依赖人工标注带来的成本与主观性问题。

Abstract: While Video Large Language Models (Video-LLMs) have recently demonstrated strong performance, reliably evaluating their fine-grained video understanding remains challenging. Existing benchmarks often rely on question answering or ground-truth caption matching, where models may succeed through superficial cues and incomplete annotations. To this end, we introduce VidOmni-Bench, a benchmark that requires models to verify whether each event in dense video captions is supported by the video. VidOmni-Bench consists of 500 videos spanning five complexity types and diverse durations from 4 seconds to 90 minutes. After collecting videos along these axes, we use diverse Video-LLMs to generate dense captions and obtain human-verified sentence-level labels, where sentences containing incorrect events serve as hard negatives for evaluation. Our experiments on VidOmni-Bench reveal three key findings: (i) Video-LLMs frequently generate hallucinated descriptions in dense video captioning; (ii) they also struggle as verifiers, failing to reliably detect plausible but incorrect event descriptions; and (iii) model weaknesses vary across video complexity and duration, revealing diverse, model-specific bottlenecks in current Video-LLMs.

</details>


### [43] [Refine Then Fusion: Training-Free 3D Point Cloud Adaptation with Priority Refinement and Multi-Modal Knowledge Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21522)
*Hang Cheng,Yan Chen,Mingyu Fan,Long Zeng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RTF（Refine Then Fusion）是一个免训练的少样本3D识别框架，先在缓存的多模态预训练特征中筛选判别性通道，再基于样本级模态可靠度进行自适应融合，最终通过实例级支持特征与类级原型的记忆缓存完成查询推断，在五个基准上取得免梯度优化的SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练基础模型虽为下游3D视觉任务提供了丰富的多模态先验，但在少样本场景下其表征效用受两个问题制约：(1) 高维特征存在大量通道冗余与任务无关噪声，直接使用会淹没关键判别线索；(2) 不同模态的可靠性因样本而异，简单的特征拼接/聚合忽略了样本依赖的模态可信度差异，可能引入不可靠模态干扰。因此需要一种既能精炼特征通道、又能按样本自适应融合多模态的方法。

Method: RTF为免训练（training-free）框架，核心包含三部分：(1) 特征通道精炼——通过联合建模类间相似性与类内稳定性来识别判别性通道，将领域特定的知识精炼与预训练模型缓存表征解耦，剔除冗余通道与噪声；(2) 可靠性感知融合——利用特征精炼引起的分布偏移（distribution shift）估计样本级模态可靠性，从而实现多模态表征的自适应聚合，避免静态融合权重忽略样本差异；(3) 记忆缓存推断——构建同时包含实例级支持特征与类级原型的记忆缓存，用于推断查询样本标签。整个过程无需梯度优化、无需额外训练数据或辅助训练，也不更新参数。

Result: 在五个基准数据集上进行了大量实验。结果表明RTF持续优于单模态基线、部分融合（partial-fusion）变体以及现有轻量级自适应方法，在无需梯度优化、额外训练数据、辅助训练或参数更新的前提下，取得少样本3D识别的最优（state-of-the-art）性能。

Conclusion: RTF的主要贡献在于提出了一种免训练的少样本3D识别范式，通过通道级特征精炼与样本级可靠性感知多模态融合，并用实例级+类级记忆缓存完成标签推断，避免了梯度优化与参数更新带来的开销，在多个基准上达到SOTA。意义在于为高效、可即插即用的多模态3D识别提供了新思路。潜在局限包括：精炼与融合高度依赖预训练特征的初始质量，可靠性估计基于分布偏移的假设在模态退化严重时可能不稳定；论文未明确讨论计算/存储开销随缓存规模的增长，以及对更大类数或更复杂3D场景的泛化能力。

Abstract: Recent pre-trained foundation models provide rich multi-modal priors for downstream 3D vision tasks. However, the effectiveness of these representations in few-shot scenarios is limited by two fundamental challenges: High-dimensional features often contain substantial channel redundancy and task-irrelevant noise, while the reliability of different modalities varies across samples. Consequently, direct aggregation of heterogeneous representations overlooks sample-dependent modality reliability and may obscure the discriminative cues essential. To address these limitations, we propose Refine Then Fusion(RTF), a training-free framework for few-shot 3D recognition. RTF first identifies discriminative feature channels by jointly modeling inter-class similarity and intra-class stability, thereby decoupling domain-specific knowledge refinement from the cached representations of pre-trained models. It then introduces a reliability-aware fusion mechanism that estimates sample-wise modality reliability from the distribution shifts induced by feature refinement, enabling adaptive aggregation of multi-modal representations. Furthermore, RTF constructs a memory cache that integrates instance-level support features with class-level prototypes to infer query labels. Extensive experiments on five benchmarks demonstrate that RTF consistently outperforms single-modal baselines, partial-fusion variants, and existing lightweight adaptation methods, achieving state-of-the-art few-shot 3D recognition performance without gradient optimization, additional training data, auxiliary training, or parameter updates.

</details>


### [44] [Purification and Regulation: Comorbidity-Aware Multi-Label Few-Shot Learning for Medical Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21541)
*Ying-Chih Lin,Po-Chih Kuo,Yong-Sheng Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出原型净化与调节（Prototype Purification and Regulation, PPR）框架，用于医学图像分析中的多标签小样本学习（MLFSL），通过样本级共病分数净化原型、并利用疾病级共病统计自适应调节类间相似度，构建共病感知嵌入空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多标签小样本学习在医学图像分析（MIA）中极具挑战。现有基于度量的元学习方法存在两个关键缺陷：（1）传统原型生成方式会混入无关疾病信息，产生被"污染"的原型，导致性能下降；（2）已有工作通常在嵌入空间中强制类间可分，却忽视了疾病之间固有的相关性（如共病现象）。这两点使得原型既不够"纯"，也未能建模疾病间关系，从而限制了小样本条件下的可靠诊断，具有重要的临床意义。

Method: PPR 是一个两阶段的元学习框架。第一步为原型净化（Prototype Purification）：利用样本级共病分数（sample-level comorbidity scores）对特征进行加权，突出疾病特异性特征，生成更能表征单一疾病的纯化原型，从而缓解多标签场景下原型被无关疾病信息污染的问题。第二步为原型调节（Prototype Regulation）：在上述纯化原型基础上，引入疾病级共病统计（disease-level comorbidity statistics）来自适应地调节类间相似度，构建共病感知嵌入空间。该设计顺序地让模型先捕获纯粹的疾病特征，再建模类间关系，从而避免以往只强制类间分离而忽略疾病相关性的做法。

Result: 论文在四个胸部 X 光（chest X-ray）基准数据集上进行了大量实验，其中包括跨域（cross-domain）评估。结果表明 PPR 一致优于当前最先进（state-of-the-art）方法，显著提升疾病检测性能，并展现出良好的泛化能力与临床适用性。评估侧重于小样本多标签场景下的疾病检测性能（如各类别分类指标），但摘要未给出具体数值、数据集名称及度量细节。

Conclusion: PPR 的主要贡献在于：针对 MIA 中多标签小样本学习的原型污染与类间关系建模缺失两个问题，提出了基于共病分数与共病统计的原型净化与调节框架，构建共病感知嵌入空间。其在多个胸部 X 光基准及跨域设置上取得一致性提升，验证了方法的有效性与泛化性。潜在局限在于：摘要未报告具体性能数值、计算开销与参数量，方法依赖共病统计信息（可能受数据集偏倚影响），且未涉及事件相机、时序建模、小目标检测或机器人/UAV 感知等领域的适用性验证。

Abstract: Multi-label few-shot learning (MLFSL) remains a significant challenge in medical image analysis (MIA). Current metric-based meta-learning methods face two critical limitations in MIA. First, conventional prototype generation often entangles irrelevant disease information, leading to contaminated prototypes and degraded performance. Second, prior studies typically enforce inter-class separability in embedding space, largely neglecting the inherent correlations among diseases. To overcome these challenges, we propose Prototype Purification and Regulation (PPR), a novel MLFSL framework for MIA. PPR first performs prototype purification by leveraging sample-level comorbidity scores to emphasize disease-specific features, producing purified prototypes that better characterize each disease. Building upon these purified prototypes, PPR further addresses the underexplored problem of inter-class prototype distance in MIA by incorporating disease-level comorbidity statistics to adaptively regulate inter-class similarity, forming a comorbidity-aware embedding space. Overall, PPR sequentially enables the model to capture pure disease features and inter-class relationships for reliable MLFSL in MIA. Extensive experiments across four chest X-ray benchmark datasets, including cross-domain evaluation, show that PPR consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods, significantly improving disease detection while demonstrating robust generalization and clinical applicability.

</details>


### [45] [From Retrieval to Recognition:How Vision--Language Models Become OCR Specialists](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21543)
*Yuanxiang Huangfu,Hanmeng Zhong,Linqing Chen,Jeffrey Tiong Jee Hui*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文通过"证据锚定+因果干预"的可解释性协议，研究了通用视觉-语言模型（VLM）获得OCR能力（尤其是全序列OCR）的机制。结论是：通用VLM并非发展出全新的"阅读回路"，而是复用已有的文本检索/复制回路，将全序列OCR实现为"稠密序列化的多模态复制-粘贴"过程；而模型从通用VLM专精化为OCR专家时，主要保留注意力头的身份不变，仅重新分配其功能与因果强度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心问题是：通用VLM在获得专门OCR能力时，是"发展出一条新的阅读回路"，还是"复用已有机制"？作者强调研究的是全序列OCR（full-sequence OCR）而非"局部答案检索"（local-answer retrieval），后者可能掩盖真实机制。理解这一问题有助于揭示多模态能力获得的机理，并为模型专精化、可解释性与能力迁移提供依据。

Method: 采用"证据锚定（evidence-grounded）"协议，并结合留出的、基于因果干预（causal interventions）的机制识别方法，在GLM-OCR、MinerU2.5、PaddleOCR-VL-1.6中识别出稀疏且稳定的"OCR头"集合。随后将这些OCR头与通用VLM中独立识别出的"文本检索/复制头"进行对比，通过top-20头集合交集比例与全头Spearman秩相关量化重叠程度；进一步通过因果干预验证功能，并通过匹配的base-to-specialized对比，考察从通用VLM到OCR专家过程中头身份与功能强度的变化。

Result: 在两个通用VLM上，视觉OCR头与独立识别的文本检索/复制头高度重叠：未调优的top-20交集分别达到73.3%，全头Spearman相关为0.677–0.886。重叠与因果干预结果表明，全序列OCR本质上是"稠密序列化的多模态复制-粘贴"——反复检索视觉证据并将其路由到当前输出位置。在从通用VLM到OCR专家的匹配对比中，专精化基本保留头身份，每个任务top-20头中保留17–20个，全头秩相关为0.874–0.942，但重新分配了其功能与因果强度。

Conclusion: 论文主要贡献在于：以证据锚定和因果干预协议，实证揭示了通用VLM的全序列OCR能力源于对已有文本检索/复制回路的复用，而非新建回路；并刻画了专精化过程中"头身份保留、强度再分配"的规律。这对多模态机制可解释性、能力复用与专精化训练具有参考价值。局限可能包括：研究仅覆盖有限的通用VLM与OCR模型、分析集中于注意力头层面的稀疏头集合，对回路的整体因果充分性及更广泛视觉语言任务的可推广性尚需进一步验证；此外"复制-粘贴"解释可能未完全涵盖OCR中的其他处理环节。

Abstract: Does a general vision--language model acquire specialized OCR ability by developing a new reading circuit or by reusing an existing mechanism? We address this question in the setting of full-sequence OCR, rather than local-answer retrieval. Using an evidence-grounded protocol with held-out causal interventions, we identify sparse and stable OCR-head sets in GLM-OCR, MinerU2.5, and PaddleOCR-VL-1.6. We then investigate the mechanistic origin of these OCR heads by comparing them with independently identified textual retrieval/copy heads in general VLMs. Across two general VLMs, visual OCR heads strongly overlap independently identified textual retrieval/copy heads, yielding untuned top-20 intersections of 73.3% and all-head Spearman correlations of 0.677-0.886. The overlap and causal interventions suggest that full-sequence OCR operates as dense sequential multimodal copy-and-paste, repeatedly retrieving visual evidence and routing it to the current output position. Finally, we examine how this shared circuit changes as a general VLM becomes an OCR specialist. Matched base-to-specialized comparisons show that OCR specialization largely preserves head identity, retaining 17-20 of the top 20 heads per task with all-head rank correlations of 0.874-0.942, while redistributing their functional and causal strengths.

</details>


### [46] [GestureFAR: Streaming Co-Speech Gesture Generation with Flow Autoregression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21576)
*Pinxin Liu,Haiyang Liu,Jiahao Luo,Junhua Huang,Chunhao Zou,Luchuan Song*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GestureFAR 提出一种流匹配（flow matching）自回归框架，用于从流式语音中实时生成共语手势：在因果连续运动潜变量上自回归建模，并用"仅头部流蒸馏"把多步 flow 采样压缩为单次网络评估，兼顾连续表达力、因果性与低延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身对话智能体需要在用户仍在说话时就生成自然手势，因此必须支持流式（在线）生成。现有流式手势系统多依赖"离散运动 token 上的自回归"，即把高维连续运动压缩进有限码本（codebook）。这种离散化虽带来因果性和易建模性，但会损失运动的连续细节与多样性，限制生成手势的真实感。核心矛盾在于：如何在保持 token 级因果性（causality）的同时保留连续运动表达力，并满足实时交互的低延迟要求。此外，逐 token 的多步 flow 采样会引入显著的推理延迟，成为实时交互的瓶颈。

Method: 1) Flow-autoregressive 主干：不再离散化运动，而是自回归地生成"因果连续运动潜变量"（causal continuous motion latents）。用 Transformer 建模流式音频-运动上下文，并配一个 per-token flow-matching head，从连续分布中采样下一个 latent，从而避免码本量化带来的表达损失，同时保持 token 因果性（每步输出只依赖历史上下文）。2) 仅头部流蒸馏（head-only flow distillation）：冻结因果主干，仅将多步的 per-token flow head 蒸馏为单次网络评估（single network evaluation），采用一致性目标（consistency）与分布匹配目标（distribution-matching）联合优化。这样将多步 ODE/flow 采样折叠为一步，去除主要延迟瓶颈，同时不破坏主干的 token 因果性。整体是"连续潜变量自回归 + 流匹配头 + 一致性蒸馏"的组合设计。

Result: 在 BEAT2 数据集上进行实验（共语手势生成的常用基准）。论文报告 GestureFAR 在"流式生成方法"中显著改善了质量—延迟权衡（quality–latency trade-off）：在保持较强手势质量的同时，实现 real-time、token-causal 的生成。评估围绕生成质量（如手势真实感/多样性相关指标）与推理延迟两方面展开。摘要未给出具体数值，需参阅论文正文。

Conclusion: 贡献有三：一是提出在连续潜变量上做流式自回归的共语手势生成框架，避免离散码本对真实感与多样性的限制；二是提出 head-only flow distillation，将多步 flow 采样压缩为单次评估以降低延迟；三是在 BEAT2 上验证了流式方法中更优的质量—延迟权衡。意义在于把 flow matching 的连续建模能力引入流式/因果的手势生成场景，兼顾实时性与表达力，适用于具身对话智能体。潜在局限：摘要未给出具体指标与消融细节；一步蒸馏相对多步采样可能存在质量损失（需一致性/分布匹配损失来补偿）；仅在 BEAT2 单一数据集上验证，跨数据集与说话人泛化性、以及不同风格/多样性的定量评估仍待补充。

Abstract: Generating natural co-speech gestures from streaming speech is essential for embodied conversational agents, where motion must be produced while a user is still speaking. Recent streaming gesture systems make online generation possible by autoregressing over discrete motion tokens, but this design compresses high-dimensional continuous motion into finite codebooks and can limit the realism and diversity of generated gestures. To preserve both causality and continuous expressiveness, we propose \textbf{GestureFAR}, a flow-autoregressive framework for streaming co-speech gesture generation. First, GestureFAR autoregresses over causal continuous motion latents, using a transformer to model streaming audio-motion context and a per-token flow-matching head to sample the next latent from a continuous distribution. Second, we introduce a head-only flow distillation strategy that freezes the causal backbone and distills the multi-step per-token flow head into a single network evaluation using consistency and distribution-matching objectives. This keeps the model token-causal while removing the main latency bottleneck for live interaction. Experiments on BEAT2 show that GestureFAR significantly improves the quality--latency trade-off among streaming-capable methods, preserving strong gesture quality while enabling real-time token-causal generation. Project Page: https://andypinxinliu.github.io/GestureFAR

</details>


### [47] [A benchmark dataset and baseline methods for four-dimensional STEM diffraction patterns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21593)
*Yuyan Guan,Haoran Zhang,Zian Mao,Antong Yang,Caifei Li,Jialong Wang,Chuying Ouyang,Hong Wang,Xiaoqin Zeng,Yujun Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发布了4D-ImageNet数据集，包含174,000张4D-STEM衍射图案（145,000张实验数据与29,000张多切片模拟数据），并附带伪标签、元数据及一个分组混合域掩码重建基准，用于表示学习、衍射盘检测、检索与模拟-实验迁移研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 4D-STEM在电子探针的每个扫描位置记录二维衍射图案，能够提供空间分辨的倒易空间信息，但数据体量巨大且高度异构，缺乏统一、大规模、带有标注与元数据的公开数据集。这限制了机器学习方法（如表示学习、盘检测、取向分析）在该领域的发展以及从模拟到实验的迁移研究。构建标准化数据集对于推动4D-STEM数据的自动化分析与可复现研究具有重要意义。

Method: 数据集由两部分构成：1）实验数据——从29次采集中选取145,000张衍射图案，覆盖Ag、Au、Au-Ag混合、CoO、Pd、ZnO等样品，跨多个视场、扫描尺寸、相机长度与曝光时间，每次采集贡献5,000张按质量排序的图案，并附带原始扫描坐标与采集元数据；2）模拟数据——29,000张多切片模拟图案，覆盖13种晶体结构，包含Euler旋转、倒易空间采样以及近似低指数束方向信息。标注方面，采用集合预测检测器生成束中心位置与布拉格盘中心的伪标签，并为每个盘提供置信度分数。此外提供了一个分组混合域掩码重建基准，以实现抗泄漏的数据加载与跨实验/模拟域评估。

Result: 数据集规模为174,000张衍射图案（145,000实验+29,000模拟）。实验部分覆盖6类样品体系（含混合体系）与多种采集配置，模拟部分覆盖13种晶体结构。每张实验图案带有质量排名、扫描坐标与采集元数据，并配有束中心与布拉格盘伪标签及其置信度。提供了分组混合域掩码重建基准，用于在防止数据泄漏的前提下跨实验与模拟数据评估模型。目标应用包括表示学习、盘检测、衍射图案检索、取向分析和模拟到实验的迁移研究。

Conclusion: 本文的主要贡献是构建并公开了大规模、异构、多模态的4D-STEM数据集4D-ImageNet，集成了实验与模拟数据、丰富的采集元数据以及模型生成的伪标签，并提供抗泄漏的分组混合域重建基准，为4D-STEM的表示学习与自动化分析提供了标准化平台。潜在局限在于标注并非人工真值而是检测器产生的伪标签（含置信度分数），其可靠性受检测器性能影响；模拟数据基于多切片近似，与真实实验分布之间可能存在域差异。

Abstract: Four-dimensional scanning transmission electron microscopy (4D-STEM) records a two-dimensional diffraction pattern at each electron-probe position, yielding spatially resolved reciprocal-space information but large, heterogeneous data volumes. Here we describe 4D-ImageNet, a collection of 174,000 diffraction patterns comprising 145,000 experimental patterns selected from 29 acquisitions and 29,000 multislice simulations. The experimental data cover acquisition-level labels for Ag, Au, mixed Au-Ag, CoO, Pd and ZnO specimens across multiple fields of view, scan dimensions, camera lengths and exposure times. Each acquisition contributes 5,000 quality-ranked patterns with source scan coordinates and acquisition metadata. A set-prediction detector provides model-derived pseudo-labels for the direct-beam position and Bragg-disk centres, with a confidence score for each disk. The simulation data cover 13 crystal structures and include Euler rotations, reciprocal-space sampling and approximate low-index beam directions. A grouped mixed-domain masked-reconstruction benchmark is provided to assess leakage-resistant loading and evaluation across experimental and simulated data. The dataset is intended for representation learning, disk detection, diffraction-pattern retrieval, orientation analysis and simulation-to-experiment studies.

</details>


### [48] [HAT: Hypothesis-Anchored Tracking for Video Monocular Spacecraft Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21597)
*André Lopo,Atabak Dehban,Rodrigo Ventura*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HAT（Hypothesis-Anchored Tracking）是一个因果式单目6-DoF位姿估计框架，利用帧间运动在多个CAD-based位姿假设之间进行选择与锚定，通过稀疏锚点与位姿融合实现逐帧输出，无需目标特定训练，兼顾精度与实时性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非合作目标的单目6-DoF位姿估计对在轨服务与空间碎片清除至关重要。单帧估计器在接近对称的航天器姿态下容易混淆方向，而传统跟踪又可能将错误位姿长期锁定并传播。因此需要一种既能在单帧模糊时正确消歧、又避免误差累积的因果式跟踪方法，且不依赖目标特定训练与微调。

Method: 提出HAT，一种因果框架：不独立选取每帧最高分假设，而是保留相互竞争的朝向历史，利用帧间运动在候选位姿假设间进行选择，选出用于锚定单目SLAM估计的相对轨迹的位姿。采用稀疏锚点与位姿融合，在初始化后提供逐帧估计且不修改过去输出。仅需标定RGB序列、度量CAD模型与目标图像区域（可由检测或分割提供）。预训练的位姿与SLAM网络无需目标特定训练或微调。给出Mega-HAT与Pico-HAT两个版本，分别基于MegaPose与PicoPose。

Result: 在SPARK-2024、SwissCube、SHIRT三个空间数据集上评估，并用YCB-Video考察空间域外性能。每种方法各采用一种时间配置，四个数据集层面比较的算术平均显示：相对独立MegaPose，Mega-HAT平均位姿误差降低9.4%，持续输入FPS为3.76倍；相对独立PicoPose，Pico-HAT平均位姿误差降低23.9%，FPS为2.42倍。在SPARK上的Mega-HAT消融实验及一个离线参考分析了各组件的贡献以及修正过去估计的影响。

Conclusion: 论文主要贡献是提出假设锚定跟踪框架HAT，通过帧间运动驱动的假设选择与锚定机制缓解对称航天器姿态歧义，并提供Mega-HAT与Pico-HAT两个实时版本，在不需目标微调的前提下显著提升精度与吞吐（最高3.76倍FPS）。局限在于评估仅使用每种方法一种时间配置，且消融范围有限，离线参考非实时；对检测/分割输入质量与CAD模型的依赖也可能影响实际部署。

Abstract: Monocular 6-DoF pose estimation of non-cooperative targets is important for on-orbit servicing and debris removal. A single-image estimator can confuse near-symmetric spacecraft orientations, and tracking can preserve an incorrect pose. We present Hypothesis-Anchored Tracking (HAT), a causal framework that uses inter-frame motion to select among competing CAD-based pose hypotheses before alignment and fusion. Rather than independently choosing the highest-scoring hypothesis in each image, HAT retains competing orientation histories and selects a pose to anchor the relative trajectory estimated by monocular SLAM. Sparse anchors and pose fusion provide per-frame estimates after initialization without revising past outputs. The method requires only a calibrated RGB sequence, a metric CAD model, and target image regions, which can be supplied by detection or segmentation. The pretrained pose and SLAM networks require no target-specific training or fine-tuning. We evaluate two versions, Mega-HAT and Pico-HAT, using MegaPose and PicoPose, on SPARK-2024, SwissCube and SHIRT, with YCB-Video assessing performance outside the space domain. Using one temporal configuration per method, the arithmetic means of the four dataset-wise comparisons show 9.4% lower mean pose error and 3.76 times the sustained input FPS for Mega-HAT relative to independent MegaPose, and 23.9% lower mean pose error and 2.42 times the FPS for Pico-HAT relative to independent PicoPose. Mega-HAT ablations on SPARK and an offline reference examine component contributions and the effect of revising past estimates.

</details>


### [49] [Learned Parametric Emotion Editing: Real-Time Affective Filtering for On-Device Social Media Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21624)
*Musa Rochi,Marcel Schubert,Christoph Gebhardt*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种单次前向推理的"情感强度调节"模型：以MobileNetV4为主干，通过FiLM进行情绪条件调制，输出可微全局变换的参数，将以往每张图约80秒的迭代优化替换为3.7 ms的单次前向计算；用户研究（N=54）表明该方法可降低观看者的唤醒度（arousal），效果与灰度"健康滤镜"相当但画质评分更高，并已集成到Android应用中实时处理Instagram视频，在三星Galaxy S23上达到60 fps。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 问题性网络使用（problematic internet use）影响人群比例持续增长，而常见干预手段（时间限制、内容屏蔽、强制休息）具有强制性且易被绕过，用户体验与依从性均较差。论文探索一种更温和的替代方案：不限制用户访问内容，而是调节视觉内容的情感强度（affective content），从感知层面降低内容的情绪唤起程度。先前工作虽已证明可以用优化方法"引导"图像的情感内容，但逐图迭代优化的代价过高（约80 s/张），无法满足实时部署需求，因此需要一种可即时预测该变换的模型。

Method: 核心思路是将"优化得到的图像情感变换"蒸馏为一次性预测问题。模型以MobileNetV4为backbone提取图像特征，采用FiLM（Feature-wise Linear Modulation）实现情绪条件调制，使同一网络能针对目标情绪状态生成不同的变换参数；网络输出的是可微全局变换（differentiable global transformations）的若干参数，而非直接生成像素，因此变换过程可保持结构一致性并允许端到端训练。该设计把先前的迭代优化过程替换为单次前向传播（3.7 ms vs. 80 s），并可直接部署于移动端。

Result: 实验包括用户研究（N = 54）：与未编辑图像相比，模型显著降低了观看者自评的唤醒度（arousal），其效果与灰度化"健康滤镜"（grayscale well-being filter）相当，但在感知画质（perceived quality）上获得更高评分。系统层面，模型被集成进Android应用，可对Instagram视频进行实时情感强度调节，在三星Galaxy S23上稳定维持60 fps。论文未给出更细粒度的数据集规模、消融实验或量化误差指标（摘要中未提供）。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于：(1) 将情感图像变换从逐图迭代优化转化为单次前向预测，实现约四个数量级的速度提升（80 s → 3.7 ms）；(2) 提出基于MobileNetV4 + FiLM情绪条件化 + 可微全局变换的轻量架构；(3) 通过用户研究初步验证其降低情绪唤醒的有效性与画质优势；(4) 完成移动端实时视频部署（60 fps）。意义在于为数字健康干预提供了一种非强制性的、可实时落地的替代路径。潜在局限：用户研究样本量较小（N=54），评估依赖自评唤醒度与主观画质，缺乏对长期使用效果、内容语义保真度、跨人群泛化性以及伦理/操纵性风险的充分讨论与量化分析。

Abstract: Problematic internet use affects a growing share of the population, yet common interventions, e.g., time limits, blocking, forced breaks, are coercive and easily circumvented. We explore a less restrictive alternative: adapting the emotional intensity of visual content. Prior work has shown that optimization can steer an image's affective content, but its per-image optimization cost makes it impractical for real-time deployment. We instead learn a model that predicts this transformation in a single forward pass: a MobileNetV4 backbone with FiLM-based emotion conditioning outputs parameters for differentiable global transformations. This replaces prior iterative optimization (80 s per image) with a single 3.7 ms forward pass. In a user study (N = 54), the model reduced viewer-reported arousal relative to unedited images, comparably to the grayscale well-being filter, while being rated higher in perceived quality. We integrate the model into an Android app that adapts Instagram video in real time, sustaining 60 fps on a Samsung Galaxy S23.

</details>


### [50] [Detection is solved, delineation is not: what governs tooth segmentation on panoramic radiographs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21628)
*Muhammad Rehan,Moaz Amjad,Syed Danial Ahmed,Mariam Adnan,Haider Ali*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文构建了包含1,422张全景X光片、42,142个专家标注牙齿多边形（32类FDI编号）的语料库，并在统一评测协议下系统性地分离了输入分辨率、模型架构与解剖先验三个因素对牙齿分割和FDI编号性能的影响。核心结论是：输入分辨率是决定性因素，架构选择在域内几乎无关紧要，而边界精度（而非检测能力）是性能的瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全景X光片上的自动牙齿分割与FDI编号是计算机辅助牙科诊断的基础任务，但长期以来学界并不清楚究竟是哪些因素主导了模型性能。现有工作往往各自提出新架构或新先验并报告孤立的性能提升，缺乏在统一协议下对不同因素（分辨率、架构、解剖先验）进行受控对比的研究，导致改进来源难以归因，社区可能把精力错误地投向架构创新。该论文旨在通过一个大规模、严格标注的语料库和统一的评测协议，厘清性能瓶颈的真实所在。

Method: 论文构建了由30名牙科从业者标注、另2名独立复核的语料库（1,422张全景片、42,142个牙齿多边形、32类FDI分类）。在此数据上开展三组受控实验：（1）分辨率消融：在640/1024/1280三档下评测，采用基于图像的配对bootstrap进行显著性检验；（2）架构对比：将query-based transformer（参数量为baseline的2.1倍）与one-stage检测器在同一协议下比较，并考察域偏移、CPU推理速度与ONNX运行时可执行性；（3）先验干预：分别评测LoRA微调的自监督编码器、可提示基础分割模型（promptable foundation segmenter）以及全局最优解剖标签分配。此外通过零样本迁移至独立多中心队列验证结论，并沿牙齿轴向分解掩膜以定位残差误差。

Result: 分辨率消融显示，mask mAP50-95在640/1024/1280下依次为0.656→0.710→0.717，两个增益在配对bootstrap下显著（p<0.001，p=0.024），而mAP50始终稳定在约0.982且变化不显著——说明分辨率提升带来的是边界精度而非检测能力。架构方面，query-based transformer与one-stage检测器在域内统计等价（95% CI [-0.0064, +0.0064]），仅在域偏移下略优，但CPU推理慢5.5倍且无法在标准ONNX运行时执行。三项先验干预均失败：LoRA自监督编码器性能更差，可提示基础分割器使掩膜质量下降39%，全局最优标签分配仅带来+0.0007的提升（尽管其修正了域外预测中40%样本违反的约束）。零样本迁移至经验证无重叠的独立多中心队列时，mask mAP50-95下降62%而mAP50仅下降18%，复现了上述解耦现象。沿牙齿轴分解掩膜后，残差误差集中于根尖三分之一区域。

Conclusion: 论文的主要贡献在于：构建了大规模、多标注者、严格复核的牙齿分割与FDI编号基准语料库；在统一协议下系统证明分辨率（边界精度）而非架构新颖性才是性能的约束瓶颈；并通过域内、域偏移及跨中心零样本迁移的一致性结果强化了这一结论。其意义在于为牙科影像分析乃至更广泛的密集分割任务提供了"改进方向应投向分辨率与采集多样性，而非架构创新"的实证依据。潜在局限包括：结论基于全景片这一单一模态，向CBCT、口内扫描等其他牙科影像及更一般的自然图像分割任务的推广性尚待验证；可提示基础分割模型表现不佳的具体原因未深入剖析；根尖三分之一误差来源的临床可干预性也有待进一步研究。

Abstract: Automatic tooth segmentation and FDI numbering on panoramic radiographs underpins computer-assisted dental diagnosis, yet which factors govern performance remains unclear. We assemble a corpus of 1,422 panoramic radiographs containing 42,142 expert-delineated tooth polygons across the 32-class FDI taxonomy, annotated by 30 dental practitioners and independently reviewed by two others, and use it to isolate input resolution, architecture and anatomical priors under a single evaluation protocol.
  First, resolution dominates: across a controlled 640/1024/1280 ablation, mask mAP50-95 rises 0.656 -> 0.710 -> 0.717 while mAP50 stays flat at ~0.982. Both gains are significant under a paired bootstrap over images (p < 0.001, p = 0.024); neither mAP50 change is distinguishable from zero. Added resolution buys boundary precision, not detection. Second, architecture is nearly irrelevant in-domain: a query-based transformer with 2.1x the parameters is statistically equivalent to a one-stage detector (95% CI [-0.0064, +0.0064]), only marginally better under domain shift, 5.5x slower on CPU and not executable under standard ONNX runtimes. Third, three targeted interventions fail: a LoRA-adapted self-supervised encoder underperforms, a promptable foundation segmenter degrades masks by 39%, and globally optimal anatomical label assignment yields +0.0007 despite correcting a constraint violated in 40% of out-of-domain predictions.
  Zero-shot transfer to an independent multi-centre cohort, verified overlap-free, costs 62% of mask mAP50-95 but only 18% of mAP50, reproducing the dissociation. Decomposing masks along the tooth axis localises the residual error to the apical third. Boundary precision is therefore the binding constraint, and effort is better directed at resolution and acquisition diversity than at architectural novelty.

</details>


### [51] [Extending Decoupled Attention to Dense Prediction and Masked Training for Multi-Channel Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21629)
*Umar Marikkar,Sameed Husain,Muhammad Awais,Sara Atito*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文针对多通道成像(MCI)数据提出一种"解耦视觉Transformer"(DC-ViT)的改进方案:通过在通道内(intra-channel)与通道间(inter-channel)分别计算注意力更新,并在通道组合前为每个通道单独构建表示,避免多通道注意力被稀释。关键创新是利用线性分配(linear assignment)在独立逐通道掩码下恢复patch对应关系,从而把解耦注意力与标准掩码多通道训练直接结合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MCI数据(如荧光显微、成像质谱流式、卫星成像)中每个通道记录语义上相互独立的信号,而非自然图像的色彩波段。现有MC-ViT将各通道独立tokenize后直接拼接为一个序列,通道数不再固定由架构决定;随后自注意力在所有通道-patch token上无约束地计算,导致单个通道特征被稀释。DC-ViT虽通过分离通道内/通道间更新缓解了这一问题,但其公式按空间位置配对token,要求每个通道可见token一致,从而无法兼容当前标准的逐通道独立掩码训练范式。论文的动机即在于解除这一限制,使解耦注意力能在通用掩码多通道训练配置下使用。

Method: 方法层面,论文延续DC-ViT的核心思想:将注意力更新解耦为通道内更新与通道间更新,并在通道融合之前为每个通道形成独立表示,而非让所有通道-patch token自由交叉注意力。针对原公式依赖空间位置配对、要求各通道可见token一致的局限,论文提出对每个通道保留下来的patch之间求解线性分配问题,以恢复跨通道的对应关系。通过该分配机制,解耦注意力可直接与当前掩码多通道训练(每通道独立掩码)的标准配置结合,而无需退化为受限配置。整体上,该方法保持MC-ViT的通道数灵活特性,但以受调控的注意力提高单通道特征表达质量。

Result: 实验覆盖三个分类基准与三个分割基准,涵盖荧光显微成像、成像质谱流式(IMC)和卫星成像,其中包括高通道数下的密集预测任务。评估以分类与分割性能为指标,结果显示所提公式在所有这些基准上均优于最强的MC-ViT基线。

Conclusion: 论文的主要贡献在于:通过线性分配恢复独立掩码下的跨通道patch对应,将DC-ViT式的解耦注意力推广到标准掩码多通道训练配置,并在多领域分类与分割任务上稳定超越最强MC-ViT基线,尤其在高通道密集预测场景中有效。局限方面,摘要未给出具体的计算开销、掩码比例敏感性、数据集规模与量化数值,线性分配求解在极高通道数或大量patch下的可扩展性亦待进一步验证。

Abstract: Multi-Channel imaging (MCI) data differs fundamentally from natural images, as each channel records a semantically distinct signal rather than a colour band. To adapt vision encoders to MCI data, Multi-Channel Vision Transformers (MC-ViTs) tokenize each channel independently and concatenate the resulting tokens into one sequence, and the channel count is no longer fixed by the architecture. Self-attention is then computed across all channel-patch tokens with no restriction on which channels attend to which, which dilutes the features of individual channels. The Decoupled Vision Transformer (DC-ViT) regulates this by separating updates computed within a channel from updates computed across channels, and by forming a representation per channel before the channels are combined. Its formulation, however, pairs tokens by spatial position, and thus requires the same visible tokens in every channel. Correspondence under independent per-channel masking is recovered by solving a linear assignment between the retained patches of each channel, which allows decoupled attention to be combined with current masked multi-channel training in its standard configuration rather than a restricted one. Across three classification and three segmentation benchmarks spanning fluorescence microscopy, imaging mass cytometry and satellite imaging, including dense prediction at high channel counts, the resulting formulation outperforms the strongest MC-ViT baseline.

</details>


### [52] [Configurable Multi-Stage Vision Pipeline for Crop Disease and Pest Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21651)
*Naga Ganesh,Chandrashekar M S,Lakshmi Pedapudi,Aakash Singh,Vineet Singh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文报告了 Digital Green 旗下小农农业咨询服务平台 Farmer.Chat 的视觉诊断系统重构工作。作者将"照片即问题"的端到端诊断拆解为质量门控（M0）、作物识别（M1）、病虫害/病害检测（M2）三级流水线，并在约 116 万张来自埃塞俄比亚、印度、肯尼亚和尼日利亚的野外手机照片上，对比了"单一微调 VLM（Qwen3-VL-4B）一次调用全包"（Route A）与"小型专用模型逐级处理"（Route B）两条路线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Farmer.Chat 的输入极其受限：农民只上传一张照片，不描述症状、不指明作物，往往完全没有文字。系统需要在光照差、相机抖动、低成本手机拍摄的条件下，判断照片是否可用、识别作物、并诊断问题。然而现有生产系统是一个不可调整的黑盒：没有可配置的照片拒绝阈值，无法新增作物或病害类别，也没有置信度截断机制。对 116 万张照片的分析揭示了三类真实失效：生产质量门控拒绝了其所判定图像的 46.8%；进入诊断流程的图像中超过四分之一未能返回作物名称；在标注为"disease"的问题里，35.8% 实为病虫害（pest），且无需作物信息即可识别。这说明将可用性判断、作物识别、问题诊断混为一体既不可解释也不可迭代，因此作者主张将其显式解耦为三级任务。

Method: 核心方法是将诊断流程分解为三个可独立调整的阶段：M0 质量门控（判断照片是否可用于诊断）、M1 作物检测、M2 病害或虫害检测。作者提出两条实现路线：Route A 采用单一微调视觉语言模型 Qwen3-VL-4B，在一次调用中同时输出三个阶段的结果；Route B 为每个阶段配置小型专用模型，涉及 DaViT（尤其是层级化的 DaViT-Base 作为作物检测器）和 YOLO26，并将生产环境的 GPT-4o 质量门控替换为轻量级 MobileNetV3 门控。该分解的关键设计价值在于：各阶段可独立设置拒绝阈值、可增量扩展作物与病害类别、可接入置信度截断，从而解决原系统不可调节的问题。Route A 的微调模型额外保留了两项专用模型不具备的能力：单次调用完成三阶段，以及在图像不足以支撑回答时主动请求重新拍摄更清晰的照片。

Result: 数据规模为约 1.16 百万张来自四个国家的真实田间照片。质量门控方面，MobileNetV3 门控以 86.9% F1、12 ms 推理延迟替代生产 GPT-4o 门控，兼顾准确率与效率。在与所有系统统一评分的同一测试集上，层级式 DaViT-Base 取得 95.41% 作物识别准确率，高于生产基线的 91.46%；在诊断任务上同样领先，且从不拒答；相比之下所有被比较的语言模型都留下大量"无诊断"的空行。这些结果支持"小型专用模型 + 显式阶段划分"在当前任务上优于通用大模型直接作答的结论。

Conclusion: 本文的主要贡献在于：基于百万级真实野外数据刻画了农业视觉诊断的实际失效模式（高拒绝率、高无作物率、虫害与病害混淆），并据此提出可调节的三阶段分解框架，同时给出两条可落地路线——单模型 VLM 与多专家模型组合。其实验证明轻量专用模型（MobileNetV3、DaViT-Base）在准确率与延迟上均可超越生产级大模型基线，而微调 VLM 则以"一次调用 + 主动求拍"的交互能力作为互补优势。局限方面：摘要未披露 M2 诊断的具体准确率数值与置信度截断的量化效果，也未给出跨国家/光照/机型的细分消融，标签噪声（35.8% 的"disease"实为虫害）虽被识别但其对训练与评估的影响未在摘要中量化，且 12 ms 延迟的测试硬件平台也未说明。

Abstract: Farmer.Chat is Digital Green's farm advisory service for smallholder farmers. When something looks wrong with a crop, the farmer takes a photograph and sends it, and that photograph is the whole question: no symptom described, no crop named, often no text at all. The service has to determine whether the picture can be used, what crop it shows, and what is wrong with it, from images taken on cheap phones in a field, in poor light and with a moving camera. The system doing this today cannot be adjusted. It has no adjustable thresholds for photograph rejection, crops and problems cannot be added, and there is no confidence cut-off to set.
  We study about 1.16 million photographs sent to Farmer.Chat from Ethiopia, India, Kenya and Nigeria. The production quality gate rejected 46.8% of the images it judged, over a quarter of those reaching diagnosis returned no crop name, and 35.8% of the labelled problems filed under "disease" are pests, identifiable without the crop. We therefore split the work into three stages: a quality gate (M0), a crop detector (M1), and a disease or pest detector (M2). Route A fills all three with one fine-tuned vision-language model (Qwen3-VL-4B) answering in a single call. Route B fills each with a small specialist model (DaViT, YOLO26).
  We replace our production GPT-4o quality gate with a small MobileNetV3 gate at 86.9% F1 in 12 ms. On one test set scored the same way for every system, a hierarchical DaViT-Base achieves 95.41% crop accuracy against 91.46% for the production baseline. It also leads on diagnosis and never declines to answer, while every language model in the comparison leaves a large share of rows with no diagnosis. The fine-tuned model retains two capabilities the specialists do not have: one call for all three stages, and a request for a better photograph when the image cannot support an answer.

</details>


### [53] [DRT: Dense Reasoning Trace for Efficient and Grounded Multimodal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21675)
*Wan Xu,Yuanfan Guo,Kevin Han,LaLa Chen,Wangmeng Zuo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Dense Reasoning Trace（DRT），一种脱离自然语言中心式 Chain-of-Thought（CoT）的多模态推理范式，将推理过程表达为紧凑的结构化轨迹（包含符号连接符与原子化中间状态），在保持视觉证据的同时大幅提升 token 效率。通过 Dense Trace Initialization 与 Trace-Grounded Reinforcement Learning 两阶段框架，在挑战性推理基准上相较 Qwen3-VL 基线实现约 5.5× 的 token 效率提升并提高 1.3 个准确率点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态大模型（MLLM）的 CoT 推理普遍受限于"自然语言表达空间"：推理轨迹需要以完整、流畅的语句书写，导致三方面问题：(1) 语言层面的冗余开销过大，token 消耗高、推理成本高；(2) 自然语言描述会稀释关键信息，冗长的叙述使视觉证据与逻辑推理混杂，造成信息稀释（information dilution）；(3) 视觉 grounding 变弱——大量语言化描述反而削弱了模型对图像证据的忠实利用，并易引发幻觉。因此，作者希望跳出"用自然语言思考"的路径依赖，探索更紧凑、更贴近逻辑结构本身的推理表达方式，从而提升 MLLM 推理的效率与可靠性。

Method: 核心方法是 Dense Reasoning Trace（DRT）范式，将推理从自然语言句子改为"紧凑结构化轨迹"：轨迹由简短的中间状态（intermediate states）、符号化连接符（symbolic connectors）构成，并显式地将视觉观测（visual observations）与逻辑推演（logical deductions）解耦。为让模型学会该模式，作者设计了两阶段框架：
1) Dense Trace Initialization：在初始化阶段将 DRT 推理模式内化到模型中，使模型能以结构化稠密轨迹形式输出，在压缩 token 的同时保留视觉证据。
2) Trace-Grounded Reinforcement Learning：为让模型忠实捕捉轨迹中的逻辑关系，构建参考轨迹（reference traces）——通过 tri-perspective verification pipeline（三视角验证流程）生成可靠参考；并采用 Trace-Grounded GRPO，配合结构化奖励（structured rewards），鼓励模型生成简洁的 DRT 风格轨迹，从而降低幻觉、增强逻辑 grounding。
关键词式创新点在于：结构化/符号化的推理表达、视觉与逻辑的显式解耦、面向轨迹忠实度的强化学习奖励设计。

Result: 论文在具有挑战性的多模态推理基准上进行了大量实验，基线为 Qwen3-VL。主要结果：DRT 在保持甚至提升准确率的前提下，实现了约 5.5× 的 token 效率提升，并相较 Qwen3-VL 基线提升约 1.3 个准确率点（accuracy points）。这表明紧凑结构化推理轨迹能够替代冗长的自然语言 CoT，在不牺牲（反而提升）推理性能的同时显著降低语言开销。作者公开了代码与数据（https://github.com/HIT-leaderone/DRT）。摘要未给出具体数据集名称、评测指标细节与消融配置。

Conclusion: 本文的主要贡献是提出 DRT 这一非自然语言中心的多模态推理范式，并配套 Dense Trace Initialization 与 Trace-Grounded RL（含三视角验证与结构化奖励 GRPO）两阶段训练框架，验证了"复杂多模态推理并不必然依赖冗长自然语言轨迹"这一假设，为下一代 MLLM 提供了一条更高效的推理路径。意义在于同时改善了 token 效率与推理可靠性（减少幻觉、增强逻辑与视觉 grounding）。局限性方面：摘要未披露所评估的具体基准、模型规模与消融实验，其结构化轨迹设计与 tri-perspective 验证流程的通用性、以及在大规模/开放域任务上的可扩展性仍有待进一步验证。

Abstract: Despite the remarkable progress in Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs), prevailing Chain-of-Thought (CoT) paradigms remain confined to the natural-language expression space. Consequently, they inherently incur excessive linguistic overhead, leading to information dilution and weak visual grounding. To address this challenge, we propose Dense Reasoning Trace (DRT), a paradigm that departs from natural-language-centered CoT by expressing reasoning as compact structured traces, which include concise intermediate states with symbolic connectors and disentangle visual observations from logical deductions. First, we introduce the Dense Trace Initialization to internalize the DRT reasoning mode into the model, substantially improving token efficiency while preserving visual evidence. To further enable the model to faithfully capture the logical relations within traces, we propose the Trace-Grounded Reinforcement Learning framework, which builds reference traces through a tri-perspective verification pipeline and employs Trace-Grounded GRPO with structured rewards, encouraging the model to generate concise DRT-style traces with reduced hallucination and stronger logical grounding. Extensive experiments on challenging reasoning benchmarks show that DRT achieves 5.5$\times$ token efficiency improvement while improving 1.3 accuracy points over the Qwen3-VL baseline. These findings suggest that complex multimodal reasoning may not require verbose natural-language traces, opening a more efficient path for next-generation MLLMs. Our code and data are available at: https://github.com/HIT-leaderone/DRT

</details>


### [54] [Diffusion-Based Tumor Inpainting for Renal Segmentation under Clinical Data Scarcity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21698)
*Ekaterina Sedykh,Salme Ussanov,Dmytro Fedorenko,Dmytro Fishman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于扩散修复（inpainting）的肾脏肿瘤合成框架，在健康CT中生成解剖合理的肿瘤，无需额外标注；并首次系统比较2D、2.5D与全3D（MAISI）合成策略。核心发现：2.5D在低数据场景下可媲美全3D，同时显著降低假阳性且计算成本更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习肾脏肿瘤分割依赖大规模标注数据，但临床实际部署中目标站点往往仅有少量肿瘤阳性病例，导致数据稀缺、模型易产生大量假阳性。传统数据增强难以生成解剖合理且位置多样的肿瘤，限制了模型在低数据临床场景下的泛化与可靠性。

Method: 提出基于扩散模型的修复式合成框架：在健康CT扫描中生成解剖学合理的肾脏肿瘤，无需额外标注。系统对比三类合成策略——2D切片级、2.5D（局部体素/切片邻域）与全3D（MAISI框架）。扩散模型在公开数据（KiTS23、KIRC）上训练，合成样本用于增强nnU-Net分割模型训练。

Result: 在内部队列上以三种低数据量设置评估nnU-Net分割性能。结果显示：2.5D与3D增强将假阳性从约18–20%降至约3–6%，同时保持Dice分数；2D合成无一致收益。关键结论：2.5D方法在所有指标上匹配全3D合成，但计算成本显著更低，说明仅局部体素一致性即足以实现有效增强。

Conclusion: 贡献在于无需标注的扩散修复肿瘤合成框架，以及对2D/2.5D/3D合成策略的首个系统对比，证明局部体素一致性足以支撑有效数据增强，适合数据与资源受限的临床场景。局限：仅在肾脏肿瘤CT单一任务验证，内部队列评估，泛化性与其他器官/模态有待进一步验证。

Abstract: Deep learning segmentation of renal tumors requires large annotated datasets, yet clinical deployments typically offer only a handful of tumor-positive cases from the target site. We propose a diffusion-based inpainting framework that synthesizes anatomically plausible renal tumors within healthy CT scans, requiring no additional annotation, and provide the first systematic comparison of 2D, 2.5D, and full 3D (MAISI) synthesis strategies for this task. Training the diffusion model on public data (KiTS23, KIRC) and evaluating nnU-Net segmentation on a internal cohort across three low-data regimes, we find that 2.5D and 3D augmentation substantially reduce false positives (from $\sim$18--20\% to $\sim$3--6\%) while maintaining Dice, whereas 2D provides no consistent benefit. Crucially, the proposed 2.5D method matches full 3D synthesis on every metric at substantially lower computational cost, indicating that local volumetric consistency alone is sufficient for effective augmentation in data- and resource-scarce clinical settings.

</details>


### [55] [SignGPT: Toward LLM-Mediated Sign Language Interaction through Gloss-Free Translation and Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21709)
*Ronghui Li,Jun Dong,Zhongyuan Hu,Zunnan Xu,Jun Zhou,Liyuan Chen,Shuoling Liu,Jiangpeng Yan,Jie Guo,Xiu Li,Linchao Bao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SignGPT 是一个统一的、基于姿态（pose-based）、免 gloss 的框架，将手语翻译（SLT）与手语生成（SLG）整合进同一个共享语言模型，通过部件感知的分层身体/手/面部表示以及非对称多 token 预测与渐进式训练，实现双向手语-文本交互，并在对话场景中支持手语到手的应答流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型对手语交互支持有限，且 SLT 与 SLG 通常由独立模型分别处理，导致手语-文本往返交互时需频繁切换模型，难以支撑连贯的手语对话。作者希望统一建模手语作为输入与输出，从而减少模型切换、提升手语交互的流畅性与可用性，并服务聋人手语使用者。采用免 gloss（gloss-free）与姿态表示也可避免对昂贵 gloss 标注的依赖，并规避像素级生成的隐私与外观敏感问题。

Method: SignGPT 是一个基于姿态的统一框架。核心做法包括：(1) 将身体、手部与面部运动解耦为部件感知（part-aware）的分层表示，并映射到共享语言模型的特征空间，使不同模态（手语姿态与文本）在同一模型内对齐与生成；(2) 采用非对称多 token 预测（asymmetric multi-token prediction），适应 SLT 与 SLG 两个方向在序列长度、信息密度与预测难度上的差异；(3) 引入渐进式训练（progressive training）策略，逐步从较易任务过渡到双向联合建模，以稳定跨模态、双向任务的联合优化。整体上模型不依赖 gloss 中间表示，并共享同一语言模型骨干完成翻译与生成。

Result: 在 How2Sign（ASL）与 Phoenix-2014T（DGS）两个手语数据集上进行评估，开展了与基准方法的对比、定性分析以及部件消融实验，验证统一建模范式与各组件（如部件感知表示、多 token 预测、渐进训练）的有效性。此外，作者与 12 名聋人 ASL 手语者开展探索性用户研究，评估由 LLM 中介的手语到手语应答流水线（sign-to-sign），结果表明统一建模在支持手语对话（SLC）方面具有潜力。

Conclusion: SignGPT 的主要贡献在于提出首个（或少见的）统一、免 gloss、基于姿态的 SLT+SLG 框架，通过部件感知分层表示、非对称多 token 预测与渐进训练，在同一语言模型中实现双向手语建模，并以 Deaf 手语者用户研究初步验证手语对话可行性。局限方面，评估仅限 How2Sign 与 Phoenix-2014T 两个数据集，用户研究样本量较小（12 名手语者）且为探索性质；姿态表示虽简化了建模，但可能丢失非手动细节与语义，生成的自然度与泛化性仍需更大规模验证。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) provide limited support for sign language interaction. Unifying sign language translation (SLT) and generation (SLG) to enable sign language as both input and output can reduce switching between separate models during sign-text interaction. We present SignGPT, a unified, pose-based framework for gloss-free SLT and SLG. SignGPT integrates part-aware hierarchical representations of body, hand, and facial motion into a shared language model and employs asymmetric multi-token prediction and progressive training for bidirectional modeling. We evaluate SignGPT on How2Sign (ASL) and Phoenix-2014T (DGS) through benchmark comparisons, qualitative analyses, and component ablations. An exploratory study with 12 Deaf ASL signers assesses an LLM-mediated sign-to-sign response pipeline, highlighting the potential of unified modeling to support sign language conversation (SLC).

</details>


### [56] [ZYT-World: A Real-Time Controllable World Model for Closed-Loop Autonomous-Driving Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21712)
*Boni Hu,Xiong Wei,Haoming Huang,Yong Huang,Chenbo Wang,Yi Yang,Jiancheng Wang,Ruicheng Zhu,Zhimin Yang,Guanglai Liu,Qiaowan Jin,Dongzhuo Wang,Haiwei Kuang,Jiajun Fan,Yue Wu,Jiaxin Wei,Hao Sun,Feihong Yan,Wei Bi,Kaixuan Wang,Zichao Guo,Xiaozhi Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ZYT-World 是一个用于自动驾驶策略闭环仿真的生成式世界模型，用单一架构原生生成 4 路鱼眼（FOV>180°）+3 路针孔视图，并通过蒸馏与工程优化，将 40 步双向教师模型压缩为单步、逐 latent 流式生成器，同时用隐式记忆模块保证重访场景的身份一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式世界模型能为端到端（E2E）与视觉-语言-动作（VLA）驾驶策略提供可控、可重复的闭环仿真，但实际部署面临三个未解决问题：(1) 如何在原生分辨率下忠实复现鱼眼与针孔混合相机装置；(2) 如何在因果、逐时间步交互与长时程稳定性、低延迟之间取得平衡；(3) 场景被重访时如何保持地点/场景身份一致性。这些问题直接影响仿真数据的几何真实性与在线闭环可用性。

Method: 核心方法包含几何、控制、条件、训练与部署多层设计：几何上使用投影特定的 Plücker 适配器（projection-specific Plücker adapters）编码相机几何，支持 4 路鱼眼 + 3 路针孔；控制上采用自车运动自适应层归一化（ego-motion adaptive layer normalization）实现全局运动控制；条件上用轻量像素对齐布局（pixel-aligned layout）以实例级框、朝向、颜色注入交通参与者与信号信息；训练采用异构训练（heterogeneous training）兼顾全装置几何覆盖与高分辨率细节。蒸馏方面，通过 teacher forcing、因果一致性蒸馏（causal consistency distillation）、自 rollout 分布匹配蒸馏（self-rollout distribution matching distillation）以及 RigCritic（对七视图装置整体评估），把 40 步双向教师转为单步、逐 latent 的流式生成器。部署上用 19M 参数 VAE 解码器 TinyVAE、W8A8 量化及自研推理引擎分别降低解码、主干与增量执行开销；此外用真实采集构造的跨轨迹数据对训练插件式隐式记忆模块，保留地点特定证据。

Result: 在内部多视图测试集上：单步模型保留教师模型超过 90% 的 PSNR 与 SSIM，FID、FVD、LPIPS 与教师差距在 11% 以内；按图 2 的纯生成器计时，速度比 40 步双向教师快 107.7 倍；TinyVAE 解码比 Wan 快 59.8 倍。30 秒 rollout 与跨轨迹重访实验验证了预期的长时程稳定性与记忆行为。

Conclusion: 主要贡献是一个统一的多视图混合相机装置生成式世界模型框架，兼具原生鱼眼-针孔几何、单步流式因果生成、显式工程加速（TinyVAE/W8A8/推理引擎）和插件式隐式记忆，用于长时程闭环驾驶仿真。其意义在于将重型双向教师蒸馏为可实时逐 latent 生成的模型并保持接近教师的感知指标。局限包括：评测主要在内部多视图测试集上进行，缺少公开基准对比；量化与速度数据依赖特定计时设置（图 2 生成器-only 计时）；记忆与长时程优势以定性/分布层面展示，尚未给出重访一致性的量化指标。

Abstract: Generative world models offer controllable and repeatable closed-loop simulation for end-to-end and vision-language-action driving policies, but production deployment exposes three unresolved requirements: faithfully reproducing a mixed fisheye-pinhole rig at native resolutions; reconciling causal, per-timestep interaction with long-horizon stability and low latency; and preserving scene identity when a location is revisited. We present ZYT-World, a single architecture that natively generates four fisheye views with field of view > 180° and three pinhole views. Projection-specific Plucker adapters encode camera geometry, ego-motion adaptive layer normalization provides global motion control, and a lightweight pixel-aligned layout conditions traffic participants and signals through instance-level boxes, headings and colors. Heterogeneous training combines full-rig geometric coverage with high-resolution detail. Teacher forcing, causal consistency distillation, self-rollout distribution matching distillation, and RigCritic transform a 40-step bidirectional teacher into a one-step, per-latent streaming generator, with RigCritic evaluating the seven-view rig jointly.
