The resulting policy selects footholds on permitted support and keeps the leg clear of forbidden regions throughout the swing phase. We perform a systematic ablation that isolates the contribution of each architectural component; removing the semantic bias alone increases forbidden contacts by 55% while velocity tracking is unchanged. We validate the policy on a Unitree B2, demonstrating sim-to-real semantic contact selection across indoor and outdoor environments and four semantic obstacle classes.

</details>


### [140] [Tilt as a Certified Resource: Preserving Motor Wrench-Rate Authority on Articulated Multirotors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21580)
*Giuseppe Silano,Martin Saska*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出利用主动形态（servo tilt）作为几何资源，通过构造仅依赖电机的"准备度证书"（可达力矩速率集合的对数体积）并将其作为控制障碍函数（CBF）嵌入统一物理指令二次规划（QP），在严重阵风扰动下为全驱动多旋翼保持了动态权威（readiness）的安全下限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全驱动多旋翼不仅要跟踪标称力矩，还必须在扰动下快速调制力矩，即保持"准备度"。传统的最小努力分配器忽略了这一动态极限；而以最大化准备度为目标会导致最优解集在拓扑上不连通，并要求物理上不可能的执行器速率。在固定几何的对称平台上强制准备度安全下限还会遇到零和退化：单纯重分配电机转速无法在不牺牲力矩跟踪的前提下提升权威。因此需要引入新的自由度来打破这一退化。

Method: 论文以主动形态作为突破口，将伺服倾转（servo tilt）视为一种几何资源，提供静态旋翼所不具备的权威恢复方向。核心构造是一个与构型相关、仅含电机的准备度证书——即可达力矩速率集合的对数体积（log-volume），并显式排除伺服容量，以避免"幽灵容量谬误"（ghost capacity fallacy），即在电机饱和时错误地把慢速机械运动学极限计入容量，而非正确地反映权威塌缩。该证书被作为控制障碍函数（CBF）嵌入一个统一物理指令二次规划（Unified Physical-Command QP）中，优化变量同时作用于电机力矩与伺服设定点。

Result: 在严重阵风扰动下对一个带铰接的八旋翼（articulated octorotor）进行了闭环仿真。结果显示：经典分配器发散；未认证的铰接式分配器违反准备度安全下限；而所提出的 CBF 滤波器能够约束系统状态并保持飞行器权威。研究以仿真验证为主，未报告真实平台实验。

Conclusion: 论文的主要贡献在于：（1）用主动形态打破固定几何平台的权威—跟踪零和退化；（2）提出仅依赖电机、显式排除伺服容量的准备度证书以规避幽灵容量谬误；（3）通过 CBF-QP 框架实现准备度安全下限的在线强制。其意义在于将"准备度"这一动态能力概念形式化为可约束的安全量。局限在于目前仅基于闭环仿真验证，未涉及实物实验与传感器噪声、执行器延迟等实际因素。

Abstract: Fully-actuated multirotor aerial vehicles must not only track nominal wrenches but retain the "readiness" to modulate them rapidly under disturbances. Classical effort-minimizing allocators ignore this dynamic limit, whereas maximizing readiness leads to topologically disconnected optimal sheets demanding physically impossible actuator rates. Enforcing a readiness safety floor on fixed-geometry symmetric platforms further encounters a zero-sum degeneracy: motor-speed redistribution cannot improve authority without conceding wrench tracking. This paper uses active morphology to break the degeneracy, treating servo tilt as a geometric resource supplying authority-recovery directions unavailable to static rotors. We construct a configuration-dependent, motor-only readiness certificate - the log-volume of the reachable wrench-rate set - that explicitly excludes servo capacity, preventing a "ghost capacity fallacy" in which the certificate would falsely credit slow mechanical kinematic limits instead of collapsing accurately at motor saturation. The certificate is enforced as a Control Barrier Function (CBF) within a Unified Physical-Command Quadratic Program acting on motor torques and servo setpoints. Closed-loop simulations of an articulated octorotor under severe gust disturbances show classical allocators diverging and uncertified articulated allocators violating the safety floor, while the proposed CBF filter bounds the system state and preserves vehicle authority.

</details>


### [141] [A High-Payload Wall-Climbing Robot Using Passive Bistable Suction Cups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21584)
*Andrew Nguyen,Mingyuan Li,Daniel Bruder*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种高负载壁面爬行机器人，采用被动双稳态吸盘实现无源粘附，并通过轨道式机构自动啮合与脱开吸盘完成移动，可垂直攀爬多种光滑表面并拖曳7.940 kg负载（负载自重比2.25）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 垂直壁面爬行机器人可用于窗户清洗、检测、维护与建筑等危险或高人力消耗任务的自动化。主动粘附方法（如负压风扇、磁吸、推进吸附）负载能力更强，但维持吸附需持续供电；被动粘附（如普通吸盘）无需耗电维持吸力，却受限于负载能力。因此，如何在保持被动粘附"零功耗维持"优势的同时提升承载能力，是该领域的关键挑战。

Method: 核心创新是采用被动双稳态吸盘（bistable suction cups）产生粘附力，无需将吸盘压向壁面即可形成并保持吸附，利用双稳态结构在两个稳定状态间切换：吸附态（贴附、保持密封）与释放态。机器人搭载轨道式（track-based）驱动系统，通过轨道的运动自动完成吸盘的啮合（engage）与脱开（disengage），实现连续的吸附—释放循环，从而在光滑表面上产生推进和移动。该设计将"吸附保持"与"驱动推进"解耦：吸附由无源双稳态结构维持，驱动仅需负责切换状态，因此降低了对持续功率的需求，同时被动吸盘的密封结构可提供较高负载能力。

Result: 机器人能够在玻璃、木材、金属和涂漆表面实现垂直壁面攀爬，并支持侧向爬行和倒挂（upside-down）爬行，表明其在多种材质光滑面上的适应性。关键性能指标为可拖曳 7.940 kg 的负载，负载自重比达到 2.25，验证了被动双稳态吸盘方案在高负载场景下的可行性。摘要未给出爬行速度、表面粗糙度上限、吸盘循环寿命或功耗等定量数据。

Conclusion: 该工作提出并验证了一种以被动双稳态吸盘为核心的高负载壁面爬行机器人，通过轨道式自动啮合/脱开机构实现无持续供能的移动，在多种材质垂直表面完成攀爬并实现 2.25 的负载自重比，为危险与高人力任务的自动化提供了低成本、低功耗的机械方案。潜在局限包括：被动吸盘对表面条件（光滑度、平整度、洁净度）可能较敏感，难以应对粗糙或非平整壁面；仅给出拖曳负载结果，缺乏爬行速度、循环寿命、功耗与失效模式等系统级量化评估；多材质适应性与长期可靠性仍需更多实验验证。

Abstract: Wall-climbing robots capable of scaling vertical surfaces could help automate hazardous or labor intensive tasks such as window washing, inspection, maintenance, and construction. Active adhesion methods achieve higher payload capacities, but require power to maintain their grip. Passive adhesion devices such as suction cups are an attractive option for such robots because they do not require power to maintain their grip, but they are limited by their payload capacity. This work presents a novel high-payload wall-climbing robot that utilizes passive bistable suction cups to generate adhesion without needing to be pushed into the wall. The robot features a track-based system that automatically engages and disengages bistable suction cups to achieve locomotion on smooth surfaces. The robot is able to achieve vertical wall climbing on glass, wood, metal, and painted surfaces, sideways and upside-down climbing, and is able to tow a payload of 7.940 kg (with a payload-to-weight ratio of 2.25).

</details>


### [142] [Potential-Field Action Representation for Reinforcement Learning in Contact-Rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21609)
*Xinyu Liu,Gökhan Solak,Arash Ajoudani*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PA-RL 提出以人工势场（Artificial Potential Field）作为强化学习的动作表示：策略不直接输出运动指令，而是调节能量型势场的参数，由势场生成状态相关的引导方向，再经笛卡尔阻抗控制器执行。该接口在接触密集的轴孔装配任务中将任务级策略与底层运动生成解耦。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无模型强化学习可在试错交互中获得接触密集的机器人操作技能，但策略常需同时学习任务策略与底层运动生成。动作表示决定了策略输出如何转化为机器人运动，直接影响探索效率与物理执行。直接笛卡尔指令接口（如笛卡尔速度/位姿）要求策略在每个决策步生成运动，将任务级自适应与连续低级控制耦合，加大了学习负担，并在接触切换处容易产生高加速度与力矩波动。作者希望设计一种动作表示，让策略只负责"往哪走"的高层引导，而把平滑、合规的底层执行交给控制器。

Method: PA-RL 的核心是把动作空间替换为人工势场的参数空间：策略在每个决策步输出势场参数，势场依据当前状态构造出类似能量的场，并由此产生状态相关的引导方向；该方向向量被送入笛卡尔阻抗控制器，转化为实际机器人运动。这样策略无需逐步生成完整运动轨迹，而是通过改变场形态间接影响运动趋势。作者在相同 RL 算法下，将 PA-RL 与三种基线动作空间比较：笛卡尔速度（Cartesian velocity）、笛卡尔位姿（Cartesian pose）以及可变阻抗（variable-impedance）动作空间。任务为轴孔装配（peg-in-hole insertion），具有非线性动力学与不连续的接触状态转移。奖励中未加入显式的运动质量惩罚项。

Result: 实验在仿真中与三类基线进行同算法对比，评价指标包括训练时间内的评估成功率以及运动质量指标（关节力矩变化、笛卡尔加速度变化）。PA-RL 是唯一在给定训练时间内达到 100% 评估成功率的方法，最佳基线为 92.6%。运动质量方面，相较最佳基线，PA-RL 将关节力矩变化降低 55.4%，笛卡尔加速度变化降低 70.8%，且这一切是在奖励中未加入显式运动质量惩罚的前提下实现的。此外，仿真训练得到的策略无需微调即可在真实机器人上完成 9/9 次插入，验证了所学势场接口的实机部署可行性。

Conclusion: PA-RL 的主要贡献在于提出以人工势场作为 RL 动作表示，将高层任务策略与底层运动生成解耦，同时借助笛卡尔阻抗控制器保证执行平滑与合规。其在接触密集装配任务上同时提升成功率与运动质量，并展示仿真到实机的零微调迁移能力。潜在局限包括：实验仅覆盖轴孔装配这一单类任务，任务泛化性有待验证；势场参数化形式、势场对复杂/动态环境的适应能力以及安全性（局部极小值）问题未在摘要中讨论；此外方法依赖笛卡尔阻抗控制器这一底层执行条件。

Abstract: Model-free reinforcement learning can acquire contact-rich robotic manipulation skills through trial-and-error interaction, but it often requires the policy to learn both task strategy and low-level motion generation. In this setting, the action representation is critical because it determines how policy outputs are converted into robot motion, shaping both exploration and physical execution. Direct Cartesian command interfaces require the policy to generate motion at every decision step, coupling task-level adaptation with continuous low-level control and increasing the learning burden. We propose PA-RL, a reinforcement-learning framework that uses artificial potential fields as the action representation. Instead of commanding motion directly, the policy adapts the parameters of an energy-like potential field, which generates a state-dependent guidance direction executed through a Cartesian impedance controller. We evaluate PA-RL on peg-in-hole insertion, a representative contact-rich task with nonlinear dynamics and discontinuous contact transitions. In simulation, PA-RL is compared with Cartesian velocity, Cartesian pose, and variable-impedance action spaces using the same RL algorithm. PA-RL is the only method to reach a 100% evaluation success rate within the allotted training time, while the best baseline reaches 92.6%. It also reduces joint-torque variation by 55.4% and Cartesian acceleration variation by 70.8% relative to the best baseline, without explicit motion-quality penalties in the reward. The simulation-trained policy further completes 9/9 real-robot insertions without fine-tuning, demonstrating the deployment feasibility of the learned potential-field interface.

</details>


### [143] [CounterPlay: Counterfactual Post-Training for Self-Play Driving Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21617)
*Jiarong Wei,Yin Wu,Runkai He,Abhinav Valada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 CounterPlay，一种反事实自博弈后训练方法，通过从失败任务回溯到更早状态并以不同驾驶风格重试，用极少算力显著提升自动驾驶策略的闭环性能与安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高吞吐模拟器中的自博弈（self-play）能训练出闭环表现稳健的驾驶策略，但随着训练规模扩大，单位模拟量带来的性能提升递减：策略在早期就学会处理常见场景，后续大量 rollout 只是反复遭遇少数未解决的失败情形。现有后训练方法主要在被访问过的状态上评估替代动作或延续轨迹（即在同一状态局部试错），但真正的成功恢复往往需要更早地改变驾驶风格，这类方法难以触及。因此需要一种能回溯到失败之前、系统性探索替代驾驶风格的后训练机制，以较低成本突破自博弈的性能瓶颈。

Method: CounterPlay 包含三个关键组件：(1) 失败驱动回溯（failure-driven backtracking）：利用策略的价值估计，从失败任务中选出一个更早的存储状态作为重试起点，从失败点向前回溯到更有利的分叉点。(2) 奖励条件化（reward conditioning）：用单一策略在该状态下以从保守（cautious）到激进（aggressive）的候选驾驶风格重试任务，即通过奖励条件控制策略风格而非训练多个独立策略。(3) 验证式筛选：仅当某次完成任务的重试未使其它车辆产生相对于事实分支（factual branch）新的或更早发生的碰撞、或新的偏离道路事件时，才保留该重试；通过验证且带新随机性的重试被蒸馏回策略在其部署条件下的行为。整体框架为反事实式的自我对弈后训练，核心在于从失败中回溯并探索风格空间。

Result: 在 BehaviorBench 基准上评估，CounterPlay 在 Interactive 和 Random 两个 split、全部八种交通场景下均取得 state-of-the-art 分数，仅使用 1B 后训练 transition，约为 anchor 策略 100B 自博弈训练预算的 1%。提升在评估的全部三种驾驶风格上均成立。CounterPlay 解决了 anchor 在 BehaviorBench 上相当一部分超时（timeout）案例，并在任务完成与安全性之间取得平衡——这是持续自博弈（continued self-play）或采用更激进驾驶风格都无法同时达到的。

Conclusion: 主要贡献：提出反事实自博弈后训练框架 CounterPlay，通过失败驱动回溯 + 奖励条件化风格重试 + 安全性验证，用极少算力（1% 预算）在 BehaviorBench 上达到 SOTA，兼顾任务完成与安全。意义：为闭环驾驶策略的高效后训练提供了新范式，说明从失败处回溯并改变驾驶风格比在访问状态上局部评估更有效。局限：文中未明确讨论风格候选集的构造方式、价值估计回溯的精度依赖、验证规则对复杂交互场景的完备性，以及方法向真实驾驶域迁移的可行性。

Abstract: Self-play in high-throughput simulators yields driving policies with robust closed-loop performance, but improvement per unit of simulation diminishes as training scales. Policies learn to handle common situations early, while further rollouts repeatedly encounter unresolved failures. Post-training offers an opportunity to target these failures, but existing methods primarily evaluate alternative actions or continuations at visited states, although successful recovery may require changing driving style earlier. We propose CounterPlay, a counterfactual self-play post-training approach that backtracks from failed tasks and retries them under alternate driving styles. CounterPlay rests on three key components. First, failure-driven backtracking uses the policy's value estimates to select an earlier stored state from which to retry the task. Second, reward conditioning enables a single policy to retry the task from this state using candidate styles ranging from cautious to aggressive. Third, CounterPlay retains task-completing retries only if no other vehicle incurs a new or earlier collision or off-road event relative to the factual branch. Retries that pass verification with fresh randomness are then distilled into the policy under its deployment condition. On BehaviorBench, CounterPlay achieves state-of-the-art scores on both the Interactive and Random splits across all eight traffic regimes using 1B post-training transitions, which is just 1% of the anchor's 100B self-play training budget. Improvements over the anchor hold across all three evaluated driving styles. CounterPlay resolves a substantial fraction of the anchor's timeout cases on BehaviorBench and achieves a balance between task completion and safety that neither continued self-play nor adopting a more aggressive driving style attains.

</details>


### [144] [Towards Fine-Grained Object Manipulation: SAM3-Guided Visuomotor Policy with Persistent Memory Learning and Focused Visual Conditioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21621)
*Haolong Meng,Fangbo Qin,Mengchen Bai,Houwu Wang,Cirong Liu,Shan Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 SAM3 引导的视觉运动（visuomotor）框架，通过"持久目标记忆"与"聚焦式视觉条件"解决细粒度物体（FO）操作难题：学习可复用的 FO 记忆 token 定位并拒绝相似干扰物，并将框内局部外观与显式边界框坐标联合用于条件化动作策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 细粒度物体操作要求机器人既能从视觉相似物体中区分出被指定的目标 FO，又能在存在场景干扰物的情况下可靠执行动作。现有视觉条件方式存在两类缺陷：场景级（scene-level）视觉条件缺乏显式的物体选择机制，无法指出"操作哪一个"；类别级（category-level）引导虽能给出类别信息，但无法在同一类别内部可靠区分不同 FO。因此，如何在可扩展、可复用的前提下实现目标级、细粒度的视觉条件化，是该问题的核心。

Method: 框架包含两个组件。其一为 FOM-SAM3（FO Memory-driven SAM3）：从有限的多视角配准图像中学习可复用的 FO 记忆 token，SAM3 主干完全冻结（fully frozen），仅训练记忆 token；通过 one-vs-rest 学习策略，使 token 编码持久的目标记忆，用于定位目标 FO 并拒绝相似替代物，所得 token 可存入 memory bank 以支持新物体扩展。其二为聚焦式空间-外观编码（Focused Spatial-Appearance Encoding, FSAE）：将 FO 内部的局部外观特征与显式边界框坐标相结合，作为动作策略的条件输入，适配 Diffusion Policy（DP）与 Action Chunking with Transformers（ACT）等策略。

Result: 在包含 4 个粗类别、30 个物理物体的 FO-30 数据集上验证了 FOM-SAM3 的有效性。在三个真实机器人 FO 操作任务中，FOM-SAM3 引导的策略表现出对干扰物的鲁棒性、在相似 FO 之间的区分能力，以及对新 FO 的可扩展性。摘要未给出具体量化指标数值。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于提出以持久目标记忆实现显式目标选择、并用聚焦式空间-外观条件连接感知与动作策略的 SAM3 引导操作框架，在不微调 SAM3 的前提下获得细粒度判别能力，并支持 memory bank 式的新物体扩展。局限在于：评估规模较小（30 个物体、4 个粗类别、3 个任务），依赖多视角配准图像与边界框标注，且未报告定量指标与推理效率。

Abstract: Fine-grained object (FO) manipulation requires robots to distinguish a specified FO from visually similar objects and execute actions reliably despite scene distractors. However, scene-level visual conditioning lacks explicit object selection, while category-level guidance cannot reliably distinguish FOs within the same category. We present a SAM3-guided visuomotor framework that addresses these challenges through persistent object memory and focused visual conditioning. First, we introduce FO Memory-driven SAM3 (FOM-SAM3), which learns reusable FO memory tokens from limited multi-view registration images while keeping SAM3 fully frozen. Through one-vs-rest learning, these tokens encode persistent memories for localizing target FOs and rejecting similar alternatives, which can be stored in a memory bank. Second, we propose Focused Spatial-Appearance Encoding (FSAE), which combines in-FO local appearance features with explicit bounding-box coordinates to condition action policies including Diffusion Policy (DP) and Action Chunking with Transformers (ACT). The effectiveness of the proposed FOM-SAM3 was validated on the FO-30 dataset comprising 30 physical objects across four coarse categories. Across three real-robot FO manipulation tasks, our FOM-SAM3-guided policies demonstrated robustness against distractors, discrimination ability among similar FOs, and extendibility to new FOs.

</details>


### [145] [SynthDemo-RL: Breaking the Zero-Reward Barrier in VLA Adaptation with LLM-Guided Synthetic Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21650)
*Hiroaki Kingetsu,Hiroaki Kurihara,Kaoru Yokoo,Kenji Fukumizu,Manohar Kaul*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SynthDemo-RL 提出一种教师-学生框架：用仿真器特权状态自动生成成功操作轨迹作为"合成示范"，通过监督微调（SFT）蒸馏出 VLA 学生策略，再用带二值任务成功奖励的 PPO 进行精炼，从而在无人类遥操作示范的情况下完成 VLA 微调，并在 LIBERO-PRO 等基准上显著提升奖励覆盖率与成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微调 Vision-Language-Action（VLA）模型通常依赖人类遥操作示范，代价高且难以规模化；而使用稀疏二值奖励的强化学习在成功轨迹极少被采样到时面临严重的探索难题（例如 pi_0.5 在 LIBERO-PRO 的 57 个评分任务中有 27 个任务成功率恰为 0%）。论文希望在完全不引入新人类示范的前提下，解决稀疏奖励下 VLA 微调的探索困境，并引入"奖励覆盖率"（reward coverage）作为平均成功率的补充指标，衡量固定评测协议下至少出现一次成功的任务比例。

Method: 提出 SynthDemo-RL 教师-学生框架，包含三个阶段：（1）自动化教师利用仿真器特权状态（privileged state）生成成功操作轨迹；（2）VLA 学生通过监督微调（SFT）从合成轨迹中蒸馏策略；（3）用带二值任务成功奖励的 PPO 进一步精炼学生策略。关键创新点在于用特权状态合成示范替代人类遥操作，并以此初始化策略，为后续稀疏奖励 RL 提供有效的探索起点。论文以奖励覆盖率为新评估维度，并与"从基线策略直接做 PPO"的对照设置进行公平对比（相同 PPO 配方与相同 RL 计算量）。

Result: 在公开基准 LIBERO-PRO（对 LIBERO 任务的扰动版本、无示范）上：基线 pi_0.5 微调策略在 57 个评分任务中 27 个为 0% 成功；直接 PPO 仅挽救其中 10 个，仍有 17 个为 0%。SynthDemo-RL 每任务仅用 50 条合成轨迹、无新人类示范，挽救全部 27 个任务，Position 轴平均成功率 97.8%、Task 轴 97.1%。在标准 LIBERO 上，同一流水线无人类示范达到 96.0%，与每任务 50 条人类示范训练的 pi_0.5 相差仅 1.7 个百分点。此外在 RoboTwin 2.0 上验证了流水线有效性，并验证 MuJoCo 孪生环境中训练的策略轨迹可在物理机器人上开环执行。

Conclusion: 主要贡献是：以仿真器特权状态合成示范替代人类遥操作，构建教师-学生 + SFT + PPO 的无人类示范 VLA 微调流水线，并提出奖励覆盖率这一补充评估指标。结果显示该方法在 LIBERO-PRO、LIBERO 与 RoboTwin 2.0 上均有效，并具备向物理机器人迁移的潜力。潜在局限包括：依赖仿真器特权状态与精确的仿真到现实对应关系，合成轨迹的质量和多样性可能制约上限；"每任务 50 条合成轨迹"等超参在更复杂/长时程任务上的可扩展性，以及奖励覆盖率指标本身对评测协议的敏感性，均有待进一步考察。

Abstract: Fine-tuning Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models commonly relies on human teleoperation demonstrations, while reinforcement learning (RL) with sparse binary rewards faces an exploration challenge when successful trajectories are rarely sampled. We propose SynthDemo-RL, a teacher-student framework in which an automated teacher converts simulator-privileged state into successful manipulation trajectories, a VLA student is distilled from them by supervised fine-tuning (SFT), and PPO with binary task-success rewards refines the student. We study reward coverage, the fraction of tasks for which at least one success is observed under the fixed evaluation protocol, as a complement to the average success rate. On LIBERO-PRO, a public benchmark of perturbed LIBERO tasks for which no demonstrations exist, 27 of 57 scored tasks are at exactly 0% success for a pi_0.5 policy fine-tuned on the original LIBERO tasks. Direct PPO from this policy, under the same PPO recipe and the same RL compute as SynthDemo-RL's refinement stage, rescues 10 of these 27 tasks and leaves 17 at 0%. SynthDemo-RL, with 50 synthesized trajectories per task and no new human demonstrations, rescues all 27 and reaches average success rates of 97.8% and 97.1% on the Position and Task axes of LIBERO-PRO, respectively. On standard LIBERO, the same pipeline reaches 96.0% with no human demonstrations, within 1.7 points of pi_0.5 trained on 50 human demonstrations per task. We further validate the pipeline on RoboTwin 2.0 and verify that trajectories from a policy trained in a MuJoCo twin execute open-loop on a physical robot.

</details>


### [146] [Outcome-Conditioned End-Effector Geometry Across Vision-Language-Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21659)
*Xingyu Lin,Zhuang Li,Zhongrun Wu,Shouquan Zhou,Dehui Du*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文研究视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略之间的"跨策略末端执行器几何一致性"问题：通过 15,000 次 LIBERO 闭环 rollout，发现"两个策略都成功"的配置对在动态时间规整（DTW）距离上比"仅一个成功"的配置对更接近，且这一排序在任务、策略对与多种动作表示下均稳健，但比例随表示方式变化数倍，因此只报告方向而非固定倍数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 策略通常以任务成功率作为唯一评价标准，但成功率无法说明不同策略是否在物理执行上真正一致——即它们是否以相似的末端执行器轨迹完成任务。理解这种"几何一致性"对策略可互换性、行为克隆数据混合、以及策略比较方法学都很重要。论文核心动机是：任务成功是否等价于执行层面的一致？跨策略的轨迹距离能否作为更细粒度的策略相似性度量？此外，该问题还涉及同一任务下不同初始状态、不同策略替换所导致的执行差异，以及视觉扰动下策略排序的稳定性。

Method: 研究基于 15,000 次 LIBERO 闭环 rollout，覆盖四个策略。主要干净条件分析构建了 3,600 个"配置匹配"（configuration-matched）的策略对，因而这些对之间存在依赖关系（同一配置被多个策略对共享）。核心度量是末端执行器轨迹的归一化动态时间规整（DTW）距离。为检验稳健性，作者在九个采样与带限（band-limited）动作表示下重复分析，并考察了 72 步动作窗口、端点与时长调整等设定。分析对比了三种结果模式：双成功、单成功、双失败（后者因支撑数据稀疏不均而被标为探索性）。此外还分析了跨任务 vs 跨初始状态的分离程度、与同任务演示（demonstrations）之间的距离关系，以及复合视觉压力（composite visual stress）下策略排名与策略对构成的变化。

Result: 在干净条件下，双成功策略对的中位归一化 DTW 距离为 0.0120 m，而恰好一方成功的策略对为 0.0380 m；该排序在每个任务、每个策略对以及九种采样/带限表示下均成立，但比值随表示变化达数倍（因此不报告固定倍数）。双失败对距离更大，但因支撑数据薄且不均，仅作探索性结论。在成功执行中，替换策略伙伴所造成的跨任务分离大于更换初始状态所造成的分离。一个匹配基线显示仍存在可测量且异质的残余策略差异，说明低跨策略距离并不等于可互换。成功执行与同任务演示之间的距离，与演示彼此之间的距离大致相当，这与"任务相关几何"相容，但无法区分训练数据重叠与任务约束。72 步动作窗口保持排序但减小幅度；端点与时长调整在相对双成功对时仍给出正的混合结果系数，但幅度依赖于具体设定。在复合视觉压力下，策略排名与策略对构成同时发生变化。

Conclusion: 贡献在于提出并系统验证了以末端执行器几何距离刻画跨策略执行一致性的分析方法，揭示了"任务成功"与"执行一致性"之间的非等价关系：成功率相近的策略在轨迹层面可能显著不同，且低距离不代表可互换。该工作强调在策略比较中需报告方向性结论并考察表示依赖性。局限包括：双失败样本支撑不足；表示选择影响效应幅度；成功执行与演示距离的解读无法区分数据重叠与任务约束；结论基于 LIBERO 仿真环境，未涉及真实机器人或事件相机/时序感知等更广泛的感知模态。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) policies solve the same manipulation task through different action interfaces, but task success alone does not establish whether their physical executions agree. We study cross-policy end-effector geometry in 15,000 closed-loop LIBERO rollouts from four policies. The primary clean-condition analysis forms 3,600 configuration-matched, and therefore dependent, policy pairs. Both-success pairs have a median normalized dynamic time warping distance of 0.0120 m versus 0.0380 m when exactly one policy succeeds. This ordering holds in every task, every policy pair, and nine sampling and band-limited representations; however, the ratio varies severalfold across representations, so we report the direction rather than a fixed multiple. Both-failure pairs are more separated again but rest on thin, uneven support, so we report them as exploratory. Within successful executions, partner replacements separate more across tasks than across initial states. A matched baseline still reveals measurable, heterogeneous residual policy differences, so a low cross-policy distance does not imply interchangeability. Successful executions sit about as far from same-task demonstrations as those demonstrations sit from each other, compatible with task-associated geometry without separating training-data overlap from task constraints. A common 72-action window preserves the ordering but reduces its magnitude; endpoint and duration adjustment likewise leaves a positive mixed-outcome coefficient relative to both-success pairs, though its magnitude is specification-dependent. Under composite visual stress, policy rankings and pair composition change together.

</details>


### [147] [RAYA: Learning Where and When to Intervene for Robot Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21690)
*Ishaan Mahajan,Charles Chen,Frederike Dümbgen,Brian Plancher*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RAYA 提出一种"学习-解析"混合控制框架：将学习得到的有限时域"可恢复性裕度(recoverability margin)"嵌入带硬约束的最优控制器，使其在动作选择阶段就考虑可恢复性；同时用一个有界的学习型调度器动态调整任务权重以腾出恢复所需的控制权限。该方法在四旋翼与自动驾驶基准上提升生存率，并零样本迁移到未见轨迹、扰动与系统变化，最终在 35g Crazyflie 上实机验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文针对一个核心矛盾：机器人即使能"预测"失败，也常常"来不及/无力"阻止失败。原因在于两处：(1) 安全机制是事后反应(veto/干预)，而此时标称规划可能已消耗掉恢复所需的控制权限；(2) 固定的任务优先级会挤占本可用于恢复的控制余量。作者的关键洞见是——可恢复性与任务优先级这两件事都应在控制器内部、在动作生成时就被决定，而非事后修正。这对高机动、欠驱动平台(如四旋翼、自动驾驶车辆)在风扰、执行器故障等极端条件下维持安全运行具有重要意义。

Method: RAYA 是一个混合 learned-analytic 框架，包含两个学习组件嵌入到有一个硬约束的最优控制回路中：
1) 有限时域可恢复性裕度：一个学习模型预测在当前状态下、固定时域内是否仍存在可行恢复策略的裕度，并作为代价/约束项直接进入最优控制器，使得"面向可恢复性的动作"在决策时即被生成，而非事后否决(instead of vetoing actions a posteriori)。
2) 有界学习型调度器(scheduler)：在可恢复性收缩时自适应地调整(shift)任务目标权重，用有界(bounded)输出保证安全性不退化，从而释放控制权限用于恢复。
控制器本身保持解析形式并带硬约束，学习组件仅提供裕度估计与权重调度，兼顾学习表达力与解析方法的安全/约束保证。此外作者给出了 RAYA 的嵌入式实现。

Result: 仿真：在四旋翼与自动驾驶车辆两个基准上，每个控制器评测 7,200 个 episode。RAYA 不仅提升生存率(survival rate)，且学习组件可零样本(zero-shot)迁移到未见轨迹、扰动、被控对象变化(plant shift)以及不同摩擦布局。硬件：作者开发了 RAYA 的嵌入式实现并部署于 35g Crazyflie 四旋翼。在风扰下、并叠加气动失配(aerodynamic mismatch)或未建模的 40% 电机指令损失(motor-command loss)的 40 次组合硬件飞行中，三个基线在全部试验中均失败，而 RAYA 完成 10/10 的六周期(six-cycle)任务。项目网站：https://raya-control.github.io/。

Conclusion: 主要贡献：提出将可恢复性从"事后安全机制"前移到控制器决策内的思想，并具体化为 RAYA 这一 learned-analytic 混合框架；通过有限时域可恢复性裕度 + 有界任务权重调度器，在仿真大规模评测中提升生存率与零样本泛化，并在真实 Crazyflie 上于风扰和执行器失效条件下显著优于三个基线。意义在于为资源受限、欠驱动平台的实时安全控制提供可行路径。潜在局限：摘要未披露可恢复性裕度学习所需的数据/仿真依赖、调度器的理论安全保证边界，以及硬件实验规模(仅 35g 级四旋翼、有限飞行次数)对更大平台的推广性；此外"7,200 episodes per controller"的具体指标与基线细节、计算开销等未在摘要中给出。

Abstract: A robot can predict failure and still be unable to prevent it. By the time a safety mechanism reacts, the nominal plan may already have spent the control authority that recovery requires, and fixed task priorities may block whatever response remains. Our key insight is that both aspects are decided inside the controller. Recoverability must inform actions while they are chosen rather than veto them afterward, and task objectives must be adapted as recoverability shrinks. Building on this, we present RAYA, a hybrid learned-analytic framework that places a learned finite-horizon recoverability margin inside an optimal controller with hard constraints and pairs it with a bounded learned scheduler that shifts task weights to facilitate recovery. Across 7,200 simulation episodes per controller spanning quadrotor and autonomous-vehicle benchmarks, RAYA not only improves survival rates, but also transfers the learned components zero-shot to unseen trajectories, disturbances, plant shifts, and friction layouts. We developed an embedded realization of RAYA and deployed it on-board a 35g Crazyflie quadrotor. Across 40 combined hardware flights under wind with either aerodynamic mismatch or an unmodeled 40% motor-command loss, each of three baselines fails in all trials, while RAYA completes 10/10 six-cycle missions. Project Website: https://raya-control.github.io/.

</details>


### [148] [NeuRIO: A Streaming Neural Estimator for Zero-Shot Sim-to-Real Multi-Robot Relative Inertial Odometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21707)
*Zhehan Li,Jiadong Lu,Shengwei Ren,Chao Xu,Yanjun Cao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: NeuRIO 是一个基于学习的流式神经估计器，仅利用机器人间已识别的方位（bearing）、距离（range）和 IMU 测量，实现无锚点的 6-DoF 相对惯性里程计，并支持零样本 sim-to-real 迁移与大规模可扩展推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人系统需要估计彼此之间的相对位姿，传统方法依赖优化（如因子图优化），在团队规模增大时计算开销急剧上升，难以满足实时性。而现有学习型方法往往难以处理变化的团队规模和时变的观测图结构，同时对真实传感器噪声与损坏缺乏鲁棒性。论文旨在构建一种既能在真实环境中零样本泛化、又能在数百机器人规模下保持低延迟的流式相对惯性里程计估计器。

Method: 1) 输入仅为机器人间已识别的方位、距离及 IMU 测量，无需外部锚点或全局定位。2) 将测量规范化到重力对齐坐标系，降低姿态与重力耦合的建模难度。3) 采用图结构建模：机器人作为节点（node），相互观测作为因子（factor），用注意力机制进行空间推理、用 GRU 进行时间建模。4) 网络全程使用共享的节点级（node-wise）和因子级（factor-wise）算子，因此可处理不同团队规模与时变观测图，无需改变架构或参数。5) 训练于一个耦合多种运动模式、设备级传感器特性以及多样、真实建模且时间持久（temporally persistent）的传感器损坏的仿真器中，从而实现零样本 sim-to-real。

Result: 在 24 条真实世界序列上，NeuRIO 达到 14.1 cm 的位置 RMSE 和 3.9° 的旋转 RMSE。在计算可扩展性方面，在多达 400 个机器人的仿真中保持每次更新低于 20 ms，而基于优化的方法在仅 24 个机器人时就已超过 20 ms 延迟。此外，尽管仅在有限团队规模上训练，NeuRIO 可直接迁移到未见过的更大团队，无需修改架构或参数。

Conclusion: NeuRIO 的主要贡献是提出了一种无锚点、图结构化的流式神经相对惯性里程计，通过共享节点/因子算子实现对团队规模和观测图变化的泛化，通过真实建模的传感器损坏仿真实现零样本 sim-to-real，并在真实序列上取得厘米级定位精度和毫秒级更新延迟。其意义在于为大规模多机器人协同感知提供了实时、可扩展、无需全局锚点且抗传感器损坏的解决方案。潜在局限在于其依赖已识别的机器人间关联（数据关联），并未在文中描述关联错误的影响；同时验证主要基于 24 条真实序列，更大规模的真实世界验证尚待补充。

Abstract: We present NeuRIO, a streaming neural estimator for anchor-free 6-DoF relative inertial odometry using only identified inter-robot bearings, ranges, and IMU measurements. NeuRIO canonicalizes measurements into gravity-aligned coordinates, represents robots as nodes and mutual observations as factors, and uses attention for spatial reasoning and GRUs for temporal modeling. As a graph network, NeuRIO applies shared node-wise and factor-wise operators throughout the network, enabling it to handle different team sizes and time-varying observation graphs. NeuRIO is trained on a simulator that couples various motion patterns, device-level sensor characteristics, and diverse, realistic modeled, and temporally persistent sensor corruptions. In this way, NeuRIO achieves zero-shot sim-to-real transfer. Across $24$ real-world sequences, NeuRIO achieves $14.1\,\mathrm{cm}$ position RMSE and $3.9^\circ$ rotation RMSE. More importantly, NeuRIO demonstrates strong computational scalability, maintaining an update cost below $20\,\mathrm{ms}$ with up to $400$ robots in simulation, while optimization-based methods exceed $20\,\mathrm{ms}$ at only $24$ robots. Moreover, even trained on limited team sizes, NeuRIO transfers directly to unseen larger teams without architectural or parameter changes.

</details>


### [149] [AgenticSwarm: Semantic Perception and Adaptive Task Allocation for Heterogeneous Multi-UAV Missions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21716)
*Muhammad Ahsan Mustafa,Yasheerah Yaqoot,Faryal Batool,Roohan Ahmed Khan,Valerii Serpiva,Dzmitry Tsetserukou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AgenticSwarm 是一个面向异构多无人机任务的智能体（agentic）框架，通过语义感知与自适应任务分配将自然语言指令、航拍影像理解与受约束的任务规划相连接，并在任务条件变化时进行残差任务重建以支持鲁棒执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂环境下的多无人机任务需要系统同时理解周围场景与人类操作者的意图，并在任务条件变化时仍保持可行的任务分配。核心问题在于：语义层面的场景/指令理解与底层的受约束任务分配之间存在割裂，导致任务在执行过程中遇到无人机故障、电量衰减或任务变更时难以自适应调整，容易产生重复作业或恢复缓慢。因此需要一个能够桥接语义推理与物理约束规划的智能体框架。

Method: 提出 AgenticSwarm 智能体框架，包含两个核心环节：(1) 语义感知——由 agent 解释航拍影像与自然语言指令，构建 grounded mission representation，将感知到的目标与区域同任务需求、能力约束、任务依赖进行绑定；(2) 受约束任务分配——在分配前纳入障碍物感知的路径可行性、能耗以及受保护返航（return-home）需求等约束。执行过程中，当出现无人机故障、电池衰减或任务修改等变化时，触发基于当前系统状态的残差任务重建（residual mission reconstruction），同时保留已完成工作与侦察进度。感知管线采用 SAM3 替代 Grounding DINO+SAM 2.1 作为基线对比。

Result: 在五个不同的 Gazebo 环境以及一个室内真实测试环境中进行评估。与 Grounding DINO+SAM 2.1 感知基线相比，基于 SAM3 的管线将类别感知召回率提升 25.2 个百分点（pp），语义标签准确率提升 29.5 pp。消融残差任务重规划后，平均重复工作量从 0% 上升到 61.7%，事件后恢复时间增加 58.6%，验证了自适应重规划对任务执行的重要贡献。

Conclusion: AgenticSwarm 的主要贡献在于将语义推理与受约束的多无人机任务分配及自适应执行相连接，并通过残差任务重建机制提升任务变化下的鲁棒性与效率，同时在仿真与真实室内环境中进行了验证。潜在局限包括：真实环境评测仅限单一室内场景，未涵盖复杂室外多无人机场景；框架的感知与规划性能高度依赖基础视觉模型（如 SAM3）的质量，计算开销与实时性在资源受限平台上的表现尚不明确。

Abstract: Multi UAV missions in complex environments require the system to understand both the surrounding scene and the intent of a human operator while maintaining feasible task allocation as mission conditions change. This paper presents AgenticSwarm, an agentic framework for semantic perception and adaptive task allocation in heterogeneous multi UAV missions. An agent interprets aerial imagery and natural language instructions to construct a grounded mission representation that links perceived objects and regions with task requirements, capability constraints, and mission dependencies. This information augments a constrained task allocation process in which obstacle aware path feasibility, energy consumption, and protected return home requirements are incorporated before assignment. During execution, changes such as UAV failure, battery degradation, or task modification trigger residual mission reconstruction from the current system state, while completed work and reconnaissance progress are retained. AgenticSwarm is evaluated across five diverse Gazebo environments and an indoor real test environment, demonstrating its ability to connect semantic reasoning with constrained allocation and adaptive multi UAV mission execution. Compared with a Grounding DINO+SAM~2.1 perception baseline, the SAM3-based pipeline improves class-aware recall by 25.2 percentage points (pp) and semantic label accuracy by 29.5 pp. Ablating residual mission replanning increases mean repeated work from 0% to 61.7% and post-event recovery time by 58.6%, highlighting the contribution of adaptive replanning to mission execution.

</details>


### [150] [A Novel Path-Tracking Algorithm for Automated Tractor-Trailer Forward and Backward Maneuvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21718)
*Alexandre Lombard,Florent Perronnet,Nicolas Gaud,Abdeljalil Abbas-Turki*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种针对单挂车铰接式车辆（牵引车-挂车）的几何路径跟踪算法，通过在挂车层面施加横向控制律并结合牵引车转向角的短时域预测调整，实现前进与倒车两种工况下稳定收敛至目标路径，且无需逐车标定或训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全自主牵引车-挂车系统在物流、农业和工业场景中日益普及，对精确鲁棒的路径跟踪能力要求很高。但牵引车与挂车之间的铰接结构引入了额外非线性，使横向与纵向控制显著复杂化，尤其在倒车机动时经典控制方法难以保证稳定性与精度。该问题对于自动化作业的安全性和可靠性至关重要，因此需要一种能统一处理前进/倒车、且不依赖大量标定或学习的控制方案。

Method: 方法为几何驱动（geometry-based）的控制策略，核心由两部分组成：一是施加在挂车层面的横向控制律，直接以挂车相对目标路径的偏差为目标进行调节；二是对牵引车转向角进行短时域（short-horizon）预测性调整，以补偿铰接带来的非线性耦合，从而在前进和倒车运动中均保证向目标路径的稳定收敛。该算法不使用数据驱动的训练，也不需要针对具体车辆的参数标定，强调通用性与可迁移性。

Result: 实验在高保真物理仿真器（high-fidelity physics simulator）中开展，针对单挂车铰接式车辆进行路径跟踪评估。评价维度包括跟踪精度、稳定性以及对扰动的鲁棒性。结果显示所提控制器在精度、稳定性和抗扰能力上能够匹配或超越经典方法及当前最先进（state-of-the-art）方法。摘要未给出具体数据集、量化指标数值或对比方法的详细清单。

Conclusion: 主要贡献是提出一种面向单挂车铰接车辆的免标定、免训练几何路径跟踪算法，通过挂车级横向控制与牵引车转向角短时域预测调整的结合，统一支持前进与倒车运动，并在仿真中展现出优于或媲美经典与先进方法的精度、稳定性与鲁棒性。局限在于验证仅停留在仿真层面，尚未提供实车实验、具体数值指标以及对多挂车或复杂动态环境的适用性讨论。

Abstract: Fully autonomous tractor--trailer systems are increasingly deployed in logistics, agriculture, and industrial environments, where precise and robust path-tracking capabilities are essential. However, the articulation between the tractor and the trailer introduces additional nonlinearities and significantly complicates lateral and longitudinal control, particularly during reversing maneuvers. This paper introduces a novel path-tracking algorithm specifically designed for articulated vehicles with a single trailer. The proposed method combines a lateral control law applied at the trailer level with a short-horizon predictive adjustment of the tractor steering angle, ensuring stable convergence toward the desired path in both forward and backward motion. The approach is geometry-based and requires no per-vehicle calibration or training. Simulation studies in a high-fidelity physics simulator demonstrate the ability of the controller to match or outperform classical and state-of-the-art methods in terms of accuracy, stability, and robustness to disturbances.

</details>


### [151] [ZeroTouch: Tactile-Supervised Visual Contact Estimation for Contact-Rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21726)
*Dmitriy Kosenkov,Daniia Zinniatullina,Miguel Altamirano Cabrera,Iana Zhura,Mikhail Derevianchenko,Dzmitry Tsetserukou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ZeroTouch 提出一种"触觉监督"的机器人抓取框架：训练时利用触觉传感器作为特权监督信号，部署时仅凭腕部 RGB 图像、夹爪状态和局部重力方向，即可预测密集接触形变、瞬时六轴力旋量（wrench）以及抓取相关的目标压缩量，实现无触觉硬件的可靠抓取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可靠抓取依赖对物理交互演化的估计以及抓取相关的压缩目标选择。触觉传感器能直接测量交互力，但需要在部署端配备专用硬件，增加成本与集成难度。论文希望在不牺牲交互感知能力的前提下，摆脱对触觉硬件的部署依赖，从而让具备力感知的抓取能力更易于在实际机器人平台上推广。

Method: ZeroTouch 是一个触觉监督框架：输入为腕部 RGB 观测、夹爪状态和局部重力方向，输出包括（1）密集接触形变预测、（2）瞬时六轴力旋量估计、（3）抓取相关的期望压缩目标。关键在于触觉测量仅作为训练阶段的特权监督（privileged supervision），用于监督这些物理量的学习，而部署阶段完全不需要触觉传感器。框架显式建模了"抓取依赖"的压缩目标，而非固定阈值，并与夹爪状态、重力方向结合以补偿姿态相关影响。

Result: 在完整验证集上，完整架构将法向力 MAE 从仅状态基线（state-only baseline）的 2.017 N 降至 0.531 N。物理评估中，每种条件进行 20 次试验：ZeroTouch 在未见物体上达到 95% 成功率，已见物体/未见抓取条件下 80%，在内容/负载偏移下 90%。相同评估协议下，OpenVLA 分别为 25%、40%、55%，SmolVLA 分别为 10%、25%、35%。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于提出一种部署时无需触觉硬件的触觉监督抓取框架，可同时预测接触形变、六轴力旋量和抓取相关压缩目标，并显著优于基线及 VLA 类方法（OpenVLA、SmolVLA）的抓取成功率。其意义在于降低硬件依赖的同时保留力感知能力，并展示了对未见物体和分布偏移的一定泛化性。潜在局限：监督信号依赖训练阶段的触觉数据，需为训练采集触觉标注；物理评估规模较小（每条件 20 次试验）；对腕部 RGB 可见性与遮挡条件可能敏感。

Abstract: Reliable robotic grasping benefits from estimating the evolving physical interaction and selecting a grasp-dependent compression target. Tactile sensors provide direct interaction measurements but require dedicated hardware at deployment. We introduce ZeroTouch, a tactile-supervised framework that predicts dense contact deformation, the instantaneous six-axis wrench, and a grasp-dependent desired compression target from wrist RGB observations, gripper state, and local gravity direction. Tactile measurements are used only as privileged supervision during training and are not required at deployment. On the full validation set, the complete architecture reduces normal-force MAE from 2.017 N for a state-only baseline to 0.531 N. In physical evaluation with 20 trials per condition, ZeroTouch achieves 95% success on an unseen object, 80% in a seen-object/unseen-grasp condition, and 90% under a content/load shift. Under the same evaluation protocol, OpenVLA achieves 25%, 40%, and 55%, while SmolVLA achieves 10%, 25%, and 35%, respectively.

</details>


### [152] [Visual Proactivity: Enhancing Human-Robot Collaboration Through Intent Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21729)
*Valerio Bo,Edison Bejarano,Anaís Garrell,Alberto Sanfeliu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出"视觉主动性"（visual proactivity）概念，一种让机器人通过视觉反馈主动传达自身意图的简易有效方法，并在人-机器人交接（handover）场景中通过用户研究验证了被动、预期性和主动性三种机器人行为的可感知性与交互效果，表明视觉主动性可提升人机协作的对齐性、透明度和流畅度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着机器人从执行重复任务转向与人类协作，理解与传达意图对人机交互至关重要。既有研究多聚焦于机器人如何推断并响应人类意图（如预期性anticipation），但较少关注机器人如何主动向人类传达自身意图。这种沟通的不对称会削弱协作的透明度与流畅性，尤其在需要精确时序协调的任务（如物体交接）中，人类若无法预判机器人行为易导致误解与低效。因此，作者提出让机器人通过视觉方式主动"表达"意图，以引导人类行为并提升协作质量。

Method: 论文提出"视觉主动性"（visual proactivity）方法，即机器人借助视觉反馈通道主动表达其意图，从而影响并引导人类行为。作者围绕人-机器人交接场景设计并实现了三类机器人行为：被动（reactive）、预期性（anticipatory）与主动性（proactive）。前两者分别对应事后响应与预判人类动作，主动性则指机器人主动采取行动并通过视觉表现引导人类朝向最优结果。研究方法以行为设计与用户研究为核心，而非提出具体网络架构或算法模型；通过用户研究评估人类对这三种行为的感知差异及其对协作协调性的影响。

Result: 实验在一项人-机器人交接场景的用户研究中开展，评估指标聚焦于人类对反应性、预期性和主动性三种机器人行为的感知与交互体验。结果显示，有效的视觉主动性能够促进人机之间更好的对齐与协调，验证了通过视觉反馈传达机器人意图的可行性与有效性，并表明主动性行为在提升交互透明度与流畅度方面优于纯反应式或仅预期式行为。论文强调用户研究验证了这三类行为的可区分性与感知效果。

Conclusion: 本文的核心贡献在于提出并验证了"视觉主动性"这一新颖而简洁的概念，将机器人意图传达从隐性推断扩展到显性视觉反馈，填补了机器人主动沟通自身意图的研究空白，并通过用户研究证明其能改善人机协作的对齐、透明度和流畅性。意义在于为更直观的人机协作提供了设计思路。可能的局限包括：研究聚焦于单一交接场景，结论的泛化性有待验证；方法依赖用户主观感知评估，缺乏更客观的量化和长期交互验证；未涉及具体视觉算法或计算实现细节。

Abstract: As robots transition from performing repetitive tasks to collaborating with humans, understanding human intent becomes crucial to effective interaction. Anticipation enables robots to predict human actions, while proactivity allows them to take initiative and guide human behavior toward optimal outcomes. Although research has largely focused on how robots infer and respond to human intentions, less attention has been paid to how robots communicate their own intent. This paper introduces visual proactivity, a novel, simple yet effective approach that enables robots to communicate their intentions through visual feedback, influencing human behavior and enhancing transparency and fluency. We develop and evaluate proactive robotic behaviors in a human-to-robot handover scenario, where a user study validates human perception of reactive, anticipatory, and proactive behaviors. The results demonstrate that effective visual proactivity fosters better alignment and coordination, paving the way for more intuitive human-robot collaboration.

</details>


### [153] [When Should Robots Intervene? Balancing Engagement and Intrusiveness in Human-Robot Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21734)
*Lavinia Hriscu,Valerio Bo,Alberto Sanfeliu,Anaís Garrell*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文研究任务导向场景下人机交互（HRI）中机器人干预策略对用户体验的影响，对比"持续寻求互动"策略与"基于用户状态和任务情境的选择性干预"策略。通过32名参与者的模拟医院环境用户研究，发现更高的交互频率并不必然带来更好的参与度，而是存在"感知支持度"与"侵入感"之间的系统性权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在任务导向的人机交互中，机器人需要在维持用户参与度的同时保持社会可接受的侵入水平。如何在"过度干预引发侵入感"与"干预不足导致支持缺失"之间取得平衡，是设计有效HRI策略的核心问题。现有研究缺乏对干预策略如何具体影响用户体验、交互动态、感知侵入感与支持感的系统性实证证据，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 论文提出并对比两种机器人干预策略：（1）持续寻求互动策略（continuous engagement-seeking），机器人持续尝试发起并维持与用户的互动；（2）情境感知策略（context-aware），机器人根据用户状态与任务情境选择性地进行干预。两种策略均依赖多模态行为线索，包括身体朝向（body orientation）与注意力信号（attentional signals），以驱动机器人的行为决策。研究在模拟医院环境中开展用户研究，招募32名参与者执行任务，通过受控实验比较两种策略对交互动态和用户主观体验的影响。

Result: 实验为包含32名参与者的模拟医院环境用户研究，参与者执行任务过程中接受两种不同机器人干预策略。研究评估了用户体验、交互动态、感知侵入感与支持感等指标。主要发现：更高的交互频率并不必然带来更好的用户参与度；存在"感知支持度"与"侵入感"之间的系统性权衡关系；该权衡受物理接近度（physical proximity）与用户投入程度（user effort）等因素调节。

Conclusion: 论文的主要贡献在于通过实证用户研究揭示了HRI中交互频率与参与度之间的非线性关系，以及支持感与侵入感之间的系统性权衡，为"有效的用户参与依赖于自适应、情境敏感的干预策略"提供了经验证据。研究意义在于指导更社会可接受、更具情境感知能力的机器人交互设计。局限方面，实验基于模拟医院环境与32名参与者，样本规模与场景单一性可能限制结论的泛化性；摘要未提供量化指标细节与具体的统计结果。

Abstract: Designing effective Human-Robot Interaction in task-oriented settings requires carefully balancing user engagement with socially acceptable levels of robot intrusiveness. In this paper, we examine how different robot intervention strategies shape user experience, interaction dynamics, perceived intrusiveness, and sense of support. We compare two approaches: a continuous engagement-seeking robot strategy, and a context-aware strategy that selectively intervenes based on the user's state and task context. Both approaches rely on multimodal behavioral cues, including body orientation and attentional signals, to guide robot actions. We evaluate these strategies in a user study with 32 participants performing a task in a simulated hospital environment. Our findings show that higher interaction frequency does not necessarily lead to better engagement. Instead, we observe a systematic trade-off between perceived support and intrusiveness, influenced by factors such as physical proximity and user effort. These results provide empirical evidence that effective engagement in HRI depends on adaptive, context-sensitive intervention policies.

</details>


### [154] [Sandwich-Residuals: Parameter-Efficient Test-time Adaptation of World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21740)
*Krishnam Soni,Aditya Sehgal,Vedant Dave,Elmar Rueckert*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 Sandwich-Residuals，一种轻量的测试时自适应方法：保持预训练世界模型完全冻结，仅在线学习围绕预测器的小型残差修正项，无需奖励、标签或源域数据，在 AdaJEPA 基准上以极少参数实现显著成功率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜空间世界模型（latent world models）通过在学到的表征空间预测动作效果来支持规划，但当测试时分布与训练条件不一致时，其预测会变得不可靠。现有测试时自适应方法通过更新预训练模型的部分参数来应对，但存在两个问题：(1) 通常需要修改数百万级参数；(2) 需要人为选择适配哪些内部组件（如哪一层的 block）。作者希望探索是否能在不修改世界模型预训练内部权重的前提下，实现有效的测试时自适应。

Method: Sandwich-Residuals 的核心设计是"冻结主干 + 学习残差"：预训练世界模型（如 JEPA 类模型）的权重保持不变，仅在预测器（predictor）周围引入小型残差修正模块，形成类似"夹心"结构。该残差以模型自身的自监督预测误差（self-supervised prediction error）作为在线优化目标，因此完全无需奖励信号、任务标签或源域数据。由于仅优化小规模残差参数，适配参数数量比现有方法少 97–99%。该原则具有通用性，作者进一步将其应用于用于三维操作的 DINO-WM 模型。

Result: 作者在 AdaJEPA 基准的 21 种测试条件下进行评估，指标为任务成功率。结果显示：Sandwich-Residuals 达到冻结模型成功率的 1.3×，同时保留最强 AdaJEPA 变体性能的 95%，而适配参数减少 97–99%。在复合分布偏移（compound shifts）下，其优势更明显，达到冻结模型成功率的 1.9×，且与内部 block 自适应方法的性能相当。此外，该适配原则在 DINO-WM 三维操作模型上也得到验证，说明方法具有一定的跨模型、跨任务泛化能力。

Conclusion: 本文的主要贡献在于证明：世界模型的有效测试时自适应并不必然需要修改其预训练内部权重。通过冻结主干、仅在线学习浅层残差修正，方法在参数效率与自适应性之间取得了良好平衡，并在多个基准与模型上得到验证。其意义在于提供了一种低开销、无需监督信号的测试时适配范式。潜在局限包括：方法依赖自监督预测误差作为适配信号，当该误差与下游任务性能相关性较弱时效果可能受限；实验主要集中于 JEPA 类与 DINO-WM 世界模型，向其他架构或真实机器人系统的推广仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: Latent world models enable planning by predicting the effects of actions in a learned representation space, but their predictions can become unreliable when test-time conditions differ from training. Existing test-time adaptation methods address this by updating parts of the pretrained model, often modifying millions of parameters and requiring a choice of which internal components to adapt. We introduce Sandwich-Residuals, a lightweight alternative that keeps the pretrained world model frozen and learns only small residual corrections around the predictor. The residuals are optimized online using the model's self-supervised prediction error and require no rewards, labels, or source-domain data. Across 21 conditions on the AdaJEPA benchmark, our method achieves $1.3\times$ the success rate of the frozen model while retaining 95% of the performance of the strongest AdaJEPA variant and adapting 97-99% fewer parameters. Under compound shifts, this advantage increases to $1.9\times$ the success rate of the frozen model, while remaining comparable to internal block adaptation. We further demonstrate the same adaptation principle on a DINO-WM model for 3-D manipulation. These results suggest that effective test-time adaptation of world models does not necessarily require modifying their pretrained internal weights.

</details>


### [155] [Understanding Engagement and Intrusiveness in Assistive Human-Robot Interaction Using Individual Traits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21744)
*Valerio Bo,Lavinia Hriscu,Alberto Sanfeliu,Anaís Garrell*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文通过32名被试的人机交互实验，论证机器人"侵入感"不仅取决于邻近学（proxemics），还取决于机器人干预频率及用户个性/人口学特征，主张自适应、个性化的机器人行为策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在陌生任务中，用户需同时理解任务要求并协调机器人，机器人协助因而尤为关键。既有研究对机器人邻近距离（proxemics）与用户参与度（engagement）关系结论不一：有研究认为更近的距离能增强交互，另一些则报告距离过近会产生侵入感。作者认为这种矛盾源于忽略了其他因素——尤其是机器人干预（intervention）频率以及个体特质（人格、人口学差异），因此有必要系统考察这些变量对交互体验的共同影响。

Method: 采用被试间/条件对比的用户实验设计。招募32名参与者，与两台辅助机器人交互：两台机器人在任务支持内容上相似，但干预策略（如主动介入的频率/时机）不同。测量维度包括整体参与度（engagement）、情感反应（affective responses）与感知侵入性（perceived intrusiveness），并采集参与者人格特质与人口学信息，分析这些个体差异如何调节不同机器人行为条件下的交互动态。

Result: 实验结果表明：在不同实验条件之间，整体参与度保持相对稳定；但情感反应与感知侵入性随人格特质出现显著差异。此外，人格对交互动态的影响方式取决于机器人行为策略本身，即人格与机器人干预方式之间存在交互效应。核心发现是机器人行为对用户体验的影响并非普遍一致，而是被人格特征所调节。

Conclusion: 论文主要贡献在于提出并验证了一个更完整的解释框架：机器人侵入感由邻近距离、干预频率和个体特质共同决定，并将"人格—行为策略"的交互效应纳入人机交互分析。其意义在于强调有效HRI应兼顾个体差异，通过定制机器人行为在维持参与度的同时尊重用户独特的情感与行为特征。可能的局限包括样本量较小（32人）且为特定任务/机器人设置，结论的泛化性与因果机制需进一步验证；论文聚焦用户感知层面，未涉及视觉/感知算法或硬件实现。

Abstract: Robot assistance is particularly crucial in unfamiliar tasks, where users must understand task requirements while coordinating with the robot. Previous research offers mixed evidence on the role of robot proxemics in user engagement: some studies suggest closer proximity enhances interaction, while others report it can feel intrusive. In this work, we argue that perceptions of intrusiveness depend not only on proxemics but also on the frequency of robot interventions, and are strongly influenced by individual traits such as personality and demographics. We conducted an experiment with 32 participants who interacted with two assistive robots that provided similar task support but differed in their intervention strategies. Results indicate that overall engagement remains stable across conditions, yet affective responses and perceived intrusiveness vary significantly with personality traits. Moreover, personality shapes interaction dynamics differently depending on the robot's behavior. These findings emphasize that effective human-robot interaction should account for individual differences, tailoring robot behavior to maintain engagement while respecting each user's unique affective and behavioral profile.

</details>


### [156] [ForceTwin: Physics-informed Digital Twins for Robotic Manipulation from Instrumented Human Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21751)
*Tim Engelbracht,René Zurbrügg,Mayank Mittal,Marco Hutter,Marc Pollefeys,Hermann Blum,Zuria Bauer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ForceTwin 通过手持力感知夹爪采集人类与铰接物体交互时的同步位姿与接触力，为铰接物体辨识"物理信息数字孪生"，显式估计惯性、库仑摩擦、粘性阻尼等参数化动力学，并用结构化神经残差建模随状态变化的机构力（如弹簧、闭门器），随后将辨识出的孪生用于阻抗控制与全身开门策略的真实部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 操纵铰接物体（门、抽屉等）不仅依赖运动学，还依赖决定其行为的物理属性，如惯性、摩擦以及弹簧/闭门器等随构型和速度变化的机构效应。这些属性无法从外观直接观测：外观完全相同的门可能所需操纵力差异巨大。现有数字孪生流程主要恢复运动学，或依赖视觉与语言先验赋予静态物理参数，容易产生物理上不合理的估计，导致状态相关的机构动力学未被辨识，也无法在标准资产格式中表达。该问题对机器人真实世界交互与操纵至关重要。

Method: ForceTwin 是一个从"带仪器的人类交互"中辨识物理信息数字孪生的系统。人类操作者使用手持力感知夹爪对目标物体进行探测，系统获得同步的位姿与交互力数据。在此基础上估计：铰接运动学（articulation）、参数化动力学（含惯性、库仑摩擦、粘性阻尼），以及一个结构化神经残差项，用于捕捉状态相关的机构力（如弹簧、门闭器带来的力）。这些辨识得到的孪生既作为前馈动力学模型用于阻抗控制，也被用于训练全身开门（door-traversal）策略。

Result: 在惯性参数估计上，ForceTwin 相比 VLM 先验几乎将误差减半。在 Spot 与 Franka FR3 上的阻抗控制任务中，作为前馈动力学模型，ForceTwin 在 9 组"物体-本体"配对上达到 87% 的目标完成率，而 VLM 先验为 60%、仅运动学孪生为 57%；增益在机构作用较强、导致两种基线停滞的物体上最为显著。此外，辨识出的孪生被用于训练全身开门穿越策略，并在真实世界完成部署。

Conclusion: 该工作提出以力感知人类交互为监督来源，辨识包含参数化动力学与状态相关机构残差的物理信息数字孪生，弥补了现有数字孪生仅恢复运动学或采用静态物理先验的不足，并在真实机器人平台（Spot、Franka FR3）上验证了其对阻抗控制与全身开门策略的价值。其意义在于强调了对外观不可观测物理属性的显式建模。潜在局限包括依赖带仪器的人类探测过程、参数化模型（惯性、库仑摩擦、粘性阻尼加神经残差）对更复杂机构泛化能力未充分说明，以及实验规模（9 组本体-物体配对、单类铰接物体）相对有限。

Abstract: Manipulating objects requires understanding not only their motion, but also the physical properties that determine it. For articulated objects, these include inertia, friction, and mechanisms such as springs or door closers, whose effects can vary with configuration and velocity. Such properties are not directly observable from appearance: visually identical doors may require very different effort to manipulate. Existing digital-twin pipelines recover primarily kinematics or assign static physical parameters from visual and language priors, which can yield physically implausible estimates. As a result, state-dependent mechanism dynamics remain unidentified and are not represented in standard asset formats. We present ForceTwin, a system for identifying physics-informed digital twins of articulated objects from instrumented human interaction. A person probes an object using a handheld force-sensing gripper, providing synchronized poses and interaction forces from which we estimate the articulation, parametric dynamics including inertia, Coulomb friction, viscous damping, and a structured neural residual capturing state-dependent mechanism forces. ForceTwin nearly halves the inertial-parameter error of a VLM prior. As a feedforward dynamics model for impedance control on a Spot and a Franka FR3, ForceTwin achieves 87% goal completion across nine object-embodiment pairs, compared with 60% using VLM-prior and 57% using kinematics-only twins, with the largest gains on objects whose strong mechanisms cause both baselines to stall. We further use the identified twins to train whole-body door-traversal policies and deploy them in the real world. Project Page: https://timengelbracht.github.io/forcetwin-website/

</details>


### [157] [PSR: Predictive Sensorimotor Representation Learning for Contact-Rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21753)
*Shengbao Li,Peng Xu,Chao Tang,Hao Wei,Jiaheng Wang,Hong Yin,Jiangtao Chen,Jinxuan Zhu,Zhong Zhou,Mengfan Wang,Tingguang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出 Predictive Sensorimotor Representation (PSR) 学习框架，通过预测未来接触动力学来学习多模态感知运动信号的层级化预测表征，并将其注入视觉运动策略的动作流中，实例化为 PSR-VLA，用于接触密集的精细操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触密集型操作要求策略不仅依赖视觉观测，还需对接触力、机器人构型与交互历史进行推理，从而生成高精度动作。现有方法通常只是被动地将力反馈作为条件输入，而非主动预测未来的接触动力学，因而难以生成高精度动作。论文的动机是让策略具备"预测式"的感知运动理解能力，从而更好地利用与接触相关的线索。

Method: PSR 框架在学习流程上分为两个阶段。预训练阶段，使用一个多模态 Transformer 对多模态感知运动信号进行联合建模，通过同时预测未来的交互动力学，学习一组层级化的预测表征（hierarchy of predictive representations）。这些表征随后被用于增强动作流（action stream），使下游策略能够在多个深度层级上利用接触相关线索。论文进一步将 PSR 实例化到 Vision-Language-Action (VLA) 模型中，形成 PSR-VLA，使 VLA 策略具备力感知的接触预测能力。核心创新点在于：(1) 主动预测未来接触动力学而非被动条件化；(2) 层级化预测表征的多深度注入机制。

Result: 论文在六个真实世界接触密集型操作任务上进行评估。PSR-VLA 取得 91.7% 的总体成功率，分别比 π_{0.5}、ForceVLA-π_{0.5}、ForceVLA2-π_{0.5} 高出 30.0、22.5 和 19.2 个百分点。任务视频与稳定性测试见项目页面。结果表明 PSR 对于力感知、接触密集操作具有有效性。

Conclusion: 论文的主要贡献在于提出 PSR 学习框架，通过预测未来接触动力学学习层级化预测感知运动表征，并将其集成到 VLA 动作流中，显著提升了真实世界接触密集型操作的成功率。其意义在于突破了传统被动利用力反馈的范式，转向主动预测式多模态感知运动建模。潜在局限包括：仅在六个真实任务上评估，缺乏大规模基准与消融细节；依赖额外多模态传感信号（如力/触觉），部署成本较高；层级化表征的泛化性与跨任务迁移能力有待进一步验证。

Abstract: Contact-rich manipulation requires policies to generate precise actions by reasoning over contact forces, robot configurations, and interaction histories beyond visual observations. Existing methods passively condition on force feedback rather than actively predicting future contact dynamics, limiting their ability to generate high-precision actions. To address this problem, we introduce Predictive Sensorimotor Representation (PSR) learning, a framework that learns a hierarchy of predictive representations from multimodal sensorimotor signals and integrates them into the action stream of a visuomotor policy. Specifically, during a pretraining stage, a multimodal Transformer is trained to learn a hierarchy of predictive representations by jointly forecasting future interaction dynamics. The learned hierarchy subsequently augments the action stream, enabling the resulting policy to exploit contact-relevant cues at multiple depths. We further instantiate PSR within a Vision-Language-Action (VLA) model, resulting in PSR-VLA, and evaluate it on six real-world contact-rich manipulation tasks. Experimental results show that PSR-VLA achieves 91.7% overall success, improving over $π_{0.5}$, ForceVLA-$π_{0.5}$, and ForceVLA2-$π_{0.5}$ by 30.0, 22.5, and 19.2 percentage points, respectively. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed PSR for force-aware, contact-rich manipulation. Videos of the tasks and stability tests are available at https://psr-vla.pages.dev/.

</details>


### [158] [CRISP: Contact-Rich Robotic Simulation Platform with Extensive Geometries and Contact Solvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21761)
*Somang Lee,Sunkyung Park,Jinhee Yun,Seoki An,Dongjun Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CRISP 是一个面向紧公差机器人操作等高复杂度多接触场景的高保真物理仿真平台，通过融合多几何表示、基于优化的碰撞检测与增广拉格朗日接触求解器，实现对接触密集与尖锐相互作用的高效稳定仿真。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 紧公差机器人操作等任务对物理仿真的保真度要求极高，既要精确刻画几何与接触交互，又需通过鲁棒的碰撞检测与接触求解实现准确的数值解算。然而现有仿真器往往存在两类不足：一是几何表示支持有限、接触建模过于简化；二是数值解算方法在精度或鲁棒性上存在固有限制。这使得接触密集、尖锐交互场景难以被真实复现，制约了机器人在此类任务中的仿真验证与策略学习。

Method: CRISP 的核心在于两个层面的整合：（1）几何与碰撞检测层面，支持多种几何表示，并采用基于优化的碰撞检测方法，从而在复杂几何上高效、一致地获取接触信息；（2）接触建模与求解层面，将接触建模与基于增广拉格朗日的接触求解器相结合，在无需引入有问题的松弛（relaxations）的前提下准确求解多接触约束。整体架构强调表达性几何建模与稳健数值解算的协同，以支撑接触密集及尖锐相互作用的仿真。

Result: 论文将 CRISP 与当前最先进的仿真平台进行对比，以验证其物理保真度，并通过复杂的机器人操作场景展示其能力。文中并未给出具体的基准数据集、评价指标数值或定量结果细节，主要结论是 CRISP 在多接触约束解算与复杂几何接触检测上表现出一致性与准确性，且可支撑接触密集型操作任务的仿真。代码已开源（https://github.com/INRoL/crisp）。

Conclusion: CRISP 的主要贡献是提出一个支持多几何表示、优化式碰撞检测与增广拉格朗日接触求解的高保真多接触仿真平台，为紧公差机器人操作提供更真实的仿真条件，并已开源以促进复现与后续研究。潜在局限在于：摘要未提供定量评估指标与系统性基准对比结果，大规模场景下的仿真效率与实时性、以及相对其他先进求解器的可扩展性仍有待进一步验证。

Abstract: We present CRISP (Contact-RIch Simulation Platform), a high-fidelity physics engine tailored for complex multi-contact simulations such as tight-tolerance robotic manipulation. Achieving high physical fidelity in robotic simulation requires both expressive modeling of geometry and contact interactions, as well as accurate numerical resolution via robust collision detection and contact solvers. However, existing simulators often either rely on limited support for geometric representations and simplified modeling of contact interactions, or employ numerical resolution methods whose accuracy or robustness is inherently constrained. Accordingly, we develop a new simulator that supports diverse geometric representations with accurate optimization-based collision detection, and combines contact modeling with robust augmented Lagrangian-based contact solvers. This integration enables efficient and consistent detection of contact information across complex geometries while accurately resolving multi-contact constraints without problematic relaxations, which is essential for simulating contact-intensive and sharp interactions. We validate the physical fidelity of our simulator against state-of-the-art platforms and further demonstrate its capabilities through complex robotic manipulation scenarios. CRISP is publicly available at https://github.com/INRoL/crisp.

</details>


### [159] [Scaling Vision-Language Reward Learning for Robot Manipulation in Parallel Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21767)
*Lobna Joualy,Eric Demeester,Nikolaos Tsiogkas*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RAPID 是一套用于基于视觉语言模型（VLM）的偏好奖励学习系统，通过 GPU 并行 rollout、自适应策略更新、单次请求偏好标注、自动奖励稳定与代表性图像采样，在 Franka Panda 操作任务上将训练时间缩短 8.0 倍、API 调用减少 95.5%，并将最终成功率从 86.3% 提升至 98.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 使用 VLM 替代人类标注者进行基于偏好的奖励学习虽可降低人工成本，但现有的串行 API 请求方式与单一环境下的数据采集导致训练既慢又昂贵，成为强化学习奖励学习规模化应用的瓶颈。该问题在需要大量交互样本的机器人操作任务中尤为突出，因此亟需一个兼顾训练效率、API 成本与最终策略性能的系统级解决方案。

Method: RAPID（Reward learning with Adaptive Parallel Image Diversity）将多项技术耦合为一个系统：(1) GPU 并行 rollout，实现跨环境并行采样以提高数据吞吐；(2) 数据感知的自适应策略更新，根据采集数据动态调整更新节奏；(3) 单次请求偏好标注，将多阶段提示压缩为一次 API 调用以减少请求次数；(4) 自动奖励稳定机制，缓解 VLM 奖励信号噪声带来的训练不稳定；(5) 代表性图像采样（Image Diversity），在送入 VLM 前挑选具有代表性的观测图像，降低冗余请求。各组件可独立启用并分别评估其贡献。

Result: 在 IsaacLab 的五个 Franka Panda 操作任务上进行评测。在匹配的两阶段提示设置下，并行 rollout 与自适应更新使平均运行时间从 9.18 小时降至 3.13 小时。启用全部 RAPID 组件后，训练在 1.15 小时内完成，每次运行仅需 896 次 API 调用（原始为 19,840 次），聚合最终成功率从 86.3% 提升到 98.7%，实现 8.0 倍端到端加速和 95.5% 的 API 使用量削减。此外，使用 Gemma 3 12B 与 GPT-4.1 mini 进行的离线评估表明，单请求提示在两个模型上均降低了标注延迟与成本。

Conclusion: RAPID 的主要贡献在于将 VLM 奖励学习中的效率与成本问题系统性拆解为并行采样、自适应更新、单请求标注、奖励稳定与图像选择五个组件，并在机器人操作基准上验证了显著的时间与 API 节省以及性能提升，同时开源了代码。其局限在于评估集中在 IsaacLab 仿真环境下的五个 Franka Panda 任务，未涉及真实机器人部署；单请求标注与代表性采样可能牺牲部分偏好标注的细粒度，其在大规模、多样化任务上的泛化性及对奖励信号质量的长期影响仍有待进一步验证。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) can replace human annotators in preference-based reward learning, but sequential API requests and single-environment data collection make training slow and costly. We present RAPID (Reward learning with Adaptive Parallel Image Diversity), a system that couples GPU-parallel rollout with data-aware policy updates, single-request preference labeling, automatic reward stabilization, and representative image sampling. We evaluate these components on five Franka Panda manipulation tasks in IsaacLab. Parallel rollout and adaptive updates provide the first substantial reduction in training time: under matched two-stage prompting, mean runtime falls from 9.18 to 3.13 hours. With all RAPID components enabled, training completes in 1.15 hours using 896 rather than 19,840 API calls per run, and aggregate final success rises from 86.3\% to 98.7\%. This represents an 8.0$\times$ end-to-end speedup and a 95.5\% reduction in API usage. An offline evaluation with Gemma~3 12B and GPT-4.1 mini demonstrates that single-request prompting reduces labeling latency and cost across both models. Code is available at: https://github.com/rapid-vlm/rapid-vlm-rl.

</details>


### [160] [TRACE: Coverage Path Planning for Unknown Environments Using Hierarchical Coverage Tree](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21777)
*Zongyuan Shen,Haodong Liu,Gao Wang,Shancheng Zhao,Dehua Zhou,Yaming Ou,Zhongqiang Ren,Yikui Zhai,C. L. Philip Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TRACE 是一种面向未知环境的实时在线覆盖路径规划（CPP）算法。它基于层次化覆盖树（hierarchical coverage tree）全局表示未覆盖空间的连通性，通过递归展开树节点显式建模新出现的连通区域，并维护一个增量式全局巡游（global tour）；局部仅在受影响区域细化路径，从而降低全局重规划的代价。理论分析给出计算复杂度、完全覆盖性以及增量全局巡游近似的近似界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线覆盖未知环境时，随着机器人的探索，新发现的障碍物和已覆盖单元会把剩余未覆盖空间切割成多个不连通区域。传统 CPP 方法需要频繁进行全局重规划，计算开销大，且容易破坏已有的覆盖访问顺序，导致覆盖过程不连贯、路径冗长、重叠率高、转弯次数多。因此需要一个既能显式表示空间连通性结构、又能在局部高效更新全局规划的在线覆盖框架。

Method: TRACE 采用层次化覆盖树作为全局表示：环境逐步揭示与覆盖后，剩余未覆盖空间分裂为不连通区域，算法递归地展开相应树节点以显式表示这些区域并组织后续覆盖规划。基于更新后的树，维护一个增量式全局巡游以引导覆盖过程；局部细化只作用于受影响的部分，同时保留未变化区域的访问顺序，减少全局重规划负担并保持覆盖进程的连续性。全局巡游引导下，局部规划器生成往复式（back-and-forth）覆盖路径，并在合适时机切换到全局巡游感知规划，以高效完成目标区域。论文还进行理论分析，给出计算复杂度、完全覆盖性质保证，以及增量全局巡游细化的近似界。

Result: 通过大量高保真仿真和真实移动机器人实验进行评测，并与六种现有 CPP 方法对比。评价指标包括覆盖时间、路径长度、重叠率和转弯次数，TRACE 在这些指标上均取得显著改进。

Conclusion: TRACE 的主要贡献是提出基于层次化覆盖树的在线 CPP 框架，通过显式建模未覆盖区域的连通结构、维护增量全局巡游并局部化细化，在保证完全覆盖的同时显著降低重规划开销、提升覆盖效率与路径质量。理论层面给出了复杂度、完全覆盖性和近似界。局限在于其评测集中于移动机器人平坦环境的二维覆盖场景，未涉及三维空间覆盖、动态障碍或非完整约束下的复杂运动学问题。

Abstract: This paper presents a novel online coverage path planning (CPP) algorithm, called TRACE, for real-time coverage of unknown environments. TRACE is built upon a hierarchical coverage tree that provides a global representation of the evolving connectivity of the uncovered space. As the environment is incrementally revealed and covered, newly discovered obstacles and covered cells may fragment the remaining uncovered space into disconnected regions. TRACE recursively expands the corresponding tree nodes to explicitly represent these regions and organize them for subsequent coverage planning. Based on the updated tree, an incremental global tour is maintained to guide the coverage process. TRACE locally refines only the affected portions while preserving the visiting order of unchanged regions, thereby reducing the computational burden of global replanning and maintaining a consistent coverage progression. Guided by the global tour, a local planner generates back-and-forth coverage paths and switches to global-tour-aware planning to efficiently complete the target regions. Theoretical analysis establishes the computational complexity and complete coverage property of TRACE, and derives an approximation bound for the incremental global tour refinement. The performance of TRACE is evaluated through extensive high-fidelity simulations and real-robot experiments using a mobile robot. Comparative evaluations against six existing CPP methods demonstrate significant improvements in coverage time, path length, overlap ratio, and number of turns.

</details>


### [161] [Compact but Moving: Intervention-Relevant Geometry in Recurrent World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21787)
*Yuming Chen,Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文在一个受控的循环世界模型中研究"紧凑干预"进入高维循环状态后的演化规律，发现纠正信号会迅速离开固定的低秩进入子空间，但通过事实循环雅可比链传递（transport）进入方向的低秩像仍能捕捉大部分非线性纠正；这种"传递/功能"模式在结构化GRU、参数匹配的LSTM等多个独立训练模型中重复出现，并进一步用有限时域"未来响应算子"刻画了全循环载体上的局部几何组织。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作在一个受控循环世界模型中识别出一个特定于检查点（checkpoint-specific）的秩-4接口，用于一次性的反事实速度干预，表明学习到的世界模型可能拥有紧凑的干预表示，即使其循环状态是高维的。然而，该纠正进入模型后究竟发生什么尚不清楚：这种紧凑结构是否在动力学传播中保持，是否意味着模型存在固定或动态闭合的低维状态，仍是未解问题。该问题对理解世界模型的可解释性、可控性与干预可迁移性具有重要意义。

Method: (1) 以受控循环世界模型为对象，追踪反事实速度纠正信号进入循环动力学后的轨迹，发现其迅速离开原本的固定低秩进入子空间。(2) 关键创新是"切空间传递"：将进入方向通过事实（factual）循环雅可比链进行传播，得到状态相关的低秩像，用以预测后续的非线性纠正，并以此进行重启（restart）实验。(3) 通过在多个独立训练的结构化GRU和参数匹配的LSTM（用特权紧凑纠正初始化）上验证该传递/功能模式的普遍性。(4) 进一步定义有限时域"未来响应算子"（future-response operator），刻画全循环载体上的响应几何；采用方向面板（direction panels）与局部算子对有限扰动效应进行排序；并做人脸/全幅度检测实验，测量事实端点切残差并检验响应重构。

Result: 实验表明：纠正会迅速离开固定进入子空间，但经雅可比链传递的进入方向低秩像仍能捕捉大部分非线性纠正；用切空间预测的纠正重启可保留可观的反事实未来功能。该模式在多个独立训练的结构化GRU以及参数匹配的LSTM上重复出现。未来响应算子的结果显示：修补（patching）会将其主要未来敏感方向移向与原生死反事实组织相匹配的方向；局部算子能在修补与原生反事实基点处对注册方向面板上的有限扰动效应进行准确排序。一项单独的全幅度检测发现显著的事实端点切残差，并在三个检查点中的两个上支持响应重构。

Conclusion: 论文的主要贡献在于：证明紧凑干预结构可以作为一种移动的、状态相关的局部几何持续存在于高维循环动力学中，而不必意味着存在固定或动态闭合的低维状态；并提出了雅可比传递与未来响应算子等分析工具来刻画这一几何。其意义在于深化了对世界模型中干预表示、可控性与可解释性的理解。潜在局限在于：研究局限于受控循环世界模型与特定的一阶反事实速度干预，模型规模与任务多样性有限；对响应重构的支持仅在三个检查点中的两个上成立，结论的普适性仍需更广泛的模型与任务验证。

Abstract: Learned world models may have compact interventions even when their recurrent state is high-dimensional, but it is unclear what happens to such a correction after it enters the model. We study this question in a controlled recurrent world model where prior work identified a checkpoint-specific rank-4 interface for one-shot counterfactual velocity interventions. The correction rapidly leaves this fixed entry subspace during autonomous rollout. Nevertheless, a low-rank image obtained by transporting the entry directions through the factual recurrent Jacobian chain continues to capture most of the nonlinear correction. Restarts using the tangent-predicted correction preserve substantial counterfactual future function. This transport/function pattern recurs across independently trained structured-GRU models and a parameter-matched LSTM initialized with a privileged compact correction. We further characterize a finite-horizon future-response operator over the full recurrent carrier. Patching shifts its leading future-sensitive directions toward the matched native-counterfactual organization, and the local operator accurately ranks finite perturbation effects over the registered direction panels at the patched and native-counterfactual basepoints. A separate full-amplitude assay finds substantial factual-endpoint tangent residuals and supports response reconfiguration in two of three checkpoints. Together, these results show that compact intervention structure can persist as a moving, state-dependent local geometry embedded in high-dimensional recurrent dynamics, without implying a fixed or dynamically closed low-dimensional state.

</details>


### [162] [GALA: Geometry-Aware Latent Action Modeling for Vision-Language-Action Model Pretraining across Embodiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21948)
*Yichen Liu,Puzhen Yuan,Xiang Zhu,Yanjiang Guo,Jianyu Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GALA 提出一种几何感知的潜在动作建模框架（Geometry-Aware Latent-Action），在基于图像的潜在动作模型（LAM）基础上引入 3D 末端执行器几何运动信息，并设计统一末端执行器运动表示（UEMR），以在保留细粒度手指级动作细节的同时提升跨具身泛化能力，为多具身数据（含无动作自我中心人类视频）上的 VLA 预训练提供有效监督。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从多具身数据集学习大规模视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型的核心难点在于不同末端执行器（夹爪、灵巧手、人手等）的动作空间高度异构，难以直接对齐。潜在动作模型（LAM）通过从多样化视频中学习与具身无关的动作表示来缓解该问题，但现有基于图像的 LAM 往往只能捕捉场景级动态，无法刻画细粒度的末端执行器关节运动，尤其是人类手和灵巧机器人手的手指级几何形变。这一局限直接制约了模型在需要精细操作的任务上的跨具身迁移能力。

Method: 1）核心思路：将图像潜在动作与 3D 末端执行器几何运动相融合，构建几何感知的潜在动作表示。2）关键问题：直接引入点云会得到细粒度较强但共享语义匮乏的动作表示，反而破坏跨具身预训练的可迁移性。3）解决方案 UEMR（Unified End-effector Motion Representation）：在保留细粒度运动信息的同时增强跨具身泛化性，使不同形态末端执行器的运动被映射到统一表示空间。4）GALA 架构：以 UEMR 为基础，同时使用两类潜在动作——视觉潜在动作负责捕捉场景级动态，几何潜在动作负责捕捉共享的细粒度末端执行器关节运动，二者共同为多具身数据（包括无动作标签的自我中心人类视频）上的 VLA 预训练提供监督信号。

Result: 论文通过三类实验进行评估：细粒度运动探测（fine-grained motion probing）、跨具身检索（cross-embodiment retrieval）以及下游 VLA 任务评测。结果表明 GALA 能够有效建模跨具身的可泛化细粒度运动，在 RoboCasa-GR1 基准上取得 68.3% 的成功率，并在真实世界任务中达到 75.5% 的成功率。论文附带代码、附录与演示（项目主页提供）。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于：(1) 提出 GALA 几何感知潜在动作建模框架，弥补图像 LAM 在细粒度末端执行器运动建模上的不足；(2) 设计 UEMR 统一末端执行器运动表示，在细粒度与跨具身共享语义之间取得平衡；(3) 通过视觉与几何潜在动作的联合监督，使无动作自我中心人类视频可用于 VLA 预训练，并在仿真与真实任务上取得较高成功率。潜在限制：抽象中未给出基线对比细节、消融设置及 UEMR 的具体构建方式，跨具身泛化在差异更大的末端执行器形态（如非人形具身）上的表现仍有待验证。

Abstract: Learning large-scale vision-language-action (VLA) models from multi-embodiment datasets remains challenging due to heterogeneous action spaces across end effectors. Although latent action models (LAMs) can learn embodiment-agnostic action representations from diverse video data, existing image-based LAMs often fail to capture fine-grained end-effector articulation, particularly finger-level geometric changes in human and dexterous robot hands. To address this limitation, we propose GALA, a Geometry-Aware Latent-Action modeling framework that augments image-based latent actions with 3D end-effector geometric motion. However, naively incorporating point clouds yields fine-grained action representations with limited shared semantics, hindering cross-embodiment pretraining. To address this issue, we introduce the Unified End-effector Motion Representation (UEMR), which preserves fine-grained motion information while improving the cross-embodiment generalizability of latent actions. Building upon UEMR, GALA combines visual latent actions that capture scene-level dynamics with geometric latent actions that capture shared fine-grained end-effector articulation, providing effective supervision for VLA pretraining from multi-embodiment data, including action-free ego-centric human videos. Experiments on fine-grained motion probing, cross-embodiment retrieval, and downstream VLA evaluation demonstrate GALA's effectiveness in modeling generalizable fine-grained motions across embodiments, achieving 68.3% RoboCasa-GR1 success rate and 75.5% real-world success rate. Code, appendix, and demos are available at https://puzhenyuan.github.io/GALA-website/.

</details>


### [163] [From Pretraining to Proficiency: Real-World Subtask RL for Long-Horizon Manipulation with Minimal Human Intervention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21788)
*Sichang Su,Benjamin Yang,Zhiyun Deng,Boyuan Liang,Yip Fun Yeung,Zelin Wang,Lingfeng Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PARTS（Policy Adaptation with RL on Targeted Subtasks）是一个真实世界的子任务强化学习微调框架：冻结预训练的机器人基础策略提供常规动作，由智能体生成的"选择器"和"成功验证器"激活残差修正并提供局部子任务奖励，使策略能够仅依赖稀疏奖励聚焦于长时序任务中的瓶颈子任务。在双臂 YAM 与单臂 Franka 任务上，完整任务成功率分别从 32% 提升至 61%、从 50% 提升至 95%，每个任务平均仅需数十分钟真实世界 RL 采样。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练的机器人基础策略（foundation policy）在长时序任务中往往能完成大部分步骤，却反复在少数关键子任务（瓶颈）上失败。传统解决路径存在两难：若通过监督微调（SFT）补数据，需要操作员重复演示策略已经掌握的行为，成本高且冗余；若用强化学习（RL）微调，现有方法在稀疏奖励下难以支撑长时序任务学习，因为完整任务级别的成功信号极其稀少、难以归因到具体子任务。因此需要一种能把训练"练习"集中到瓶颈子任务、同时尽量减少人工介入（仅需重置而非持续演示）的真实世界 RL 微调方案。

Method: 核心思路是在冻结的预训练基础策略之上学习残差修正，并把学习范围收缩到瓶颈子任务。执行时预训练策略始终输出名义动作（nominal actions），保证回放能以最小人工干预向前推进；由智能体自行生成的 selectors（选择器）决定何时激活残差策略、success verifiers（成功验证器）判定子任务是否完成，从而提供局部（子任务级）结果奖励。这种局部奖励使方法能在完整任务成功样本稀少的情况下，仍从成功子任务中学习。训练流程将在线 RL 与"成功加权重训练"（success-reweighted retraining）结合，每轮重训练得到的残差策略被重新部署以采集新经验，形成迭代式数据收集与策略改进循环。人类职责被限制在初始阶段识别瓶颈子任务，以及在需要时进行物理重置。

Result: 在双臂 YAM 和单臂 Franka 真实机器人平台上评测。PARTS 将完整任务成功率分别从 32% 提升到 61%、从 50% 提升到 95%，平均每个任务仅消耗数十分钟的真实世界 RL 采样。在与现有真实世界 RL 微调方法在相同机器人采样预算（robot-rollout budget）下的对比中，PARTS 将完整任务成功率提升超过 25%，同时所需人工介入更少。

Conclusion: 主要贡献是提出面向真实世界长时序机器人任务的子任务级 RL 微调框架，通过冻结基础策略 + 残差修正 + 局部子任务奖励 + 成功加权迭代重训练，突破了稀疏奖励下长时序 RL 微调的瓶颈，并在两套真实平台上以较低采样与人工成本显著提升完整任务成功率。潜在局限包括：瓶颈子任务仍需人工在配置阶段识别、需要人工物理重置、选择器与成功验证器的可靠性影响学习信号质量，且评测规模限于两个平台与有限任务数量，泛化性有待验证。

Abstract: A pretrained robot foundation policy may execute most of a long-horizon task yet repeatedly fail at a few critical subtasks. Collecting additional full-task demonstrations for supervised fine-tuning (SFT) requires operators to repeat behaviors the policy already performs well. Reinforcement learning (RL) fine-tuning offers a promising path to bridge this gap, but existing approaches struggle to solve long-horizon tasks using only sparse rewards. We present PARTS (Policy Adaptation with RL on Targeted Subtasks), a real-world subtask RL framework that concentrates practice at these bottlenecks while allowing training rollouts to proceed with minimal human intervention. The frozen pretrained policy supplies nominal actions throughout execution, while agent-generated selectors and success verifiers activate residual corrections and provide local outcome rewards. These rewards support learning from successful subtasks even when complete-task successes are scarce. Training combines online RL with success-reweighted retraining, and each retrained residual policy is redeployed to collect further experience. Humans identify bottlenecks during setup and perform physical resets when needed. On bimanual YAM and single-arm Franka tasks, PARTS improves complete-task success from 32% to 61% and from 50% to 95%, respectively, using tens of minutes of real-world RL rollouts per task on average. Compared with existing real-world RL fine-tuning methods, PARTS raises full-task success by more than 25% under the same robot-rollout budget while requiring less human involvement.

</details>


### [164] [AcousticDiffusion: Semantically Conditioned Audio-Guided Diffusion Policy for Search-and-Rescue Assistance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21792)
*Iana Zhura,Didar Seyidov,Dmitrii Plotnikov,Hajira Amjad,Miguel Altamirano Cabrera,Dzmitry Tsetserukou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AcousticDiffusion 提出一种语义条件下、音频引导的扩散策略，让救援机器人通过声学观测（而非依赖视觉）向人类呼救者导航。核心是将语义角色识别、麦克风阵列到达方向与机器人自运动补偿融合进贝叶斯鸟瞰信念场，作为扩散模型的生成条件输出路径点轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 救援机器人在视觉退化或被遮挡的场景（烟雾、废墟、黑暗）中，视觉感知失效，难以发现并向被困人员靠拢。声音是人类呼救最自然且视觉受阻时仍可获取的线索，但声学观测存在定位模糊、混响与多说话人干扰等问题。该工作旨在把不确定的声学观测转化为可靠的、可执行的导航行为，特别强调区分“呼救者”与“干扰说话人”，使机器人优先接近需要帮助的人。

Method: 1) 采用冻结的预训练音频识别器处理 10.24 秒音频窗口，通过语音门控（speech gating）与困境感知优先级（distress-aware prioritization）把识别结果映射为“源级导航角色”（如 HELP 指定呼叫者）。2) 将麦克风阵列的 DOA 测量递归整合进以机器人为中心的贝叶斯鸟瞰（BEV）信念场，形成对声源位置的语义信念表示。3) 通过自运动补偿对齐连续观测，逐步约束声源位置，同时保留由方位（bearing）导致的距离不确定性。4) 以语义信念、近期声学观测、音频特征和机器人状态作为条件，驱动扩散模型生成路径点轨迹（生成式扩散策略）。5) 未在仿真训练的模型上额外重训练即可部署到 ZSL-1 四足机器人上在线运行。

Result: 在基于真实录音的合成导航验证集上：平均终点方位误差 11.20°，91.78% 的轨迹落在呼叫者 30° 方位内；干扰源拒绝率为 89.20%–98.99%；在 91.07% 的窗口中被指定为 HELP 的呼叫者优先于竞争说话人。在 ZSL-1 四足机器人上在线部署（无额外重训练）：平均方位误差 64.9°，优于 A*（98.2°）与 RRT（90.4°），规划器平均计算时间 6.07 ms。尽管声学定位并不完美，平均最终声源距离由使用 ODAS 引导的经典规划器的 3.96 m 降至 2.48 m，提升 37.4%。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于将语义化的音频理解、贝叶斯声源信念融合与扩散策略相结合，构建了面向人类呼叫者的音频引导导航框架，并实现了从仿真到真实四足机器人的零重训练在线部署，验证了声学感知在视觉受阻救援场景中的可用性。局限包括：验证主要在基于录音的合成导航集上进行，真实机器人方位误差（64.9°）明显高于仿真（11.20°），说明声学定位精度与仿真到现实的差距仍显著；依赖预训练音频识别器与人工设定的语义角色（如 HELP 优先）机制，其泛化到更复杂、多说话人真实环境的能力尚待验证。

Abstract: Navigating toward human callers is an important capability for rescue robots operating where visual contact is degraded or occluded. We present AcousticDiffusion, a semantically conditioned, audio-guided diffusion policy for human-directed navigation. A frozen pretrained audio recognizer processes 10.24 s windows, with speech gating and distress-aware prioritization converting recognition outputs into source-level navigation roles. Microphone-array direction-of-arrival measurements are recursively integrated into a robot-centric Bayesian bird's-eye-view belief field. Ego-motion compensation aligns successive observations, progressively constraining source position while preserving bearing-induced range uncertainty. The semantic belief, recent acoustic observations, audio features, and robot state condition a diffusion model that generates waypoint trajectories. On a synthetic-navigation validation set using recorded audio, AcousticDiffusion achieves a mean end-point bearing error of 11.20 degrees, with 91.78% of trajectories aligned within 30 degrees of the caller. Distractor rejection ranges from 89.20% to 98.99%, and the policy favors a HELP-designated caller over a competing speaker in 91.07% of windows. Deployed online on a ZSL-1 quadruped without additional retraining, it achieves a mean bearing error of 64.9 degrees, compared with 98.2 degrees for A* and 90.4 degrees for RRT, with a mean planner compute time of 6.07 ms. Despite imperfect acoustic localization, the reported mean final source distance is reduced from 3.96 m for the classical planners using ODAS-derived (Open embedded Audition System) guidance to 2.48 m, a 37.4% improvement. These results demonstrate the framework's ability to translate uncertain acoustic observations into closer approaches to human callers.

</details>


### [165] [Contact-Rich Motion Planning via GPU-Parallel Mode Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21803)
*Jiayun Li,Georgia Chalvatzaki*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CoMET：重新审视接触丰富运动规划（CRMP）中的“广域接触模式评估”，用 GPU 并行轨迹评估 + 贪心接触模式扩展替代搜索启发式/优化重构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触丰富运动规划（contact-rich motion planning, CRMP）对机器人操作与运动至关重要，但由于接触决策具有组合爆炸特性（组合式的接触模式序列），计算上非常困难。现有方法通常通过搜索启发式（如树搜索）或对问题进行优化重构，来规避对接触模式序列的“广域评估”（broad evaluation），从而牺牲了全局最优性或依赖强假设。作者提出：在现代 GPU 硬件条件下，直接进行广域显式接触模式评估可能重新变得可行且高效，这一视角值得重新审视。

Method: 提出 CoMET（Contact-Mode Expansion with parallel Trajectory optimization），核心是两个组件的结合：(1) GPU 并行的轨迹评估器（high-throughput trajectory evaluator），可对大量候选接触模式下的轨迹进行批量并行求解/评估；(2) 贪心接触模式扩展（greedy contact-mode expansion），以局部、GPU 友好的方式逐步扩展接触模式空间。方法不依赖复杂的搜索启发式或优化重构，而是直接枚举式评估接触模式并对轨迹做并行优化。消融实验用于分离性能来源。

Result: 在平面推动（planar pushing）基准上，CoMET 在解质量与规划时间上与基于优化、采样以及树搜索的基线方法具有竞争力；在几乎所有实例上达到与全枚举参考（full-enumeration reference）相当的结果，同时使用更少的评估次数和更短的规划时间。消融实验表明，性能提升的很大一部分来自高吞吐量轨迹评估器。在双臂非抓取操作（bimanual nonprehensile manipulation）任务中，随着模式空间增大，GPU 友好的局部模式扩展相比所测试的自适应树搜索取得更高的规划成功率。

Conclusion: 贡献在于提出一个简单但有效的 CRMP 替代方案：利用 GPU 并行计算直接进行广域显式接触模式评估，并用贪心扩展控制搜索规模；实验证明其在平面推动与双臂非抓取操作任务上兼具解质量与效率优势，消融显示高吞吐轨迹评估器是关键。局限在于：贪心模式扩展可能陷入局部最优（相较全枚举）；实验局限于平面推动与特定双臂非抓取任务，对更复杂三维接触场景的泛化性尚待验证；性能强依赖 GPU 硬件与可并行化的轨迹评估器。

Abstract: Contact-rich motion planning (CRMP) is essential for robotic manipulation and locomotion, yet remains computationally challenging due to combinatorial contact decisions. Existing methods typically avoid broad evaluation of contact-mode sequences through search heuristics or optimization reformulations. We revisit broad evaluation in light of modern GPU hardware and introduce Contact-Mode Expansion with parallel Trajectory optimization (CoMET), which combines GPU-parallel trajectory evaluation with greedy contact-mode expansion. On planar pushing benchmarks, CoMET is competitive with optimization-based, sampling, and tree-search baselines in solution quality and planning time, matching the full-enumeration reference on nearly all instances with fewer evaluations and shorter planning times. Ablations suggest that much of the performance gain comes from the high-throughput trajectory evaluator. In bimanual nonprehensile manipulation, GPU-friendly local mode expansion achieves higher planning success than the tested adaptive tree search as the mode space grows. These results demonstrate that broad explicit mode evaluation provides a simple yet effective alternative for CRMP.

</details>


### [166] [A Sim-to-Real Integration Pipeline for Training and Deployment of Chunk-Based VLA Manipulation Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21817)
*Mathilde Kappel,Clémence Grislain,Mohamed Chetouani,Olivier Sigaud,Louis Annabi,Faïz Ben Amar,Stéphane Doncieux,Mahdi Khoramshahi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个开源的 sim-to-real 实验协议，通过在仿真中生成专家轨迹并在真实 Franka FR3 上开环回放来采集真实视觉与本体感知观测，从而以低成本构建符合 VLA 训练格式的机器人演示数据集，并复用同一部署栈进行闭环策略评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型已成为将语义指令、场景视觉观测与本体感知映射到机器人动作的主流范式，其中多数先进模型在末端执行器位姿空间中以动作块（action chunks）序列的形式预测动作。然而训练与评测这类模型依赖大规模真实世界演示数据，需要在真机上同步采集机器人动作与对应的视觉、本体感知观测。此类数据通常依靠人类遥操作采集，成本高、耗时长、难以规模化，构成了 VLA 研究的主要瓶颈。该协议正是为缓解这一数据采集瓶颈而设计。

Method: 核心方法是构建一条 sim-to-real 一体化的数据采集与评测流水线：（1）在仿真环境中生成专家轨迹；（2）将该轨迹在真实 Franka FR3 上开环回放，同步记录真实视觉与本体感知观测，并转换为与 VLA 训练兼容的数据格式；（3）复用同一部署栈，在闭环条件下对训练好的策略在相同硬件配置上进行评测。由于每段真实记录都与其对应的仿真轨迹配对，该协议可直接度量 sim-to-real 差距。作者开源了采集的数据集（托管于 Hugging Face）与流水线源码。需要注意的是，本文为实验协议/工具类工作，摘要中并未描述具体的 VLA 模型架构或策略学习算法。

Result: 实验平台为真实 Franka FR3 机械臂。数据采集采用仿真专家轨迹的开环回放，策略评测采用闭环部署，二者共享完全一致的硬件与部署栈配置。协议的一个关键产出是配对的（仿真轨迹，真实观测）数据，可用于直接量化 sim-to-real 差距。作者公开释放了采集的数据集与源码。摘要中未给出具体的任务数量、轨迹条数、成功率等量化指标，也未报告与基线方法的对比结果，评测指标与数据集规模需参阅全文。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于提出并开源了一套低成本、可复现的 sim-to-real 数据采集与评测协议，将数据收集与策略评测统一在同一硬件配置下，并通过仿真-真实配对数据提供 sim-to-real 差距的直接测量，从而缓解 VLA 研究对昂贵人类遥操作数据的依赖。其意义在于降低了 VLA 训练数据获取的门槛并提升了实验可复现性。潜在局限包括：依赖仿真专家轨迹的质量与真实性、开环回放对初始条件与状态漂移较为敏感、协议目前绑定于 Franka FR3 这一特定硬件平台，且摘要未提供定量验证结果来证明所采集数据对策略训练的实际增益。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have become a prominent paradigm for mapping multimodal inputs, including semantic instructions, visual observations of the scene, and proprioceptive observations, to robot actions. Most state-of-the-art models predict actions in the end-effector pose space as sequences of action chunks. Training and evaluating these models requires large-scale collections of real-world demonstrations, pairing robot actions with the corresponding visual and proprioceptive observations. Collecting such data on real hardware typically relies on human teleoperation, making the process costly, time-consuming, and difficult to scale. We present an open-source sim-to-real experimental protocol that addresses this bottleneck: expert trajectories generated in simulation are replayed open-loop on a real Franka FR3 setup, where the corresponding real visual and proprioceptive observations are recorded and converted into a format compatible with VLA training. The same deployment stack is then reused, in closed-loop, to evaluate a trained policy on that setup, so that data collection and evaluation share an identical hardware configuration. Because each real recording is paired with the simulated trajectory that produced it, the protocol also yields a direct measurement of the sim-to-real gap. We release the collected datasets on Hugging Face together with the pipeline source code https://gitlab.isir.upmc.fr/kappel/sim2real_public_chunk_control.

</details>


### [167] [LunaDrive: A Delay-Compensated High-Voltage GaN FET-Based Motor Driver for Dynamic Robots with Flat BLDC Motors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21818)
*Sota Yuzaki,Temma Suzuki,Hiromi Tada,Masanori Konishi,Kento Kawaharazuka,Kei Okada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文针对动态机器人中高功率扁平BLDC电机在48V以下额定电压限制下转速受限的问题，开发了一款基于GaN FET的紧凑型电机驱动器，并研究了超出额定范围高速区间的延迟补偿效果。实验在96V下实现30A连续电流、80A峰值电流与3110Hz最大电频率，并用24S LiPo电池（100V）完成了高速负载起升实验，验证了其在动态机器人中的适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高功率扁平BLDC电机性能的提升推动了动态机器人的发展，但商用伺服电机通常假设工作电压为48V或更低，这限制了最大转速。动态机器人需要快速能量输出，电压约束直接制约了运动性能。因此，超出额定电压驱动电机以提升瞬时最大转速是必要的。然而，电机驱动器中的半导体器件存在耐压与电流容量之间的权衡：传统基于Si MOSFET的驱动器难以同时实现高电压与大电流。GaN FET虽然具有潜力，但能够安装在扁平BLDC电机后侧的紧凑型驱动器仍然有限。本文正是针对这一硬件瓶颈展开研究。

Method: 本文设计并实现了一款面向高功率扁平BLDC电机的GaN FET驱动器。核心思路是利用GaN FET在高压与大电流兼顾方面的优势，构建可安装于扁平BLDC电机后侧（rear side）的紧凑型驱动器，从而适配动态机器人的空间与功率需求。此外，研究还考察了在超出额定工作范围的高速区间中延迟补偿（delay compensation）的作用，以改善高速区域的控制性能。硬件设计关注高电压（96V乃至100V）与大电流（峰值80A）条件下的驱动能力，并配合散热片支持持续电流输出。

Result: 实验结果表明：在96V工作电压并加装散热片的情况下，驱动器可实现30A的连续电流；同时确认了80A的峰值电流以及3110Hz的最大电频率。研究还进行了由LiPo电池24S（100V）驱动的高速负载起升实验，验证了该驱动器适用于动态机器人操作。论文同时分析了高速区（超出额定工作范围）中延迟补偿的效果。

Conclusion: 本文的主要贡献是开发了一款基于GaN FET、面向高功率扁平BLDC电机的紧凑型驱动器，突破了传统Si MOSFET驱动器在高压与大电流之间的权衡限制，并验证了其在超出额定电压条件下提升瞬时转速的可行性，同时考察了高速区延迟补偿的作用。该工作对动态机器人电机驱动系统具有实际工程意义。可能存在的局限包括：紧凑驱动器的散热与持续功率能力受散热片依赖、高压大电流下的可靠性与电磁兼容问题，以及实验主要集中于负载起升场景，缺乏更广泛的动态机器人运动任务验证。

Abstract: The performance improvement of high-power flat BLDC motors has accelerated the development of dynamic robots. However, many commercially available servo motors assume operating voltages of 48 V or lower, which limits the maximum rotational speed. Dynamic robots require rapid energy generation, so this voltage constraint restricts motion performance. Therefore, driving motors beyond the rated voltage is desirable to increase the instantaneous maximum speed. On the other hand, semiconductor devices used in motor drivers have a trade-off between voltage rating and current capacity. Conventional drivers using Si MOSFETs have difficulty achieving both high-voltage and high-current operation. Although GaN FETs are promising, compact drivers that can be mounted on the rear side of flat BLDC motors remain limited. In this study, a motor driver for high-power flat BLDC motors using GaN FETs is developed. The effect of delay compensation in the high-speed region beyond the rated operating range is also investigated. In the experiments, under 96 V operation, a continuous current of 30 A was achieved with a heat sink attached. A peak current of 80 A and a maximum electrical frequency of 3110 Hz were confirmed. A high-speed load lifting experiment driven by a LiPo battery 24S (100 V) was also conducted, demonstrating applicability to dynamic robot operation.

</details>


### [168] [PopNavShift: Stress-Testing Social Navigation under Behavioral Population Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21838)
*Kaizhen Tan,Diyu Zheng,Tim Guangyu Wu,ChengHe Guan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出 PopNavShift——一个配对（matched）仿真框架，用于在社会导航策略评测中系统性地改变行人群体行为分布。它通过大模型生成"群体条件化"的行人运动参数，并在相同机器人/行人配置下比较多种导航策略，发现策略排名对行人行为分布（尤其是时间压力等上下文因素）高度敏感，且这种敏感性在行人负担指标上远大于机器人通行效率指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
