Motivation: 社会导航（social navigation）算法通常只在固定的行人行为分布下评估，但现实中不同个体和社会情境下行人面对机器人时的反应差异很大。这导致评测结论可能无法泛化：一个"更优"的策略可能只是对特定行人群体/行为假设过拟合。论文的动机是要提供一个能压力测试（stress-test）导航策略在行人群体分布偏移（population shift）下鲁棒性的框架，并同时关注机器人表现与行人承担的代价（行人负担）。

Method: 1) PopNavShift 是一个"配对"仿真框架：保持机器人起始/目标、人群体配置等条件一致，只改变行人群体行为，从而进行公平的对照比较。<br>2) 行人运动建模：使用 MatrAIx Persona 1M 中 600 条合成人物（persona）记录作为提示，输入 LLM（文中称 Gemini 3.7 Flash）生成群体条件化的行人运动画像，再将模型响应确定性地映射为有界（bounded）的运动参数（如速度、让行/回避倾向、时间压力等）。<br>3) 对比三种代表性导航策略：反应式避让（reactive avoidance）、提前让行（early yielding）、互惠避碰（reciprocal collision avoidance）。<br>4) 在 8 种群体条件下、共 7,488 次配对机器人运行（matched robot runs）中进行评测；并做"同 202 个 persona、仅改变时间压力"的配对干预实验，以隔离单一行为因素对策略排名的影响。<br>5) 采用第二个行人动力学模型验证结论的模型无关性。

Result: 评测覆盖 8 种群体条件、7,488 次配对运行。关键发现：在相同 202 个 persona 上仅改变时间压力，策略排名翻转比例取决于评价指标——基于机器人通行时间为 8.6%，基于行人平均延误为 22.4%，基于最差十分位（worst-decile）延误则达 23.9%。跨群体条件看，这种对行为分布的敏感性在"行人负担"指标上高于"机器人通行时间"，并在空间受限（spatially constrained）场景中进一步加剧；相同的定性模式在更换为第二个行人动力学模型后依然成立。指标包括机器人通行时间、行人平均延误、最差十分位延误等。

Conclusion: 主要贡献是提出 PopNavShift 这一配对仿真与压力测试框架，将 LLM 驱动的群体条件化行人建模引入导航策略评测，并系统量化了行为分布偏移（尤其是时间压力、空间约束）如何改变策略排名。核心结论是：社会导航策略应跨行为群体进行评估，并同时报告机器人性能与行人负担，否则结论可能不稳定甚至误导。意义在于揭示现有固定分布评测的脆弱性。可能的局限：评测基于仿真与合成 persona/LLM 映射的运动参数，未在真实机器人或真实行人数据上验证，行为参数映射的生态效度与保真度有待检验；结论依赖于所选策略、指标与两种行人动力学模型。

Abstract: Social-navigation algorithms are often evaluated under a fixed pedestrian-behavior distribution, despite substantial variation in pedestrian responses to robots across individuals and social contexts. We introduce PopNavShift, a matched simulation framework for stress-testing social-navigation strategies under pedestrian population shifts. PopNavShift constructs population-conditioned pedestrian motion profiles by prompting Gemini 3.7 Flash with 600 synthetic persona records from MatrAIx Persona 1M and deterministically mapping the responses into bounded motion parameters. It then compares three representative navigation strategies, reactive avoidance, early yielding, and reciprocal collision avoidance, across eight population conditions and 7,488 matched robot runs. In a matched intervention on the same 202 personas, changing only time pressure reverses 8.6% of controller rankings based on robot travel time, but 22.4% based on mean pedestrian delay and 23.9% based on worst-decile delay. Across population conditions, this sensitivity is greater for pedestrian burden than for robot travel time and increases in spatially constrained settings; the same qualitative pattern persists under a second pedestrian dynamics model. These findings support evaluating navigation strategies across behavioral populations using both robot performance and pedestrian burden.

</details>


### [169] [VIRGA: Virtual-Agent-Intermediated Riemannian Geometry for Active-Sensing Air-Ground Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21883)
*Fenghe Guo,Runjie Shen,Chenyang Sun,Junrui Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VIRGA 是一个用于空地协同自主的神经-几何协调框架，将双 LiDAR 观测转化为有界、来源特定的黎曼场（Riemannian fields），并通过带互惠弹性反馈的"虚拟智能体"进行耦合，从而在动态障碍、异构运动和任务主动性变化下维持 UAV 对 UGV 云台 LiDAR 的可观测性与安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空地自主协同的核心难点在于：UAV 必须始终被搭载在 UGV 上的云台 LiDAR 观测到，同时两个平台需要规避动态障碍，并协调异构运动（空中 vs 地面）、受限的感知范围和不断变化的任务主导权（task initiative）。这是一个闭环耦合问题：安全性（避障）、可观测性（保持视场）和运动可行性三者相互制约。已有方法要么几何避障强但实时性差，要么响应快但余隙不足或违反安全性，无法在统一框架内同时满足空地协同要求。该问题对仓储巡检、洞穴/受限空间探索等场景具有实际意义。

Method: 提出 VIRGA——神经几何协调框架。核心是：1）将双 LiDAR（UAV 与 UGV 云台）观测转换为有界、来源特定（source-specific）的黎曼场，用于建模平台的几何约束与感知约束；2）通过一个"虚拟智能体"（virtual agent）以互惠弹性反馈（reciprocal elastic feedback）方式耦合两套场，实现双向协商式的协调参考生成；3）平台感知的执行映射（platform-aware execution maps）将共享的协调参考转换为 UAV、UGV 及云台各自的可行指令，并施加主动观测保护机制（active-observation safeguards）以确保 UAV 始终处于视场内。框架强调在线几何评估，并通过消融验证了在线几何评估、虚拟智能体中介和互惠反馈三部分各自的贡献。

Result: 与三个互补基线对比：1）改造的 Ray-RMP 控制器黎曼响应最快，但在耦合空地任务中安全余隙不足；2）稠密解析黎曼场几何避障更好，但评估代价高，无法稳定维持视场；3）改造的 ColAG 控制器延迟最低，但仍出现安全性和可观测性违规。VIRGA 在所有配对的仓储（warehouse）条件下均安全完成任务；在无重训练的情况下，长距离洞穴（cave）压力测试中在非规则、受限几何下仍保持持续协调。消融实验确认了在线几何评估、虚拟智能体中介和互惠反馈的贡献。

Conclusion: VIRGA 的主要贡献在于将空地双平台协调统一建模为有界黎曼场下的闭环问题，用虚拟智能体与互惠弹性反馈替代单侧主导控制，并通过平台感知映射保证指令可行与主动观测安全，在仓储与洞穴场景中同时满足安全性、可观测性和实时性。其局限可能在于：论文摘要未给出定量指标（如成功率、余隙、延迟数值），也未说明对动态障碍速度/密度的敏感度、LiDAR 退化或通信受限情况下的表现，以及虚拟智能体参数的可迁移性与跨场景泛化边界。此外"长距离洞穴压力测试"仅说明无需重训练，但未报告与基线的对比结果。

Abstract: Air-ground autonomy becomes harder when the unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) must remain observable by a gimbal light detection and ranging (LiDAR) mounted on the unmanned ground vehicle (UGV). The platforms must avoid dynamic obstacles while coordinating heterogeneous motion, limited sensing, and changing task initiative within one closed loop. This paper presents VIRGA, a neural geometric coordination framework that turns dual-LiDAR observations into bounded source-specific Riemannian fields and couples them through a virtual agent with reciprocal elastic feedback. Platform-aware execution maps convert the shared coordination reference into feasible UAV, UGV, and gimbal commands while enforcing active-observation safeguards. Evaluation against three complementary baselines reveals distinct limitations. An adapted Ray-RMP controller provides the fastest Riemannian response but produces insufficient clearance in the coupled air-ground task. A dense analytical Riemannian field improves geometric avoidance, yet its high evaluation cost prevents stable field-of-view maintenance. An adapted ColAG controller achieves the lowest latency but still incurs safety and observability violations. VIRGA completes all paired warehouse conditions safely, while a long-range cave stress test without retraining demonstrates sustained coordination in irregular and confined geometry. Ablations confirm contributions from online geometric evaluation, virtual-agent mediation, and reciprocal feedback.

</details>


### [170] [CommitFlow: Semantic Commitment Verification and Local Correction for Long-Horizon Robot Manipulation VLA Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21908)
*Zixiang Zhao,Yansong Feng,Yang Yang,Chaoyu Wang,Haoran Xiao,Hui Zhang,Chuang Cheng,Jianjun Ma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CommitFlow 是一个闭环执行框架，通过"语义承诺监控 + 局部修正"来缓解视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略中语义承诺、物理条件与实际物理状态之间的失配问题；在保持基础策略（π0.5）冻结的前提下，于 RoboTwin 2.0 的 10 个任务上取得 75.9% 的平均成功率，较基线提升 22.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时序机器人执行中，VLA 策略常会在某个任务阶段所要求的物理效应尚未真正建立或维持时，就推进到下一阶段。作者将这一现象定义为"语义承诺（semantic commitments）—阶段需建立或维持的物理条件—实际物理状态"三者之间的失配（mismatch）。由于单纯的动作指令无法确认该物理条件是否已满足，局部偏差会逐步传播并最终导致任务失败。该问题对机器人长时序可靠执行至关重要，是当前 VLA 策略从"语义合理"走向"物理可靠"的关键瓶颈。

Method: CommitFlow 是一个模块化闭环框架，基础策略保持冻结、不参与训练。其包含三个组件：(1) Semantic Commitment Monitor (SCM)：将阶段需求（stage requirements）与当前状态证据进行比对，当所需条件未满足或被违反时，抑制依赖该条件的后续动作；(2) BoundaryFlow：以当前状态与基础动作为条件，生成局部修正（local correction）；(3) Relation and Gain Calibration (RGC)：选择满足相关约束的最小修正强度，从而以最小干预实现条件满足。整体思路是将"承诺监控"与"局部修正"解耦并结合，避免对基础策略的重训练。

Result: 作者在 RoboTwin 2.0 的 10 个常见基准任务上进行评测。以 π0.5 为基础策略时，CommitFlow 取得 75.9% 的平均成功率，相比基础策略提升 22.7%。跨策略（cross-policy）实验显示增益具有一致性，表明该方法并不局限于某一特定基础策略，具备较好的通用性。评价指标为任务成功率。

Conclusion: 论文的主要贡献在于提出并形式化了 VLA 长时序执行中"语义承诺—物理条件—物理状态"失配这一问题，并给出一个无需重训基础策略、模块化的闭环监控与局部修正框架，在 RoboTwin 2.0 上显著提升成功率并跨策略泛化。可能局限在于：依赖阶段需求与状态证据的显式定义/建模，修正仍以局部校正为主，面对严重物理偏差或需求难以显式表述的任务时效果有待验证。

Abstract: Although vision-language-action (VLA) policies have advanced rapidly, long-horizon execution may still progress to the next task stage before the required physical effect has been established. We call this a mismatch between semantic commitments, physical conditions that a stage must establish or maintain, and the actual physical state. Because an action command alone cannot confirm such a condition, local deviations can propagate and cause task failure. To address this problem, we present CommitFlow, a closed-loop execution framework that combines commitment monitoring with local correction while keeping the base policy frozen. CommitFlow integrates three components. A Semantic Commitment Monitor (SCM) compares stage requirements against current state evidence and holds back dependent actions when a required condition is unmet or violated. BoundaryFlow then generates a local correction conditioned on the current state and base action, and Relation and Gain Calibration (RGC) selects the smallest correction strength that satisfies the relevant constraints. Across the ten common RoboTwin 2.0 benchmark tasks, CommitFlow achieves a mean success rate of 75.9 percent, improving on the base policy pi0.5 by 22.7 percent. Cross-policy experiments show consistent gains, pointing toward reliable long-horizon robot execution.

</details>


### [171] [MAAP: Multi-Agent Active Perception for Collaborative Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21929)
*Bruno N. Y. Chen,Li Kang,Heng Zhou,Xiufeng Song,Zhemeng Zhang,Jiahua Ma,Yiran Qin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出 MAAP（Multi-Agent Active Perception）：让每条机械臂在执行操作任务的同时，用其腕部相机充当团队的移动视点，从而把协作操作本身转化为主动感知机制；并配套提出 RAIL（Role-Aware Imitation Learning）控制器，在预测动作块的同时预测各臂的当前角色并以此条件化动作生成，将多视角主动感知与角色感知模仿学习统一在一个网络内。四类仿真任务中平均成功率从固定相机的 56.5% 提升到全腕视角的 70.0%，MAAP+RAIL 达 79.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体操作场景中天然存在多个任务驱动的视点：每条机械臂都搭载腕部相机，并在操作过程中于场景中移动。然而这些观测通常被低效利用；主动感知（active perception）在操作任务中往往被当作需要额外专用感知智能体的问题来处理，导致硬件与策略复杂度上升。论文的动机是：既然机械臂本身就在移动并携带相机，何不将"执行操作"与"提供视点"这两个功能合而为一，从而在不增加专责感知臂的前提下获得更丰富的多视角观测、缓解遮挡与视角受限问题。

Method: 核心方法包含两部分。(1) MAAP：每条臂为"双用途"（dual-purpose）智能体——同时承担操作动作执行与移动视点提供两种职能，团队所有腕部相机共同构成动态多视角感知体系，无需独立的感知智能体。(2) RAIL：一种角色感知模仿学习控制器，在每个决策步同时预测各臂的当前角色（role）与动作块（action chunk），并以预测出的角色作为条件来生成动作，从而在单一网络内表示"角色相关（role-dependent）"的动作。这种设计显式建模了多臂任务中角色随任务阶段发生切换的结构，使同一策略能够在不同角色间共享表示又保持行为区分。该方法建立在模仿学习（如 ACT 类动作分块）框架之上，将角色预测作为辅助监督信号与动作生成为联合任务。

Result: 在四个仿真任务上评估：固定相机平均成功率 56.5%，单一主动腕部视角 62.5%，全部腕部视角 70.0%，MAAP+RAIL 达 79.2%，表明"拓宽感知范式"（增加主动视角）本身即带来稳定增益，而 RAIL 进一步叠加提升。RAIL 的额外增益集中在三臂 Microwave 任务上：在完全相同的多腕视角输入下，成功率由 47% 提升至 82%，说明角色建模主要在多臂协作、角色切换频繁的场景中发挥作用。在双臂真实平台上，MAAP+RAIL 在 20 次放置试验中成功 14 次，而固定视角的 ACT 为 0/20，显示出显著的实机优势。指标为任务成功率或试验成功次数。

Conclusion: 论文的主要贡献在于提出一种新的视角：协作操作本身即可作为主动感知机制，将腕部相机由被动观测器变为主动感知资源，避免引入专用感知智能体；并通过 RAIL 的角色感知建模，验证了在多臂场景中显式建模角色对动作生成的必要性。结果说明多视角主动感知与角色建模具有互补性，且在双臂实机上对固定视角基线形成决定性优势。可能的局限包括：实验规模有限（四个仿真任务加一个双臂平台的小样本试验），真实平台仅评测单一放置任务且试验数较少（20 次），RAIL 的增益高度依赖任务中角色结构是否清晰（在非三臂 Microwave 任务上增益不明显），以及方法的泛化性与可扩展臂数尚未充分验证。

Abstract: Multi-agent manipulation naturally produces multiple task-driven viewpoints: every arm carries a wrist camera and moves through the scene while acting. Yet these observations are typically underutilized, and active perception in manipulation is still often treated as requiring a dedicated sensing agent. We introduce MAAP (Multi-Agent Active Perception), in which every arm is dual-purpose: it executes manipulation actions and, through the wrist camera it carries, simultaneously serves as a moving viewpoint for the team. We pair this with RAIL (Role-Aware Imitation Learning), a controller that predicts each arm's current role alongside its action chunk and conditions action generation on it, representing role-dependent actions within one network. Across four simulated tasks, widening the perception regime lifts average success from 56.5% with a fixed camera to 62.5% with one active wrist view and 70.0% with all of them, while MAAP+RAIL reaches 79.2%. RAIL's additional gain is concentrated on the three-arm Microwave task, where success rises from 47% to 82% on identical multi-wrist inputs. On a dual-arm platform, MAAP+RAIL succeeds in 14 of 20 placement trials compared with 0 of 20 for fixed-view ACT. Collaborative manipulation can thus serve as an active perception mechanism in its own right.

</details>


### [172] [When Should a Failing Robot Ask? Initiating Corrective Human-Robot Dialogue from Audited Sensor Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21942)
*Eshika Pathak,Leela Krishna*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文构建了一个"失败原因已知"的机器人纠错对话仿真基准，量化每种传感器对故障成因的可诊断性，并系统评测六个开源视觉-语言模型（VLM）在"自主诊断 / 查询其他机载传感器 / 向人类求助"三动作决策中的表现。核心发现是：VLM 的行为受提示表面形式驱动而非证据驱动，拒绝率随选项位置从78-100%骤降至0-6%；但把力觉数据以十行文本形式提供后，六个模型中有四个首次超过多数类基线，说明其失败主要源于传感器数据缺失而非能力不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人任务失败后的首个决策是：依据自身诊断行动、调用其他机载传感器、还是打断人类求助。要做出正确选择，机器人必须知道（1）各传感器能在多大程度上揭示失败成因，（2）自身诊断的可靠性有多高。然而现有 VLM 驱动的机器人系统普遍缺乏对这两点的量化度量，"是否求助"往往取决于模型的自信程度而非实测准确率与代价，导致在真实场景中可能频繁误求助或错误自主行动。论文旨在建立一个成因可控、可校验的数据泄漏基准，把这一决策形式化为可评估的三动作问题。

Method: 1) 仿真基准：在仿真环境中注入已知成因的失败，从而获得真实标签（ground truth cause），并显式设计数据泄漏检查以验证"某传感器是否真的携带该成因信息"。
2) 传感器可诊断性评测：对比相机图像与力觉（force）数据对失败成因的判别能力（力觉 0.99，图像方法均不超过 0.55）。
3) VLM 评测：测试六个开源 VLM，扫描提示变体（选项顺序、是否含示例、是否声明置信度），检验其诊断准确率与拒绝（求助）行为。
4) 传感器数据文本化：将力觉数据转写为十行文本输入同一批模型，验证"数据缺失 vs 能力缺失"的假设。
5) 决策形式化：将问题建模为三动作决策问题（act / consult sensors / ask human），其最优策略由实测诊断准确率推导得出，用于与模型实际行为对照。

Result: 实验显示：(1) 力觉数据对失败成因的可诊断性达 0.99，而所有基于图像的方法均不超过 0.55，说明存在传感器固有的可诊断性差异。(2) 三组模型/家族配对在把"拒绝"选项从答案列表末位移到首位后，拒绝率从 78-100% 塌缩至 0-6%，表明行为由提示表面而非证据驱动。(3) 在所有提示变体下（无论有无示例），基于帧的准确率均处于或低于多数类基线；模型自报置信度与正确性无相关性。(4) 将力觉数据以十行文本形式输入，六个模型中有四个首次获得超过基线的诊断结果，说明失败很大程度来源于传感器数据缺失。(5) 模型不遵循由实测准确率推出的最优策略，其求助率对"提问代价"的四倍变化不敏感；但只要向人类提一个问题，其表现即可从基线提升到接近回答者的可靠度（求助时为 0.70-0.81）。

Conclusion: 论文主要贡献在于：首次以成因可控的仿真基准量化机器人故障中各传感器的可诊断性，并揭示评测 VLM 时提示表面效应会严重扭曲其"求助/拒绝"行为结论；同时以三动作决策框架给出应基于实测准确率与代价、而非模型自信度来决定是否求助的原则。意义在于为机器人纠错对话中的传感器选择与人机交互策略提供了可复现的评估范式，并强调把非视觉传感器数据显式提供给 VLM 的价值。局限在于结论建立在仿真环境与已知注入成因之上，向真实机器人迁移时传感器噪声、成因分布与人类回答者可靠性可能不同；此外所测模型限于六个开源 VLM。

Abstract: A robot that fails at a task faces the first decision in corrective dialogue: act on its own diagnosis, consult another onboard sensor, or interrupt a person. Choosing well requires knowing how much the robot's sensors reveal about the cause and how reliable the robot's own diagnosis is. We build a simulated benchmark in which every failure's true cause is known, because we injected it, and measure what each sensor reveals, with explicit checks against data leakage. Some failures are diagnosable from camera images; others only from the robot's force data (0.99 from force data, no image method above 0.55). We then test six open vision-language models. Their behavior tracks the surface of the prompt, not the evidence: moving the refusal option from last to first in the answer list collapses refusal rates from 78-100% to 0-6% in three of the six swept model-and-family pairs. Accuracy from frames stays at or below a majority-class baseline under every prompt variant, with or without worked examples, and stated confidence carries no information about correctness. Handing the same models the force data as ten lines of text produces the first above-baseline diagnoses, in four of the six models: much of the failure reflects missing sensor data, not missing ability. We pose the choice as a three-action decision problem, act, consult your own sensors, or ask a human, whose optimal policy follows from measured accuracy. The models do not follow it, and their ask rates ignore a fourfold change in question cost. One question to a human still lifts them from that baseline to roughly the answerer's own reliability (0.70-0.81 when they ask). The decision to ask should be tied to measured accuracy and stated costs, not to the model's confidence.

</details>


### [173] [CARF: Contrastive Attraction-Repulsion of Failure-Guided Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21982)
*Shuqi Zhao,Bang Du,Cheng-En Wu,Yichen Xie,Yixiao Wang,Masayoshi Tomizuka*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CARF 提出一种基于"对比式吸引-排斥"的不完美机器人数据学习框架：用仅在成功示范及其扰动上训练的进展评分器，将失败轨迹区分为"有进展段"和"失败关键段"，在统一的流匹配目标中分别施加吸引（模仿）与排斥（规避）的监督信号，并排除模糊段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人示范采集不可避免地包含失败或不完美轨迹，直接丢弃会浪费数据，而现有利用失败数据的方法多只挖掘其中仍向任务完成推进的片段，忽略了"直接导致任务失败"的关键行为。作者认为这两类片段提供的是非对称监督信息：有进展段应被模仿，失败关键段应被显式规避；若不加区分地学习，会引入不可靠甚至有害的监督。因此需要一个能同时利用两类信息、并回避模糊区域的策略学习框架。

Method: CARF（Contrastive Attraction-Repulsion of Failure-guided framework）的核心包含两部分：一是基于进展的重要性评分器（progress-based importance scorer），仅使用成功专家示范及其扰动数据训练，从而估计每一步对任务完成的贡献，并据此在失败轨迹中定位信息量高的区域，同时识别模糊段；二是统一的流匹配（flow-matching）策略学习目标，在评分引导下对轨迹施加两种方向相反的监督：吸引策略趋向有进展行为、排斥策略远离失败关键行为，并显式排除模糊段。这种将"模仿"与"规避"统一到一个对比式目标中的设计，是相对现有仅做进展片段筛选方法的主要技术差异。

Result: 论文在仿真环境和真实世界中均进行了大量实验，覆盖多种失败场景，结果显示 CARF 相较竞争基线取得一致性的性能提升；消融实验进一步验证了所提出的进展评分机制与吸引-排斥机制各自的有效性。由于摘要未给出具体数据集名称、评测指标数值及实验规模，需以正文数据为准。

Conclusion: CARF 的主要贡献在于重新定义了不完美机器人数据中"有进展段"与"失败关键段"的非对称监督关系，并给出可落地的评分器加流匹配对比目标实现，从而更充分地利用失败轨迹、避免模糊失败段的不可靠监督，在仿真与真实场景中均优于基线。潜在局限在于：评分器依赖成功示范及其扰动的分布，其泛化性可能受限于与失败场景的分布差异；对失败关键段的排斥强度与模糊段判定阈值等超参数可能影响稳定性，且摘要未给出定量结果与失败场景覆盖范围的细节。

Abstract: Robot demonstration collection often produces imperfect or failed trajectories in addition to successful demonstrations. Existing methods typically exploit failed trajectories by identifying segments that still make progress toward task completion, but largely overlook \textit{failure-critical behaviors} that directly lead to task failure. Here we argue that these two types of segments provide fundamentally asymmetric supervision: progressive segments should be imitated, whereas failure-critical segments should be explicitly avoided. Based on this observation, we propose CARF, a Contrastive Attraction-Repulsion of Failure-guided framework for learning from imperfect robot data. CARF introduces a progress-based importance scorer, trained solely on successful expert demonstrations and its perturbation results, to estimate step-wise contributions toward task completion and identify informative regions in failed trajectories. These scores guide a unified flow-matching objective that attracts the policy toward progressive behaviors and repels it from failure-critical ones, while excluding ambiguous segments. This enables more comprehensive utilization of imperfect data and avoids unreliable supervision from ambiguous failure segments. Extensive experiments in simulation and the real world demonstrate consistent improvements over competing baselines across diverse failure scenarios, with ablations further validating the effectiveness of the proposed scoring and attraction-repulsion mechanisms. Our website is https://zhao-sq.github.io/carf/#.

</details>


### [174] [SkelWAM: A Skeleton-Guided World-Action Model for Zero-Shot Cross-Embodiment Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21983)
*Pengjun Niu,Yujia Xie,Rui Peng,Hang Zhao,Ke Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SkelWAM 提出一种骨架引导的"世界-动作"模型，用统一的显式几何表征（手臂中心线几何 + TCP 位姿 + 平行夹爪指令，共 25 维）架起感知与控制的桥梁，实现单源跨本体（cross-embodiment）操作迁移，无需关节一一对应、也不需要目标本体的示范数据或策略微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复用操作经验到不同机器人本体是扩展机器人学习、减少重复任务数据采集的关键，但本体变化会改变视觉外观、动作维度与语义，以及能够实现同一工具位姿的全身构型。已有的跨本体方法通常依赖关节空间对应关系，或需要在目标本体上做示范/策略更新，这限制了可扩展性与迁移范围。作者希望用一套与本体的形态无关、但保持几何一致性的共享表征，使策略可以在视觉与不同运动学结构（如关节型机械臂与连续体机器人）之间迁移。

Method: 核心是构造一个跨本体共享的 25 维动作/状态空间：手臂中心线几何、工具中心点（TCP）位姿、平行夹爪指令；同一套定义同时用于规范化的第三人称与腕部视角观测，以及未来的全身动作目标。模型采用 video-action mixture of transformers（视频-动作混合 Transformer），通过预测性视觉监督训练，输出"规范化骨架动作块"（canonical skeleton action chunks）。随后由本体特定的受约束解码器（constrained decoder）将这些骨架动作块转换为关节控制或连续体机器人控制。该形式无需本体之间的关节一一对应，且不需要目标任务的示范数据或目标策略更新。

Result: 作者提出 LIBERO-Cross10 基准，这是一个仅含源数据的跨本体迁移评测，覆盖 10 个任务与分布于 4 个形态组的 10 个目标本体。在 Franka 上训练的策略在该基准 1000 个 episode 上取得 43.3% 成功率，比表现最好的被评测基线高出 36.2 个百分点。此外，作者将 JAKA mini2 上训练的策略部署到 Feagine A03 连续体机器人上完成三项桌面操作任务，验证了真实世界跨本体迁移的潜力。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于：以显式几何（手臂中心线、TCP 位姿、夹爪命令）作为共享的感知-控制接口，配合视频-动作混合 Transformer 与本体特定约束解码器，实现无关节对应、无目标示范的无监督源→目标跨本体迁移；并提出 LIBERO-Cross10 源-目标跨本体基准及真实连续体机器人部署示例。其意义在于为规模化机器人学习提供了与本体现形态解耦的可迁移表征。潜在限制包括：成功率为 43.3%，绝对性能仍有限；共享 25 维状态对本体形态假设（手臂中心线、平行夹爪）依赖较强，对非平行/灵巧手或高度异构本体可能不适用；解码器仍需为本体分别设计；且在受限基准与少量真实任务上评估，泛化性有待更广泛验证。

Abstract: Reusing manipulation experience across robot embodiments is important for scaling robot learning and reducing repeated task-specific data collection. However, changes in embodiment alter visual appearance, action dimensionality and semantics, and the whole-body configurations that can realize the same tool pose. We present SkelWAM, a skeleton-guided world-action model that couples perception and control through one explicit geometric representation for single-source cross-embodiment manipulation. Arm centerline geometry, tool-center-point (TCP) pose, and parallel-jaw commands form a shared 25-D state. The same definition underlies canonical third-person and wrist observations and future whole-body action targets. Trained with predictive visual supervision, a video-action mixture of transformers predicts canonical skeleton action chunks, which embodiment-specific constrained decoders convert into joint or continuum-robot controls. This formulation requires no one-to-one joint correspondence and uses no target-task demonstrations or target policy updates. We introduce LIBERO-Cross10, a source-only cross-embodiment transfer benchmark covering ten tasks and ten target embodiments across four morphological groups. On this benchmark, Franka-trained SkelWAM achieves 43.3% success over 1,000 episodes, exceeding the best-performing evaluated baseline by 36.2 percentage points. We further deploy a JAKA mini2-trained policy on the Feagine A03 continuum robot for three tabletop manipulation tasks, illustrating the approach's potential for real-world cross-embodiment manipulation. Project page: http://www.liukepku.com/skelwam/index.html

</details>


### [175] [Gripper-Aware Automatic Dense Packing of Irregular Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22062)
*Tianhao Qin,Connor McCann,Berk Calli,Jing Xiao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一个面向真实机械臂的闭环密集不规则物体装箱流水线，将感知、考虑夹爪的放置优化与力控执行集成在一起，在3D打印异形物体与YCB子集上验证可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仓储作业中自动密集装箱需求广泛，但仍是机器人操作的基础性难题。现有不规则物体装箱研究多在仿真中开展，依赖理想化接触假设，把物体视为孤立刚体；抓取夹爪通常只作为离散的事后可行性检查，甚至完全不被考虑；同时执行过程中感知误差与接触漂移不断累积而未被处理。论文的动机是弥合仿真装箱与真实机械臂执行之间的差距，使优化与执行形成闭环。

Method: 核心是闭环流水线，含三个环节。(1) 夹爪感知的放置优化（gripper-aware placement optimization）：把被抓物体与夹爪建模为单一"复合体"，用层次化球体树（hierarchical sphere trees）表示，在GPU上以CMA-ES框架搜索五个自由度，竖直方向坐标由当前高度图（heightmap）解析地接地确定。(2) 力引导执行（force-guided execution）：执行一次受力监测的竖直下降，在首次接触时停止，避免刚性碰撞。(3) 释放后整合推挤（post-release consolidation push）：弥补夹爪感知规划残留的横向间隙。每次放置之间重新感知容器，以抑制漂移累积。此外方法使用从网格导出的几何信息。

Result: 在Franka Emika Panda机械臂上验证，装箱对象为3D打印的平坦、弯曲与凹形物体集合，以及YCB物体子集。消融实验分别隔离了夹爪感知优化、整合推挤与网格导出几何对端到端成功率、达成密度和计算代价的贡献。并与高度图最小化（heightmap-minimization）方法作为先前不规则物体装箱工作的代表性基线进行基准对比。

Conclusion: 主要贡献是将夹爪建模纳入放置优化、力控执行与重感知闭环统一到真实机械臂的不规则物体密集装箱系统，并通过消融与基线对比量化各组件作用。潜在局限：摘要未报告具体成功率与密度数值；竖直自由度依赖高度图解析接地，可能受容器形状与感知精度限制；五自由度搜索配合CMA-ES与球体树表示的实时性在更大规模物体集合下的表现尚待评估。

Abstract: Automatic dense packing is widely desired in warehouse operations but remains a fundamental challenge in robotic manipulation. Existing work on irregular-object packing largely targets simulation with idealized contact, treating the object as an isolated rigid body. The gripper often enters as a discrete, post-hoc feasibility check, if considered at all, and the perception and contact drift accumulated during execution are not addressed. We present a closed-loop pipeline that integrates perception, gripper-aware placement optimization, and force-guided execution on a real manipulator. The optimizer represents the object together with the gripper as a single composite body of hierarchical sphere trees. It searches over five degrees of freedom on a GPU within a CMA-ES framework, with the vertical coordinate grounded analytically against the current heightmap. During execution, a force-monitored vertical descent stops on first contact. A post-release consolidation push then closes the residual lateral clearance that gripper-aware planning leaves behind. The container is re-perceived between placements so that drift does not accumulate. We validate the system on a Franka Emika Panda robot packing a 3D-printed set of flat, curved, and concave objects, and a YCB object subset. An ablation study isolates the contribution of gripper-aware optimization, the consolidation push, and mesh-derived geometry to end-to-end success, achieved density, and computational cost. We further benchmark against the heightmap-minimization method as a baseline representative of prior irregular-object packing work.

</details>


### [176] [Duty Factor Predicts Robust Constrained Quadrupedal Locomotion Across Gait Types](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22073)
*James Zhu,David Ologan,George Ortiz,Thomas Chun Fai Lee,Selvin Garcia Gonzalez,Ardalan Tajbakhsh,Pinhas Ben-Tzvi,Aaron M. Johnson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文研究四足机器人步态参数（尤其是占空比 duty factor）与运动鲁棒性之间的关系，跨越三种不同控制架构（全身轨迹优化+LQR、学习型运动控制器、质心模型预测控制），并通过实体机器人在狭窄地形上的实验验证。核心结论：占空比比标称步态类型（如行走/小跑）更能预测局部误差收敛与运动鲁棒性，可作为选择和调节鲁棒运动策略的低维参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四足机器人越来越多地部署在需要运动鲁棒应对扰动和受限地形的环境中。步态类型（如行走 walking、小跑 trotting）常被用作表征四足运动的标签，但步态类型并不能唯一确定运动行为——同一步态类型下，占空比、速度、站立宽度等参数仍可变化。这意味着以步态类型作为分析或设计单位可能掩盖真正影响鲁棒性的关键因素。论文旨在揭示这些步态参数之间的关系，并寻找一个更简单、更具预测力的低维参数来理解和选择鲁棒运动策略，尤其在狭窄/受限地形这一挑战性场景下。

Method: 论文从三个互补的控制架构出发研究步态参数关系：(1) 使用全身轨迹优化配合 LQR 反馈，分析占空比与局部误差收敛的关系；(2) 使用学习型运动控制器研究占空比的选择，探讨占空比如何作为适应狭窄地形鲁棒性的低维参数；(3) 在质心模型预测控制（centroidal MPC）框架下验证上述趋势是否持续存在。最后在实体四足机器人上进行狭窄地形实验以验证仿真与控制器层面的发现。研究聚焦占空比、速度、站立宽度等参数，而非单纯的标称步态类型划分。

Result: 实验涵盖三种控制方法（轨迹优化+LQR、学习型控制器、质心 MPC）与实体四足机器人。主要发现：占空比比标称步态类型更能预测局部误差收敛；占空比可作为低维参数用于在狭窄地形环境中调节运动鲁棒性；上述趋势在不同控制架构间保持一致，并在实体机器人的狭窄地形实验中得到验证。评估侧重局部误差收敛与狭窄地形下的鲁棒性表现，而非标准的速度跟踪或能耗基准。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于：识别出占空比是比步态类型更有效的鲁棒性预测与设计参数，并在三种不同控制架构和实体平台上验证其一致性，为理解与选择四足鲁棒运动提供了简单而通用的基础。意义在于提供了一种跨步态、跨控制架构的低维调节视角。潜在局限：研究主要围绕占空比及相关步态参数，未显式涉及感知、事件相机或视觉信息；狭窄地形实验规模与指标细节在摘要层面未充分展开；结论在不同硬件与更复杂动态环境中的泛化性仍需进一步验证。该工作与实时机器人运动控制、受限地形下的鲁棒运动相关，但不直接属于事件视觉、脉冲神经网络或小目标检测等方向。

Abstract: Quadrupedal robots are increasingly deployed in environments where locomotion must remain robust to disturbances and constrained terrain. Gait type, such as walking or trotting, is commonly used to characterize quadrupedal locomotion. However, gait type does not uniquely define locomotion, as parameters such as duty factor, speed, and stance width can vary within a single gait type. In this work, we investigate the relationship between these gait parameters using three distinct quadrupedal locomotion control approaches. First, using whole body trajectory optimization with LQR feedback, we show that duty factor is a stronger predictor of local error convergence than nominal gait type. Second, we investigate duty factor selection with a learned locomotion controller, suggesting how duty factor may serve as a low-dimensional parameter for adapting locomotion robustness in narrow-terrain environments. Finally, we show that these trends persist under a centroidal model predictive control framework and validate them through narrow-terrain experiments on a physical quadruped. These results show that duty factor provides a simple and effective basis for understanding and selecting robust quadrupedal locomotion across gait types and control architectures.

</details>


### [177] [LIMBO: Learning and Internalizing Model-Free Barrier Objectives for Agile and Safe Whole-Body Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22075)
*Jake Gonzales,Arturo Flores Alvarez,Yu-Ming Chen,Aaron D. Ames,Lillian J. Ratliff,Manikantan Nambi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LIMBO提出了一种通过学习状态-动作控制屏障函数（Q-CBF）并将其安全结构蒸馏到任务策略中的框架，在29自由度人形机器人上实现了无在线安全滤波器的全身避碰与平衡控制，并发现风险引导的边界采样可探索"可恢复性边界"，涌现出从下蹲到后仰limbo动作的多样策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全身控制（whole-body control）需要在高度非线性、高维动力学下同时协调避碰与平衡，这使得安全证书（safety certificate）的设计与跨行为复用极为困难。控制屏障函数（CBF）虽能提供形式化安全保证，但传统方法依赖已知动力学模型或对安全集做人为假设，难以扩展至高维全身控制。本文的动机在于：如何在黑箱转移（black-box transitions）条件下、在完整控制维度上合成可用的安全证书，并将其安全结构内化到任务策略中，从而在部署时无需在线安全滤波器（online safety filter）即可保持安全性，同时兼顾敏捷行为。

Method: LIMBO的核心是在冻结的基控制器（frozen base controller）周围的残差动作空间上学习Q-CBF。具体地：(1) 以黑箱系统转移和基于状态的失败规范（failure specification）为输入，从残差动作学习状态-动作安全值函数，使Q-CBF合成在完整控制维度上可行，同时将证书置于任务策略的控制空间中；(2) 合成阶段，学习到的安全值驱动风险引导采样（risk-guided sampling），使探索集中在估计的"可恢复性边界"附近；(3) 任务学习阶段，安全值作为教师提供动作级安全反馈，直接塑造任务策略，从而产生鲁棒策略并消除部署时对在线安全滤波的需求。此外，作者论证了风险引导边界采样是一种有理论依据的可恢复性边界探索方式，采样集中度（sampling concentration）作为超参数会系统性地影响最终策略的形态。

Result: 在29自由度（29-DoF）人形机器人上评估，任务包括躲球（dodgeball avoidance）和低障碍物下行走/穿越（locomotion beneath low obstacles）。在相同安全规范、其他条件不变的前提下，改变采样集中度可产生从下蹲（crouching）到新颖的后仰limbo动作等多种策略。所学策略在两个场景中均无需在线安全滤波即可迁移到真实硬件，表明学习式安全合成可扩展至敏捷全身控制。

Conclusion: 主要贡献包括：(1) 将学习式Q-CBF扩展到高维全身控制（29 DoF），通过在残差动作空间合成并蒸馏到任务策略；(2) 提出风险引导边界采样作为探索可恢复性边界的有理论依据的方法，并展示采样集中度可调控行为形态；(3) 在真实硬件上实现无在线安全滤波的敏捷全身行为迁移。局限性方面：方法依赖已有的基控制器（残差动作），安全保证基于从黑箱数据学习的证书而非解析证明；实验仅在特定人形平台与两类任务上验证，泛化到其他形态与任务尚待检验。

Abstract: Safe whole-body control requires coordinating collision avoidance and balance under high-dimensional, nonlinear dynamics--making safety certificates difficult to design and reuse across behaviors. We present LIMBO, a framework for synthesizing a state-action control barrier function and distilling its safety structure into a task policy. LIMBO learns the safety certificate from black-box transitions and a state-based failure specification over residual actions around a frozen base controller, making Q-CBF synthesis tractable in the full control dimension while placing the certificate in the task policy's control space. During synthesis, the learned safety value drives risk-guided sampling near the estimated boundary of recoverability; during task learning, it serves as a teacher that provides action-level safety feedback, yielding a robust task policy and alleviating the need for an online safety filter at deployment. We demonstrate LIMBO on a 29-degree-of-freedom humanoid performing dodgeball avoidance and locomotion beneath low obstacles. Beyond scaling learned Q-CBFs to whole-body control, we show that risk-guided boundary sampling provides a theoretically grounded way to explore the edge of recoverability. Under the same safety specification, ceteris paribus, varying the sampling concentration produces strategies ranging from crouching to a novel backward-leaning limbo maneuver. In both settings, the learned policies transfer to hardware without online safety filtering, showing that learned safety synthesis scales to agile whole-body control.

</details>


### [178] [SeeQ: Training Generalist Value Functions for Long-Horizon Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22085)
*Saksham Singh,Zheyuan Hu,Max Sobol Mark,Jeffrey Yu,Zackory Erickson,Aviral Kumar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SeeQ（Subtask-elicited Q-functions）提出一种面向当前活跃子任务（subtask）的价值函数学习框架，而非直接学习任务级别的 Q 值。该框架利用离线机器人数据中已有的子任务标注，配合时序差分（TD）目标学习短视界价值函数，并在测试时通过自回归方式用自然语言先预测当前子任务再估计其价值，无需人工标注或独立的子任务预测模块。SeeQ 基于视觉-语言主干网络，先在多源开源机器人操作数据上预训练，再在下游任务上微调，在两种双臂机器人平台的四个真实操作任务上显著提升了 best-of-N 策略引导效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用机器人策略（generalist robot policies）在复杂、长时序（long-horizon）任务上仍然脆弱，这类任务往往包含多个阶段，或需要在同一阶段反复尝试与推理才能成功。Q 值函数可以通过对候选动作排序或引导策略改进来增强策略，但从稀疏的任务级奖励中学习 Q 值面临三大困难：(1) 信用分配（credit assignment）时间跨度极长；(2) Bellman 备份（Bellman backup）难以有效进行；(3) 对数据覆盖范围（data coverage）要求很高。这些因素导致传统任务级价值函数在长时序操作任务上学习效率低、泛化性差，因而需要一种能够缩短价值预测视界、更易学习且能利用大规模（可能次优）离线数据的替代方案。

Method: 核心思想是将价值学习从任务级（task-level）下沉到子任务级（subtask-level），从而缩短价值预测的时间视界，使得 TD 目标能够有效学习。训练阶段：利用离线机器人数据中天然存在的子任务级标注构建任务分解，因此可以从广泛但可能次优的机器人数据集中学习，而不依赖任务级稀疏奖励。测试阶段：为消除对人工标注或模块化子任务预测系统的依赖，SeeQ 的价值函数架构被设计为自回归地先用自然语言预测当前活跃子任务，再估计该子任务的价值。模型实现上采用视觉-语言主干网络（vision-language backbone）作为基础，先在大规模开源机器人操作数据上进行预训练，再针对下游任务微调。整体是一个统一的、子任务驱动的 Q 函数，兼具子任务识别与价值估计能力。

Result: 在实验设置上，作者在两种双臂（bimanual）机器人平台、四个真实世界操作任务上评估 SeeQ。评估方式为 best-of-N 策略引导（即用价值函数在 N 个候选动作中筛选最优动作）。主要结果表明，SeeQ 价值函数显著提升了 best-of-N 的策略引导效果，验证了子任务级价值学习在长时序操作任务上的有效性，也说明基于离线子任务标注的 TD 学习配合视觉-语言主干的预训练+微调范式可以泛化到真实机器人平台。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于提出 SeeQ 这一子任务驱动的 Q 函数学习框架，通过将价值预测视界从任务级缩短到子任务级，缓解了稀疏奖励下的信用分配难题，并使 TD 学习在大规模可能次优的离线数据上变得可行；同时通过自回归自然语言子任务预测，消除了测试时对人工标注和独立子任务模块的需求。意义在于为长时序机器人操作中的价值函数学习与策略引导提供了一种可扩展、数据驱动的范式。潜在局限包括：方法依赖训练数据中存在子任务级标注（尽管测试时不需要）、子任务分解粒度可能影响性能、实验仅在四个真实任务与两种双臂平台上验证，向更广泛任务和单臂/移动平台的泛化性尚待检验，且 best-of-N 引导本身受限于候选动作集合的质量与多样性。

Abstract: Despite rapid progress, generalist robot policies remain brittle on complex, long-horizon tasks that comprise multiple stages or require repeated attempts and deliberation on the same underlying stage before success. Q-value functions can improve these policies by ranking candidate actions or guiding policy improvement, but learning from sparse task-level rewards entails long credit-assignment horizons, difficult Bellman backups, and broad data-coverage requirements. We introduce SeeQ (Subtask-elicited Q-functions), which instead learns Q-values for the currently active subtask. This shortens the value-prediction horizon and enables effective learning with temporal-difference (TD) objectives. During training, subtask-level annotations present in offline robot data provide the decomposition and enable learning from broad, potentially suboptimal robot datasets. To eliminate the need for human annotations or modular subtask prediction systems at test time, our Q-function architecture is trained to autoregressively predict the active subtask in natural language before estimating its value. We instantiate SeeQ using a base vision-language backbone, pretrain it on diverse open-source robot manipulation data, and finetune it on downstream tasks. Across four real-world manipulation tasks on two bimanual robot platforms, the SeeQ value function substantially improves best-of-N policy steering.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [179] [Sparse Priors for Efficient Distribution Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20883)
*Saumya Goyal,Barnabás Póczos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出"稀疏先验"(sparse priors)与"稀疏维度"(Sparse Dimension)概念，证明在此类先验下的分布学习可将样本复杂度从随维度 d 指数退化的 O(n^{-1/Θ(d)}) 改善为 Ω(√(k/n)) 的贝叶斯风险下界，并给出 TV 距离下匹配的上界，表明合适先验可克服对 n 的维度诅咒，且结果同样适用于"学习采样"问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前生成式AI（如扩散模型、GAN等）虽广泛成功，但分布学习的理论基础受制于维度诅咒：从 d 维分布中学习，理论上界与下界均退化为 O(n^{-1/Θ(d)})，意味着所需样本量随维度指数增长。尽管该速率已被证明是极小极大(minimax)最优的，作者认为这一结论过于悲观——因为仅靠光滑性(smoothness)假设不足以刻画实际应用中分布所具备的结构性。因此需要引入更能反映真实数据结构的先验假设，以获得维度无关或弱依赖维度的学习保证。

Method: 作者定义了一类新的先验——稀疏先验(sparse priors)，并提出"稀疏维度"(Sparse Dimension) k 作为衡量分布空间上先验稀疏程度的度量。在 k-稀疏先验假设下，作者通过贝叶斯风险分析框架推导分布学习的信息论下界，得到 Ω(√(k/n)) 的风险下界（在常见距离度量下成立）。进一步，在温和的附加假设下，针对全变差(TV)距离给出了与下界匹配的上界（在 n、k 渐近意义下至多相差对数项）。此外，作者证明了在贝叶斯框架下分布学习与"学习采样"(learning to sample)在统计意义上等价，从而将结论推广至采样任务。

Result: 论文以理论分析为主，核心结果包括：(1) k-稀疏先验下分布学习的贝叶斯风险下界为 Ω(√(k/n))，该下界不显式依赖环境维度 d；(2) 在 TV 距离与温和假设下给出匹配的（至多对数因子）上界；(3) 证明分布学习与学习采样在贝叶斯设定下的统计等价性。结果表明，当 k 不依赖于（或弱依赖于）维度 d 及内在维度时，学习速率对样本数 n 的依赖可摆脱维度诅咒。论文未报告具体数据集实验，属于纯理论工作。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于提出稀疏维度这一新度量并构建稀疏先验学习理论，证明合理先验可将分布学习与采样任务从指数维度诅咒中解放，使样本复杂度仅依赖稀疏维度 k。其意义在于为生成式建模的实际可行性提供理论解释，并连接了分布学习与采样学习两个问题。局限包括：上界依赖"温和附加假设"且仅对 TV 距离成立、仍含对数松弛项；k 的现实可估计性与先验构造的可行性未充分讨论；结果尚未经实证验证。

Abstract: Despite the widespread use and success of generative AI techniques today, theoretical guarantees on learning a distribution supported in $d$ dimensions from $n$ samples degrade as $O(n^{-1/Θ(d)})$, though shown to be minimax optimal. We hypothesize that present bounds are too pessimistic because smoothness assumptions are not enough to capture the structure of distributions that often appear in real applications. Consequently, we introduce the class of sparse priors and define the "Sparse Dimension" as a measure of sparsity of a prior over the space of all distributions. We show that distribution learning under a $k$-sparse prior achieves a Bayesian risk lower bound of $Ω(\sqrt{k/n})$ under common distance metrics, and show a matching (up to logarithmic terms asymptotically in $n,k$) upper bound for the TV distance under mild additional assumptions. We show the statistical equivalence of distribution learning and learning to sample in the Bayesian setting so that our results apply to learning to sample as well. While $k$ can still depend on the dimension $d$, or a notion of intrinsic dimension, our results show that learning under an appropriate prior overcomes the curse of dimensionality with respect to the dependence on $n$.

</details>


### [180] [BI-Agent and BI-Bench: Towards Automating End-to-End Business Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20886)
*Chuxuan Hu,Yeye He,Penny Zhou,Wee Hyong Tok,Daniel Kang,Surajit Chaudhuri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出首个面向端到端商业智能（BI）的基准 BI-Bench，并设计工具增强智能体 BI-Agent 及配套的后训练框架（SFT+RL），使 LLM 无需用户手动准备数据即可直接回答 BI 问题，准确率最高提升 40 个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 BI 工作流（如 Power BI、Tableau）要求用户先完成一系列繁琐的数据准备步骤：(1) 识别相关数据表，(2) 执行数据变换，(3) 构建连接（join）关系，之后才能 (4) 回答业务问题。这一过程复杂且耗时，是企业用户使用 BI 的核心障碍。尽管大语言模型在数据任务上表现出较强能力，但其能否端到端地完成整个 BI 流程此前缺乏系统性研究。作者希望探究 LLM 能否替代用户完成这些准备步骤，从而降低 BI 使用门槛。

Method: 方法包含三个部分：(1) BI-Bench 基准——从公开渠道收集大量真实企业 BI 项目，并从真实用户仪表盘中人工抽取（问题，标准答案）对，系统性评估 LLM 的端到端 BI 能力；(2) 工具增强智能体 BI-Agent——将 BI 工作流分解为面向结构化数据的子任务（如搜索 search、连接 join、变换 transform），并跨 BI 各阶段编排专门的数据管理方法，实现工具增强推理；(3) 后训练框架——从真实 BI 项目中合成训练轨迹，对 BI-Agent 依次进行监督微调（SFT）与强化学习（RL），以适配复杂 BI 工作流。

Result: 在 BI-Bench 上，即便前沿 LLM 表现也很差，准确率不足 50%。引入工具增强后，BI-Agent 在原始 LLM 上取得最高 40 个百分点的准确率提升；经 SFT+RL 后训练的 BI-Agent 进一步获得最高 30 个百分点的增益。结果表明显式工具编排推理与领域特定后训练相结合对复杂 BI 流程至关重要。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于：首次构建端到端 BI 评测基准 BI-Bench，揭示前沿 LLM 在此任务上的严重不足；提出工具增强的 BI-Agent，将 BI 工作流分解为可编排的数据管理子任务；并设计基于真实项目轨迹合成的 SFT+RL 后训练框架。其意义在于展示了工具增强推理与领域后训练结合的潜力。潜在局限：基准依赖人工从真实仪表盘抽取问答对，规模与领域覆盖可能受限；后训练轨迹由合成生成，与真实分布可能存在偏差；智能体性能仍依赖底层 LLM 能力，端到端准确率仍有较大提升空间。

Abstract: Business intelligence (BI) is a cornerstone of enterprise decision-making and is widely used by enterprise users in software such as Power BI and Tableau. In traditional BI workflows, users need to prepare data by (1) identifying relevant tables, (2) performing data transformations, and (3) building join relationships, before they can (4) answer their business questions. These steps can be complex and time-consuming, making BI challenging.
  Given the strong capabilities of large language models (LLMs) in working with data, we study their ability to answer BI questions end-to-end, without requiring users to manually perform the tedious preparation steps. To do this, we harvest a large collection of real-world BI projects from public sources, and manually extract pairs of (questions, ground-truth answers) from real user dashboards. The resulting benchmark, BI-Bench, is the first benchmark to systematically study LLMs' ability on end-to-end BI.
  We find that even frontier LLMs perform poorly on BI-Bench, with less than 50% accuracy. To address their limitations, we design a tool-augmented BI-Agent that decomposes BI workflows into subtasks on structured data, such as search, join, and transform, and orchestrates specialized data management methods across BI stages. Furthermore, we develop a post-training framework that synthesizes training trajectories from real BI projects, enabling BI-Agent to be further post-trained using both supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and reinforcement learning (RL). BI-Agent achieves substantial accuracy gains of up to 40 percentage points with vanilla LLMs, and post-trained BI-Agent yields gains of up to 30 points. Our results highlight the importance of combining tool-augmented reasoning with domain-specific post-training in complex BI workflows, and point to promising directions for future research.

</details>


### [181] [Elastic Threshold Attention: Learned Contextual Sparsity for Long-Context Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20888)
*Themistoklis Haris,Henry Li,Maryam Karimzadehgan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 Elastic Threshold Attention (ETA)，一种端到端可训练的稀疏注意力架构：从 query 表示中动态预测上下文相关阈值，在训练时以"乘性抑制"方式将低于阈值的 logits 平滑压向零而非直接删除，从而在约 85% 训练稀疏度下保持稠密模型质量，并在推理时通过 Triton 定制 kernel 实现最高 2.5× 的解码加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文解码中，大规模 KV cache 造成严重的内存带宽瓶颈。稀疏注意力通过选择性加载缓解该问题，但现有方法依赖刚性启发式规则，会丢弃必要上下文，导致质量下降。核心矛盾在于：既要硬件友好的稀疏计算，又不能牺牲稠密模型的精度。作者希望找到一种可训练、可自适应分配上下文的稀疏策略，使简单步骤走稀疏路径、困难检索或推理步骤仍能获得接近稠密的上下文。

Method: 1) 动态阈值预测：ETA 直接从 query 表示预测动态、上下文相关的阈值，实现按需分配上下文的弹性稀疏策略。2) 训练机制：为避免从头训练时的表示坍缩，对低于阈值的 logits 采用乘性抑制（multiplicatively suppress）而非删除，使其平滑趋近于零；这一"均匀注意力下界"充当分布式概率蓄水池，并报告了初始 token 上的局部注意力汇（attention sink）随之消失。3) 推理机制：基于该训练特性，模型可在推理时硬剪枝无信息 KV 块，并吸收由粗粒度 GPU 块选择误引入的附带 token。4) 定制 Triton 解码 kernel：利用缓存的几何-概率界，在 O(1) 时间内筛选 KV 块。5) 离线校准算法：用于领域特定部署，冻结每头（per-head）的常数阈值以消除预测器开销。

Result: 以 1.45B 预训练模型为对象，在约 85% 训练稀疏度、约 38% 解码活跃密度下，ETA 在语言建模、常识推理和长上下文"大海捞针"检索任务上可与稠密注意力相媲美。推理阶段，基于缓存的几何-概率界在 O(1) 内完成 KV 块筛选，相比 FlashAttention-2 在最长 512K token 序列上实现最高 2.5× 的实际解码墙钟时间加速。此外，离线校准通过冻结每头常数阈值消除预测器开销，进一步降低 27% 的注意力计算量。

Conclusion: ETA 的主要贡献在于：将稀疏注意力的阈值从刚性启发式转变为可端到端学习的动态策略，并用乘性抑制的平滑训练目标解决了稀疏训练中的表示坍缩与注意力汇问题，从而在保持稠密质量的同时获得实际的硬件加速；配套的 Triton kernel 与离线校准进一步提升了工程可用性。局限方面：摘要未给出与更多稀疏注意力基线的直接对比，未报告校准后质量损失的具体幅度，固定阈值部署的泛化性以及 512K 以外的更极端长度表现仍待验证。

Abstract: Massive KV caches can cause severe memory-bandwidth bottlenecks during long-context decoding. Sparse attention methods mitigate this via selective loading, but that comes at a cost: rigid heuristics drop necessary context, leading to quality degradation. We introduce \textbf{Elastic Threshold Attention (ETA)}, an end-to-end trainable architecture that achieves hardware-accelerated decoding speed without sacrificing dense model quality. ETA predicts dynamic, contextual thresholds directly from query representations, allowing the model to allocate dense-like context to difficult retrieval or reasoning steps while pruning routine tokens. To learn this policy from scratch without representation collapse, ETA \emph{multiplicatively suppresses} sub-threshold logits toward zero during training rather than deleting them. Training against this smooth uniform attention floor provides a distributed probability reservoir that \textbf{causes localized attention sinks on initial tokens to disappear}. It also enables the model to hard-prune uninformative KV blocks at inference time and absorb incidental tokens co-admitted by coarse GPU block selection. As a result, a 1.45B pretrained ETA model rivals dense attention across language modeling, commonsense reasoning, and long-context needle retrieval at $\approx 85\%$ training sparsity and $\approx 38\%$ active decode density. At inference time, we implement a custom decode kernel in Triton that screens KV blocks in $O(1)$ time using cached geometric-probabilistic bounds, delivering up to $2.5\times$ wall-clock decode speedups over FlashAttention-2 on sequences up to 512K tokens. Finally, we introduce an offline calibration algorithm for domain-specific deployments that freezes per-head constant thresholds to eliminate predictor overhead, cutting attention compute by an additional $27\%$.

</details>


### [182] [Bio-MF: Low-Latency and High-Fidelity EEG-to-fNIRS Cross-Modal Generation for Hybrid Motor-Imagery Brain--Computer Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20904)
*Boyuan Zhao,Sifan Zhang,Luping Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出 Bio-MF，一个无隐空间（latent-free）、单步（one-step）的 MeanFlow 生成框架，用于由 EEG 条件生成 fNIRS 信号。方法直接进行信号空间的 x-prediction，并将其转换为 MeanFlow 速度监督，实现单次网络评估的推理，兼顾低延迟与生理结构保真度，服务于混合运动想象脑机接口（MI-BCI）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合 MI-BCI 结合 EEG（电生理）与 fNIRS（血流动力学）信息可优于单模态 EEG 系统，但配对 EEG-fNIRS 采集往往不可得或不便利。EEG-to-fNIRS 跨模态生成因此成为替代方案，然而现有方法生成缓慢且多需预训练，难以满足实时 MI-BCI 需求。一步生成模型虽可降低延迟，但去除迭代细化会损害生成保真度并引入非生理伪影，因此需要一种既快速又能保留任务相关血流动力学结构的生成方案。

Method: Bio-MF 采用无隐空间的单步 MeanFlow 框架进行 EEG 条件 fNIRS 生成：(1) 直接执行信号空间的 x-prediction（而非在隐空间扩散），并将该信号空间输出转化为 MeanFlow 的 velocity 监督；(2) 推理仅需一次网络评估（单步）；(3) 针对异构传感器布局，设计 Spatial-Temporal Interactive 4D Encoding 以保留与任务相关的血流动力学结构；(4) 引入跨模态 classifier-free guidance；(5) 使用 noise-level-gated FFT 正则化以抑制非生理伪影。

Result: 在 Dataset 1 上，EEG + 合成 fNIRS 相比仅用 EEG 使 ACC 分别提升 3.37（HbR）和 4.15（HbO）个百分点；在 Dataset 2 上，于未见过的 64 通道 EEG 导联布局下，增益仍为 2.98（HbR）和 2.50（HbO）个百分点。在 RTX PRO 6000 GPU 上，Bio-MF 生成单个 fNIRS trial 仅需 7.0 ms，相比 1000 步的 SCDM 对应约 857 倍加速。结果表明其可实现快速 EEG-to-fNIRS 合成并保持任务相关的生成质量，支撑下游混合 MI 解码。代码已开源。

Conclusion: Bio-MF 是面向 EEG 条件 fNIRS 生成的无隐空间单步 MeanFlow 框架，通过信号空间 x-prediction 与 MeanFlow 速度监督、时空交互 4D 编码、跨模态 CFG 及噪声级门控 FFT 正则化，在实现毫秒级（7.0 ms）单步生成的同时提升了下游混合 MI 解码精度，并在未见导联布局下具备一定泛化性。其意义在于为实时混合 MI-BCI 提供低延迟、任务相关的跨模态合成路径。局限方面：论文摘要未披露所用数据集规模与训练细节，单步生成在极端条件下的保真度上限及跨被试/跨设备泛化仍待进一步验证。

Abstract: Hybrid motor-imagery brain-computer interfaces (MI-BCIs) combining EEG and fNIRS can outperform EEG-only systems by exploiting complementary electrophysiological and hemodynamic information. To obtain such hybrid information when paired EEG-fNIRS acquisition is unavailable or inconvenient, recent studies have focused on EEG-to-fNIRS cross-modal generation. However, existing methods still suffer from slow generation and often require pretraining, limiting their use in real-time MI-BCI scenarios. Although one-step generative models offer an attractive route to low-latency synthesis, removing the iterative refinement process can reduce generation fidelity and introduce non-physiological artifacts. To address these problems, this paper proposes Bio-MF, a latent-free one-step MeanFlow framework for EEG-conditioned fNIRS generation. Bio-MF performs direct signal-space x-prediction, converts this signal-space output into MeanFlow velocity supervision, and completes inference with one network evaluation. To preserve task-relevant hemodynamic structure under heterogeneous sensor layouts, Bio-MF integrates Spatial-Temporal Interactive 4D Encoding, cross-modal classifier-free guidance, and noise-level-gated FFT regularization. On Dataset 1, EEG + synthetic fNIRS improves ACC over EEG-only by 3.37 and 4.15 percentage points for HbR and HbO, respectively. On Dataset 2, the corresponding gains remain 2.98 and 2.50 percentage points under the unseen 64-channel EEG montage. On an RTX PRO 6000 GPU, Bio-MF generates one fNIRS trial in 7.0 ms, corresponding to an 857x speedup over the 1000-step SCDM latency. These results show that Bio-MF enables fast EEG-to-fNIRS synthesis while preserving task-relevant generation quality for downstream hybrid MI decoding. Our code is available at https://github.com/psychosiwa/Bio-MF.

</details>


### [183] [Continuous Delayed-Memory Stochastic Gradient Descent and Continuous-Time Reinforcement Learning from History of Astrophysical Time Series Studies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20906)
*Debartha Paul,Juncheng Yi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文是一篇综述与理论探索性质的工作，回顾了随机微分方程（SDE）如何结合神经网络参数化来建模类星体光变曲线，并提出 Continuous-Delayed-Memory 随机梯度下降（SGD）与一种连续时间策略梯度的强化学习结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 类星体（Quasars）是宇宙中明亮的天体，其亮度随机波动（stochastic brightness variations）编码了驱动它们的超大质量黑洞（supermassive black holes）的信息。从地面巡天数据得到的时间序列（即光变曲线，light curves）中建模这种波动是一个统计难题，因为观测数据噪声大、采样不规则且过程具有连续的随机动力学性质，传统离散统计方法难以刻画。作者希望借助 SDE 与神经网络参数化的结合来刻画该类连续随机过程，并进一步改进优化与策略学习算法。

Method: 1) 综述层面：梳理历史上 SDE 如何被神经网络参数化（neural network parameterizations）以适应类星体光变曲线建模这一挑战，即用神经网络学习 SDE 的漂移项与扩散项。2) 提出 Continuous-Delayed-Memory SGD：这是一种依赖于离散迭代过程过去状态（past state of the discrete iteration process）的随机梯度下降变体，通过引入延迟记忆项，使优化轨迹带有历史状态依赖。3) 提出一种连续时间策略梯度（continuous time policy gradients）的强化学习结构，用于探索性策略（exploratory policies），其特点是无需求解 HJB（Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman）偏微分方程。作者证明该结构的最优性条件可退化为已有工作中的 Gibbs 策略。

Result: 在若干二维景观（2-dimensional landscape）上进行了仿真实验。结果显示：与 Vanilla SGD 相比，通过调整超参数，Continuous-Delayed-Memory SGD 表现出更强的探索性（wider exploration）和更精确的收敛行为（more precise convergent behavior）。在强化学习部分，作者从理论上展示了所提连续时间策略梯度结构的最优性条件能够恢复（recover）先前工作中的 Gibbs 策略，即两者在最优解上一致。摘要未提及具体的类星体观测数据集、评价指标或定量数值结果。

Conclusion: 本文的贡献在于：一是对 SDE 与神经网络参数化在天体光变曲线建模中的历史演进做了系统性回顾；二是提出 Continuous-Delayed-Memory SGD，通过引入过去状态依赖在二维景观上改善了探索与收敛性质；三是提出无需解 HJB PDE 的连续时间策略梯度强化学习结构，并证明其最优性条件与 Gibbs 策略一致。局限在于：实验仅在合成二维景观上进行，未在真实类星体巡天光变曲线数据上验证；所提 SGD 的收敛性缺乏严格理论保证；强化学习部分主要停留在最优性条件的理论推导，未给出实际任务上的策略性能评估。

Abstract: Quasars are luminous objects in the universe that exhibit stochastic brightness variations encoding information about the supermassive black holes powering them, and modeling these variations from ground-based survey data time series, known as light curves, is a statistical challenge. This paper reviews how stochastic differential equations (SDEs) have been adapted with neural network parameterizations to overcome this challenge in history. We create the Continuous-Delayed-Memory Stochastic Gradient Descent which depend on the past state of the discrete iteration process. We performed the simulation on some 2-dimensional landscape and observed some wider-exploration and more precise convergent behavior compared to Vanilla SGD by adjusting hyperparameters. Besides, we proposed a reinforcement learning structure with continuous time policy gradients for exploratory policies without solving HJB PDE, and we show that its optimality conditions recover the Gibbs policy of previous works.

</details>


### [184] [Do Quantum Models Scale Like LLMs?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20912)
*David S. Berman,Ying-Jer Kao,Roger G. Melko,Alexander G. Stapleton*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究在相互作用里德堡原子阵列投影测量数据上训练的自回归Transformer——RydbergGPT的神经标度律，并利用熵归一化、有限样本校正的两点互信息函数比较里德堡测量与自然语言语料的统计结构。核心发现是：临界点附近的标度行为可由带损失下限修正的幂律刻画，且该处两点函数最接近自然语言，暗示多尺度依赖性对稳定神经标度具有重要作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经标度律（随数据量增加损失按幂律下降）是深度学习的核心经验规律，但其普遍性及背后的数据因素尚不清楚。现有研究多基于自然语言，缺乏对其他具有明确物理结构的复杂数据分布的检验。里德堡原子阵列系统在激光失谐参数变化时呈现有限尺寸临界点，为研究"模型-数据对"的标度行为提供了可控、可解释的物理平台，有助于理解标度律究竟源于模型还是数据统计特性。

Method: 提出RydbergGPT，一个在里德堡原子阵列量子比特投影测量序列上训练的自回归Transformer。将测量数据建模为序列预测任务，通过改变激光失谐参数使其从远离临界点过渡至临界点附近。分析两方面：(1) 以训练数据集规模为变量拟合损失曲线，建模为带损失下限（loss floor）修正的幂律并评估拟合质量；(2) 构造熵归一化、有限样本校正的"两点"互信息函数，分别刻画Rydberg测量数据与自然语言语料的统计结构，并进行跨域对比，以量化多尺度依赖特征。

Result: 实验在不同失谐参数（临界点附近与远离临界点）和不同训练集规模下进行。结果显示：在临界点附近，Transformer损失随训练数据量的变化能够被带损失下限修正的幂律很好地描述；而远离临界点时幂律描述的拟合质量显著下降。两点互信息函数比较表明，临界统计下的两点函数与自然语言中最接近，远离临界点的量子比特构型的函数衰减更快，说明多尺度依赖性较弱的统计结构对应的神经标度更不稳定。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于将神经标度律研究拓展至量子多体测量数据，并提出以熵归一化两点互信息作为连接量子测量统计与自然语言统计的分析工具；实证支持"多尺度依赖有助于稳定神经标度"的假设，并提出标度行为应被视为模型-数据对的联合属性，而非仅由模型决定。局限可能在于：研究基于特定里德堡系统与单一模型规模，样本规模与失谐参数范围有限；两点函数仅为二阶统计，未涵盖更高阶结构；其对自然语言标度律的推论属于相关性支持而非因果证明。

Abstract: In this work, we study the neural scaling laws of RydbergGPT, an autoregressive transformer model trained on qubit projective measurement data gathered from interacting Rydberg atom arrays. The quantum system is known to exhibit a finite-size remnant of a critical point as the laser detuning parameter is varied. We find that near the critical point the transformer loss as a function of training dataset size is well described by a power-law with a loss floor correction. However, away from criticality the quality of the power-law description is substantially reduced. We then compare the statistical structure of both Rydberg measurements and natural-language corpora using an entropy-normalised, finite sample corrected mutual information "two-point" function. We find that near-critical statistics of the two point functions are closest to those observed in natural-language, whilst other qubit configurations far from the critical point have two-point functions that decay more rapidly. This supports the hypothesis that multi-scale dependence contributes to stable neural scaling, and that scaling behaviour should be viewed as a property of the model-data pair.

</details>


### [185] [When AI Reviews Train AI Reviewers: Scientific-Judgment Collapse and Mitigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20942)
*Sy-Tuyen Ho,Minghui Liu,Furong Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究大语言模型（LLM）参与学术评审时的"递归反馈"风险：当后续模型学习由前代模型生成的评审时，会出现评分分布压缩与语义多样性下降的现象，作者称之为"科学判断崩塌"（scientific-judgment collapse），并提出 TrustReviewer 系统，通过训练阶段的语料筛选与测试阶段的配对激活引导（activation steering）来缓解这一问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 越来越多地被用于自动评审或辅助人类评审，模型生成的评审文本会进入公开数据和未来的训练语料，从而可能形成递归循环：后代评审模型会从早期模型产生的判断中学习。这种自我强化的反馈回路可能导致判断同质化、评分分布退化，进而损害学术评审的多样性与可靠性。论文的动机在于刻画该反馈回路中的单步效应，验证"AI 评审递归训练"是否会导致系统性退化，并提供可操作的缓解手段。

Method: 论文以 Llama 3.1 8B 为基座，先在 ICLR 2018–2023 官方评审数据上微调出一个"评审者"模型，再基于 ICLR 2024 数据训练四个后继模型，这些后继模型的训练语料中官方评审与模型生成评审的混合比例被系统性地变化。作者通过评分分布和"同论文级"与"语料级"语义多样性指标来度量合成评审带来的影响。缓解方案 TrustReviewer 在两个阶段介入：(1) 训练时预防——在单阶段训练中，使用经过筛选的高质量语料，减少低质量与语义退化的监督信号；(2) 测试时纠正——采用配对激活引导（paired activation steering），在不再训练、也不依赖额外专家标注的前提下，抑制残余的评判崩塌倾向。

Result: 实验基于 ICLR 官方评审数据（2018–2023 用于初始微调，2024 用于后继模型训练）。主要发现是：引入合成评审后，评分分布被压缩，且同一论文内的语义多样性与整个语料库层面的语义多样性均下降，即出现"科学判断崩塌"现象。TrustReviewer 的训练时语料筛选与测试时配对激活引导被报告为能缓解评判崩塌倾向，并改善推荐结果的对齐性。具体数值指标与定量对比在摘要中未给出。

Conclusion: 论文的主要贡献包括：一是刻画并命名了递归 AI 评审训练中的"科学判断崩塌"现象，即评分分布压缩与语义多样性下降；二是在受控设置下通过混合语料训练后继模型，实证验证了该反馈回路单步效应；三是提出开源的 TrustReviewer 系统，结合训练时语料治理与测试时激活引导，在不额外训练或专家标注的情况下缓解判断退化。其意义在于揭示了 AI 辅助科学评价中的一个具体风险，并提供实用干预。局限方面：摘要中未报告具体量化结果与显著性检验，实验仅基于 ICLR 数据与 Llama 3.1 8B，反馈回路也只研究了一步而非多代递归，方法在其他会议、领域与模型上的泛化性有待进一步验证。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly participate in scientific evaluation, both as automated reviewers and as assistants to human reviewers. As model-generated reviews enter public data and future training corpora, AI peer review can become recursive: later reviewers learn from judgments produced by earlier models. We study one step of this feedback loop in a controlled setting. Starting from Llama 3.1 8B, we first fine-tune a reviewer on official ICLR reviews from 2018--2023 and then train four successor models on ICLR 2024 data with systematically varied mixtures of official and model-generated reviews. Our study shows that introducing synthetic reviews compresses rating distributions and reduces both same-paper and corpus-level semantic diversity. We call this pattern $\textbf{scientific-judgment collapse}$.
  To mitigate this failure mode, we introduce $\textbf{TrustReviewer}$, an open-source LLM-based system for generating peer reviews of AI and machine learning papers. TrustReviewer intervenes at two complementary stages. For training-time prevention, we train the core reviewer in a single stage on a curated corpus designed to reduce low-quality and semantically degenerate supervision. For test-time correction, paired activation steering aims to further mitigate residual tendencies toward collapsed judgments without further training or additional expert annotation. Together, these results characterize a concrete risk of recursive reviewer training and provide practical interventions for preserving judgment diversity and improving recommendation alignment in AI-assisted scientific evaluation.

</details>


### [186] [Efficient Bayes-Adaptive Reinforcement Learning with Temporal Logic Specifications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20954)
*Jonathan Hau,Alessandro Abate*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向线性时序逻辑（LTL）规约的端到端、基于模型的强化学习算法：将LTL任务的极限确定性Büchi自动机（LDBA）与环境的贝叶斯自适应马尔可夫决策过程（BAMDP）同步，并提出一种新的贝叶斯自适应蒙特卡洛规划（BAMCP）算法，在未知环境中实现近似贝叶斯最优的策略综合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在未知环境中满足LTL规约（如安全性、可达性）的策略综合是一个重要问题，广泛适用于机器人与自主系统的任务规划。传统无模型RL方法在处理此类任务时样本效率低，且难以实现有效的探索-利用权衡；非贝叶斯方法亦无法充分利用不确定性信息。作者希望借助贝叶斯RL与模型规划，在未知环境下高效、可靠地综合满足LTL约束的策略，并减少训练中的违规行为。

Method: 核心创新包括三部分：(1) 将LTL任务的LDBA表示与环境的BAMDP表示进行同步（synchronisation），构造统一的决策过程，从而以贝叶斯方式建模环境不确定性并借助贝叶斯RL改善探索-利用权衡；(2) 提出新的Bayes-Adaptive Monte-Carlo Planning（BAMCP）算法，在该同步BAMDP结构上进行近似贝叶斯最优规划；(3) 将方法与传统BAMCP进行对比，并应用于"谨慎"RL（cautious RL）场景。整体为端到端、基于模型的RL框架，区别于传统无模型方法。

Result: 实验涵盖有限时域与无限时域任务，评估指标包括LTL性质满足情况与样本效率。结果表明，与传统的无模型方法相比，所提方法在性质满足和样本效率上均更优。消融研究对比了本文提出的BAMCP与经典BAMCP，凸显了新算法在LTL任务满足上的价值。此外，作者展示了该框架在谨慎RL中的成功应用，即减少策略训练过程中的任务违规次数。

Conclusion: 本文的主要贡献是将LDBA与BAMDP同步，并引入新型BAMCP算法，实现未知环境下满足LTL规约的近似贝叶斯最优策略综合，提升了性质满足率与样本效率，并支持谨慎RL以减少训练违规。潜在局限可能在于：结果主要来自抽象或有限规模任务实验，方法的可扩展性与在真实高维复杂系统中的适用性尚未充分验证；BAMCP的近似规划在大规模问题上可能存在计算开销。

Abstract: We present a novel end-to-end model-based Reinforcement Learning (RL) algorithm for efficient policy synthesis under given Linear Temporal Logic (LTL) specifications (e.g., safety or reachability) in unknown environments. To do so, a Limit-Deterministic B{ü}chi Automaton (LDBA) representation of the LTL task is synchronised with a Bayes-Adaptive Markov Decision Process (BAMDP) representation of the environment, which allows us to leverage an enhanced exploration-exploitation trade-off that is achieved via Bayesian RL, as opposed to traditional non-Bayesian approaches. We further propose a novel Bayes-Adaptive Monte-Carlo Planning (BAMCP) algorithm to allow for approximate Bayes-optimal strategy synthesis in the synchronised BAMDP construct. A range of finite- and infinite-horizon task experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach in terms of both property satisfaction and sample efficiency, when compared to traditional model-free approaches. Additional ablation studies also successfully highlight the value of the novel BAMCP algorithm in comparison to classical BAMCP for LTL task satisfaction. Finally, we also showcase a successful application of our approach for \textit{cautious} RL, namely to reduce the number of task violations incurred during policy training.

</details>


### [187] [From Switching to Dynamic Regret: A Simple Reduction via Unbiased Random Sequences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20968)
*Yibo Wang,Wenhao Yang,Sifan Yang,Yuanyu Wan,Lijun Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个简洁的归约框架，将非平稳在线学习中的动态遗憾最小化问题归约为切换遗憾（switching regret）最小化问题，从而借助现成的切换遗憾算法直接获得动态遗憾界，并在强凸、指数凹与一般凸损失下分别达到极小极大最优界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在非平稳在线学习中，动态遗憾衡量学习器相对于随时间变化的比较器序列的表现。尽管已有诸多进展，但针对强凸（strongly convex）和指数凹（exp-concave）损失获得最优界通常需要复杂且紧耦合的分析。本文的动机是寻找一种更简单、更通用的分析途径：如果能将动态遗憾归约为已有成熟保证的切换遗憾，便可复用现成算法及其界，避免繁琐的定制化分析，并统一处理多种损失类型。

Method: 核心思想是构造性归约：对任意比较器序列，构建一个每轮无偏（unbiased at each round）的辅助随机序列，同时控制其方差与切换次数。结合合适的代理损失（surrogate losses），将动态遗憾分解为「相对于该随机序列的期望切换遗憾」与「其受控方差」两部分。这样，任何具有切换遗憾保证的现成算法都可直接转用于动态遗憾最小化。该框架不依赖针对特定损失函数的复杂分析，而是通过归约通用地导出界。

Result: 理论结果：对强凸与指数凹损失，建立 $\widetilde{O}(T^{1/3}P_T^{2/3})$ 的动态遗憾界，其中 $T$ 为时间步长，$P_T$ 为比较器序列的路径长度（path-length）。对一般凸损失，同一归约恢复出 $O(\sqrt{T(1+P_T)})$ 的动态遗憾界。所有结果均与这三类损失的极小极大最优（minimax optimal）结果相匹配。论文以理论分析为主，未涉及具体数据集实验。

Conclusion: 主要贡献是提出一个统一的、简单的归约框架，将动态遗憾归约到切换遗憾，从而借助现成算法获得最优动态遗憾界，覆盖强凸、指数凹和一般凸三类损失。其意义在于简化了已有复杂分析并增强了通用性。潜在局限在于：结果以理论界为主，缺乏实证验证；归约的有效性依赖于代理损失选择与随机序列方差、切换次数的可控构造；且未明确讨论非凸损失或更复杂约束下的适用性。

Abstract: In non-stationary online learning, dynamic regret has attracted increasing attention as a measure of how well an online learner performs against a time-varying comparator sequence. Despite considerable advances, attaining optimal bounds for strongly convex and exp-concave losses often involves intricate analysis. In this paper, we present a \textit{simple} framework that reduces dynamic regret minimization to switching regret minimization. As a result, we can derive dynamic regret bounds by using off-the-shelf algorithms with switching regret guarantees. The key idea of our reduction is to construct, for \textit{any} comparator sequence, an auxiliary random sequence that is unbiased at each round, with the controlled variance and a manageable number of switches. Combining this construction with suitable surrogate losses, we can decompose dynamic regret into the expected switching regret against the random sequence and its controlled variance. Theoretically, for strongly convex and exp-concave losses, we establish the $\widetilde{O}(T^{1/3}P_T^{2/3})$ dynamic regret bounds, where $T$ denotes the time horizon and $P_T$ denotes the path-length of the comparator sequence. Moreover, for general convex losses, the same reduction also recovers the $O(\sqrt{T(1+P_T)})$ dynamic regret bound. Notably, all our findings match the minimax optimal results for these three types of losses, highlighting the versatility of our proposed framework.

</details>


### [188] [Generative inversion for early ranking of competing geologic interpretations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20978)
*Harun Ur Rashid,Daniel O'Malley*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种将竞争性地质解释转化为"替代性空间先验"并依据水头观测进行排序的工作流。核心理念是用生成式基础模型把地质解释文本化，借助变分自编码器构建解释专属隐空间，再通过监督逆网络将水头观测映射到该隐空间，从而定量化地评估哪种地质解释最符合观测数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高风险的次地表（subsurface）决策常在数据极度稀缺的条件下做出。专家对同一地下系统可能提出相互竞争的解释，但在项目早期几乎没有实用手段判断哪种解释最符合真实情况。这种不确定性往往要等到钻探多口井之后才能消除，代价高达数百万美元。现有评估地质解释的方法要么依赖主观判断，要么依赖早期勘探中很少能获得的密集数据。因此，如何在数据稀缺的早期阶段，客观地对竞争性地质解释进行排序，是一个重要且具有经济价值的问题。

Method: 工作流将每种竞争性地质解释转化为一个替代性空间先验，并依据其与水头观测的一致性进行排序。具体步骤为：(1) 对每种解释，使用文本到图像（text-to-image）基础模型生成 1600 张地质图像的集合；(2) 单独训练一个变分自编码器（VAE），为该解释提供专属的隐表示（interpretation-specific latent representation）；(3) 训练一个监督式逆网络（inverse network），将水头观测映射到该隐空间；(4) 用冻结的 VAE 解码器生成图像，并将其映射为对数渗透率（log-conductivity）场；(5) 进行稳态流动模拟得到预测水头，把观测与预测的失配转化为高斯形式的兼容性得分（compatibility score），据此排序各解释。关键创新在于结合生成式基础模型的空间先验、VAE 隐空间以及物理模拟的失配度量。

Result: 在基于 Johansen 组（Johansen Formation）的合成基准上，作者构建了三种与参考表示一致性递减的地质解释。在 925 个测试案例上，平均水头 RMSE 由"精确且准确"（Precise & Accurate）解释的 0.197，升至"准确"（Accurate）解释的 0.227，再到"不匹配"（Mismatched）解释的 0.280，表明框架能按预期区分不同一致性水平的解释。随后将工作流应用于 Waste Isolation Pilot Plant 处 Culebra Dolomite Member 的两个已发表概念模型：修订后的模型获得 0.991 的兼容性权重，而原始模型仅为 0.009，与独立证据一致。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于提出了一条从地质解释文本到水头兼容性评分的完整可计算流程，将生成式基础模型、VAE 隐表示与逆问题求解、稳态流动模拟相结合，为数据稀缺条件下的地质解释评估提供了定量排序手段。合成基准与实际场址案例（Culebra Dolomite）均显示其能区分解释质量。局限方面：评估主要基于合成基准和单个真实场址案例，真实泛化性有限；结果依赖 text-to-image 生成图像的质量、VAE 隐空间的表达能力以及逆网络的训练；兼容性得分基于稳态流动假设且以高斯形式建模失配，可能无法覆盖瞬态或更复杂的地质情形。论文与事件相机、SNN 或小目标检测等主题无关。

Abstract: High-consequence subsurface decisions are often made under severe data scarcity. Experts may arrive at competing interpretations of the same subsurface system, yet early in a project there is rarely a practical way to determine which one is most realistic. This uncertainty can persist until several wells are drilled, often costing millions of dollars. Existing approaches for evaluating geologic interpretations rely either on subjective judgment or on dense data that are rarely available in early-stage investigations. We present a workflow that addresses this challenge by translating competing geologic interpretations into alternative spatial priors and ranking them according to their consistency with hydraulic-head observations. For each interpretation, a text-to-image foundation model generates an ensemble of 1600 geologic images, and a separately trained variational autoencoder provides an interpretation-specific latent representation. A supervised inverse network maps the head observations into this latent space, and the frozen decoder produces an image that is mapped to a log-conductivity field. Steady-state flow simulation then provides predicted heads, and the resulting mismatch is converted into a Gaussian-form compatibility score. We evaluate the framework using a synthetic benchmark based on the Johansen Formation and three interpretations of decreasing consistency with the reference representation. Across 925 test cases, the mean head RMSE increases from 0.197 for the Precise \& Accurate interpretation to 0.227 for the Accurate interpretation and 0.280 for the Mismatched interpretation. We subsequently apply the workflow to two published conceptual models of the Culebra Dolomite Member at the Waste Isolation Pilot Plant. The revised model receives a compatibility weight of 0.991, compared with 0.009 for the original model, consistent with the independent evidence.

</details>


### [189] [ASGARD: Action-Space Guard for UAV Resilience via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20982)
*Mohsen Salehi,Karthik Pattabiraman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ASGARD 是一种面向 RL 无人机控制器的两阶段"教师-学生"防御框架，专门应对运行时动作空间攻击（即在策略生成动作之后、执行器执行之前篡改动作指令）。教师阶段利用包含攻击相关特权信息的编码器训练策略与动作监控器；学生阶段通过监督学习将编码器与监控器蒸馏到仅依赖无人机物理状态历史的机载模型，从而在运行时无需特权信息即可纠正被污染的动作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RL 控制器已被广泛用于无人机导航与控制，但其动作空间攻击面常被忽视：攻击者可在策略输出动作后、执行器执行前覆盖动作指令，直接操控飞行行为。现有防御大多针对策略输入端（观测/状态）的攻击，而少数处理动作空间攻击的方法依赖训练时重新训练策略，无法在运行时对实时被篡改的动作保持鲁棒。因此，需要一种能在运行时不依赖攻击特权信息、仍可纠正受损动作的部署方案。

Method: ASGARD 采用两阶段教师-学生流水线。教师阶段：一个编码器将无人机物理状态与"动作攻击相关的特权信息"（privileged information）联合编码为动作攻击感知的隐表示（action-attack-aware latent），该隐表示一方面用于训练 RL 控制策略，另一方面驱动一个监控器（monitor）生成修正后的动作指令输出至执行器。学生阶段：编码器与监控器均以教师对应模块为监督信号进行监督学习，使其在机载运行时仅依赖无人机物理状态历史（不使用特权信息），从而完成从教师到可部署学生模型的迁移。

Result: 论文在针对无人机不同动作指令的攻击场景下对 ASGARD 进行了评估。结果表明：ASGARD 对动作空间攻击具有鲁棒性，即使遭受攻击仍能完成任务；并能泛化到未见过的攻击类型；对隐蔽型（stealthy）攻击同样保持鲁棒。

Conclusion: ASGARD 的主要贡献在于提出运行时动作空间攻击的防御范式，并通过特权信息教师-学生蒸馏实现仅依赖物理状态历史的机载部署，兼具对未见攻击与隐蔽攻击的泛化鲁棒性。其意义在于将动作命令层面的安全性引入 RL 无人机控制。潜在局限包括：依赖教师阶段可获得攻击相关特权信息，学生性能受蒸馏质量制约；评估主要基于特定攻击场景与动作命令类型，在真实飞行平台、更广泛攻击模式与实时算力约束下的表现仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) controllers have been recently adopted for Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAV) navigation and control. However, they are susceptible to action-space attacks that overwrite the action commands after the policy generates them and before the actuators execute them. While most existing defenses target attacks on the policy's inputs, those addressing action-space attacks retrain the policy at training time and are not resilient to corrupted actions at runtime. We propose ASGARD, a two-phase teacher-student pipeline for making RL-based UAV control resilient to action-space attacks. In the teacher phase, an encoder combines the UAV's physical state with action-attack-related privileged information to produce an action-attack-aware latent that trains the RL control policy and a monitor that outputs corrected action commands to the actuators. In the student phase, both the encoder and the monitor are trained via supervised learning from their teacher counterparts to run on-board using only the UAV's physical state history. We evaluate ASGARD across attack scenarios targeting different action commands on UAV. We find that ASGARD is resilient to action-space attacks and completes the missions despite the attack. We further find that ASGARD generalizes to unseen attacks and remains resilient against stealthy attacks.

</details>


### [190] [From Stress to Affect: Multimodal Deep Learning for Physiological Emotion Recognition Across Wearable Sensor Modalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20991)
*Desta Haileselassie Hagos,Saurav Keshari Aryal,Legand L. Burge*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对用于可穿戴生理信号情感识别的三种时序深度学习架构（双向LSTM、TCN、Transformer）进行系统性对比研究，在WESAD与EmoWear两个多模态数据集上，跨手腕、胸部及多模态传感配置，采用LOSO-CV评估，考察集成、传感器消融、采样频率与显著性分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生理情感识别在心理健康监测、情感计算与人机交互中具有重要应用价值。但既有研究通常只评估单一模型、单一传感配置或单一数据集，难以揭示"架构×传感模态×数据集"等因素如何共同影响识别性能，缺乏对选型决策的系统性指导。

Method: 工作为对比性实证研究而非提出新模型。选取三类时序架构：双向LSTM（循环建模）、TCN（卷积感受野建模）与Transformer（自注意力建模）。在WESAD和EmoWear两个多模态可穿戴数据集上，分别配置手腕-only、胸部-only与多模态输入，采用被试独立的留一被试交叉验证（LOSO-CV）以保证泛化评估的严谨性。此外还分析soft-voting集成、传感器消融、采样频率影响，并借助基于梯度的显著性（saliency）进行可解释性分析。

Result: WESAD上Transformer取得最高多模态准确率99.02%±0.51%；EmoWear上LSTM在arousal（91.80%±1.06%）与valence（89.96%±0.36%）均获最佳多模态结果。多模态传感在两数据集上一致优于手腕或胸部单模态配置。采样频率分析表明4 Hz是实用工作点，性能与更高采样率相当但训练成本显著降低。核心发现：架构的相对优劣取决于数据集特性，不存在普遍最优的单一架构。

Conclusion: 该研究为可穿戴生理情感识别中架构、传感模态与采样频率的选型提供了实证依据，强调需按数据集特性与多模态融合进行决策，并指出低频采样的可行性。局限在于结论基于两个数据集、模型主要为对比而非创新，且未在事件相机/神经形态或小目标视觉等方向展开，属生理信号时序建模范畴。

Abstract: Physiological emotion recognition using wearable sensors has important applications in mental health monitoring, affective computing, and human-computer interaction. However, existing studies typically evaluate a single model, sensing configuration, or dataset, limiting our understanding of how these factors influence recognition performance. We present a comparative study of temporal deep learning architectures for physiological emotion recognition using two multimodal wearable datasets: WESAD and EmoWear. Bidirectional long short-term memory (LSTM), temporal convolutional network (TCN), and Transformer models are evaluated under wrist-only, chest-only, and multimodal sensing configurations using participant-independent leave-one-subject-out cross-validation (LOSO-CV). We also investigate soft-voting ensembles, sensor ablation, sampling frequency, and gradient-based saliency. The Transformer achieved the highest multimodal accuracy on WESAD (99.02% +/- 0.51%), whereas the LSTM achieved the best multimodal accuracy on EmoWear for both arousal (91.80% +/- 1.06%) and valence (89.96% +/- 0.36%). These results show that relative architecture performance depends on dataset characteristics rather than one architecture being uniformly superior. Multimodal sensing consistently outperformed wrist-only and chest-only configurations across both datasets. Sampling-frequency analysis showed that 4 Hz provides a practical operating point, with performance comparable to higher frequencies at substantially lower training cost. These findings provide guidance for selecting architectures, sensing modalities, and sampling frequencies for wearable physiological emotion recognition.

</details>


### [191] [MOSAIC-SR: Transformer-Guided Symbolic Regression for Scientific Equation Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20997)
*Peiyi Zheng,Yanming Kang,Hans De Sterck,Giang Tran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MOSAIC-SR 将预训练 Transformer 用于符号回归，先用其生成多个表达式"草图"作为搜索起点，再通过尺度感知的常数优化与局部符号修复进行联合结构-常数搜索，从而兼顾灵活的结构搜索与高效推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 符号回归的目标是从观测数据中恢复闭式方程，以获得可解释、可用于科学发现的模型。现有方法存在两难：基于搜索的方法能精修表达式结构，但依赖昂贵的组合优化，且常从随机初始化出发，在高维表达式空间中效率低下；预训练神经模型虽能近乎即时生成公式，但预测常含符号性错误。论文希望结合二者的优势——用学习到的先验把搜索聚焦到有希望的表达式结构上，同时保留搜索对结构和常数的精确修正能力。

Method: MOSAIC-SR 采用"神经提议+搜索精修"的两阶段框架：1）用预训练 Transformer 一次性提出多个初始草图（initial sketches），相当于在巨大表达式空间中给出多个有前景的搜索区域，避免随机起点；2）从每个草图出发执行搜索，联合恢复结构并优化常数。关键创新在于两个组件：尺度感知的常数优化（scale-aware constant optimization，处理不同量级常数/系数的拟合问题）与局部符号修复（local symbolic repair，对草图中有误的符号结构进行局部纠正）。整体思路是把神经先验用于搜索初始化，把数值优化与符号修复用于最终解的精确恢复。

Result: 实验在 SRSD-Feynman 数据集（含与不含 dummy 变量的两种设定）以及六个额外基准上进行，评估指标包括符号解率（symbolic solution rate，即完全恢复正确表达式）与预测精度。结果显示：MOSAIC-SR 在所有数据集上取得最高的符号解率，同时在预测精度上位居前二。在引入无关 dummy 输入的情况下，这一优势依然保持，说明方法对冗余/干扰变量具有一定鲁棒性。消融性结论指出常数数值优化与符号修复对正确恢复表达式均有重要贡献。

Conclusion: MOSAIC-SR 的核心贡献在于验证了"学习到的先验可有效引导搜索聚焦于有希望的方程结构"，并证明数值优化与符号修复是精确恢复表达式的关键环节，从而在符号解率和预测精度两方面同时取得竞争力。其意义在于把神经符号回归的即时生成能力与搜索方法的可靠性结合，提升科学发现场景下的可解释建模能力。潜在局限包括：性能仍依赖预训练 Transformer 的草图质量（若草图未覆盖正确结构，搜索可能难以补救）；符号解率与预测精度之间存在权衡（精度并非始终最优）；方法在含 dummy 变量时虽保持优势，但整体对于噪声、高维输入与复杂算符集合的可扩展性尚未在摘要中充分说明。

Abstract: Symbolic regression aims to recover closed-form equations from observations, providing interpretable models for scientific discovery. Existing approaches struggle to combine flexible structural search with efficient inference. Search-based methods can refine expression structure but often rely on costly combinatorial optimization with random initialization. Pretrained neural models generate formulas almost instantly, but their predictions often contain symbolic errors. We introduce MOSAIC-SR, which uses a pretrained Transformer to propose multiple initial sketches. These sketches initialize searches in several promising regions, avoiding random starts in the vast expression space. Each search jointly recovers structure and constants through scale-aware constant optimization and local symbolic repair. We evaluate MOSAIC-SR on the SRSD-Feynman dataset with and without dummy variables and on six additional benchmarks. MOSAIC-SR obtains the highest symbolic solution rate on every dataset while ranking among the top two methods in predictive accuracy. This advantage persists in the presence of irrelevant dummy inputs. The results show that learned priors can focus search on promising equation structures, and that numerical optimization and symbolic repair are important for recovery.

</details>


### [192] [On the Limits of Maximal Coding Rate Reduction for Out-of-Distribution Generalisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21001)
*Menghui Zhou,Gaoshan Bi,Vitaveska Lanfranchi,Po Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从信息论与表示学习的角度，严格分析了最大编码率降低（$\mathrm{MCR}^{2}$）目标在分布偏移（OOD）下的泛化局限，证明编码最优化与预测稳定性之间存在根本性的解耦，并指出单靠 $\mathrm{MCR}^{2}$ 或直接引入不变性原理（IRM/REx）都不足以保证 OOD 预测可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: $\mathrm{MCR}^{2}$ 被提出作为一种可从信息论角度学习类别子流形结构化判别表示的白盒/可解释框架，旨在提升学习系统的安全性、鲁棒性与泛化能力。然而作者观察到 $\mathrm{MCR}^{2}$ 在分布偏移下可能完全失效，因此需要厘清其在 OOD 泛化上的理论边界。该问题重要在于：若所谓"可解释、结构化"的编码目标本身无法保证跨环境稳定预测，则基于该目标构建的白盒架构在真实世界（环境相关特征易发生反转）应用中的可信度将被削弱。

Method: 论文采用理论构造与反例分析的方法，而非提出新模型。核心贡献是建立 $\mathrm{MCR}^{2}$ 在 OOD 泛化上的两个理论局限：(1) 构造 exact-optimum 反例：一种完全依赖不稳定环境特征的表示可达到全局编码最优，但在环境特征相关性反转后预测完全失败，即便数据中存在完美的稳定特征；该反例甚至包含训练时不可能出现的测试输入。进一步地，即使所有测试输入都可能在训练中出现，编码质量可任意接近最优而预测误差可任意接近 100%。(2) 证明直接引入 IRM/REx 背后的不变性原理并不能消除该失效：失败表示在多个训练环境上共享同一最优编码算子，说明编码最优性的共享不蕴含预测的稳定性。

Result: 本文为纯理论分析，未进行传统意义上的数据集实验或定量指标评测。所依赖的"实验"形式是构造性反例与极限分析：(a) 展示存在达到 $\mathrm{MCR}^{2}$ 全局最优但发生相关性反转后完全失败的表示；(b) 通过构造给出编码质量趋近最优而预测误差趋近 100% 的情形；(c) 论证该失败表示在训练环境间具有相同的最优编码算子，从而说明 IRM/REx 风格的不变性约束不足以提供可靠 OOD 保证。主要发现是 $\mathrm{MCR}^{2}$ 的编码最优性与预测稳定性之间存在解耦。

Conclusion: 主要贡献：系统刻画了 $\mathrm{MCR}^{2}$ 的 OOD 泛化极限，指出编码最优化并不保证跨环境稳定预测，并证明引入不变性原理（IRM/REx）无法自动修复该问题，从而呼吁需要新的假设或学习原则来建立跨环境的稳定预测关系。意义：为基于信息论目标构建可解释白盒架构的研究提供了重要的负面理论证据，提示社区不能将"结构化/最优编码"等同于"鲁棒泛化"。局限：分析聚焦于 $\mathrm{MCR}^{2}$ 及不变性原理的理论构造，未给出可行的修复方案或实证验证；所得结论依赖特定构造条件，如何在实际数据与架构上量化和缓解该失效仍需进一步研究。

Abstract: Substantial efforts have been devoted to making deep learning objectives, representations, and architectures interpretable, with the goal of improving the safety, robustness, and generalisation of learning systems in diverse real-world applications. The recently proposed maximal coding rate reduction ($\mathrm{MCR}^{2}$) offers a promising information-theoretic framework for learning structured, discriminative representations of class-wise submanifolds and has inspired interpretable white-box architectures. However, we observe that $\mathrm{MCR}^{2}$ can completely fail under distribution shift, motivating our study of its out-of-distribution (OOD) generalisation limits. We establish two limitations of $\mathrm{MCR}^{2}$ for OOD generalisation. First, the $\mathrm{MCR}^{2}$ objective alone can admit complete prediction failure: a representation based entirely on unstable environmental features can achieve the global coding optimum yet fail completely after correlation reversal, despite an available perfectly stable feature. This exact-optimum example includes test inputs that cannot occur during training. Even when every possible test input can also occur during training, coding quality can be arbitrarily close to optimal while prediction error is arbitrarily close to 100%. Second, directly incorporating the invariance principle underlying widely successful invariant risk minimisation (IRM) and risk extrapolation (REx) does not eliminate this failure. The failing representation admits the same optimal coding operator across training environments, showing that shared coding optimality does not ensure stable prediction. Reliable OOD guarantees for $\mathrm{MCR}^{2}$ therefore require additional new assumptions or learning principles that establish stable predictive relationships across environments.

</details>


### [193] [Scaling Discovery through Test-Time Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21032)
*Jongho Park,Vasilis Kontonis,Shivam Garg,Akshay Krishnamurthy,Dimitris Papailiopoulos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究测试时（test-time）多智能体通信对困难任务求解能力的影响，发现让多个无预设分工的智能体通过共享目录进行通信协作，可显著超越独立并行尝试。团队规模为 k 的通信智能体（team@k）在 ARC-AGI-3 上达到约 4k 个独立智能体的成功率，且优势随规模增大而增强，并能迁移到研究型任务，甚至超越已知最佳人类解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学进步依赖协作，但现有多智能体系统几乎没有捕捉到这一协作本质；通信是否真正有助于智能体提升表现，此前研究结果混杂、结论不明。为此，作者希望系统性地研究"测试时通信"的扩展规律（scaling），即在不预设角色、仅通过共享目录通信的设置下，通信能否带来超越独立并行采样（independent parallel attempts / best@k）的性能提升，并检验这种增益是否能突破单个智能体的能力上限。

Method: 核心设计是 team@k：k 个智能体，无预先规定的角色分工，通过一个共享目录进行通信协作，可在测试时交换中间进展与"关键突破"。对比基线为独立并行采样的 best@k 与 4k 个独立智能体。研究在 ARC-AGI-3（需要新颖问题求解的基准）上做扩展性实验，测量其成功率随 k 的增长规律，并进一步将方法迁移到多联骨牌打包（polyomino packing）与 MNIST 分类器压缩等研究导向任务。文中还探讨了通信增益的边界条件，即计算预算与反馈清晰度对通信有效性的影响。

Result: 在 ARC-AGI-3 上，team@k 的成功率约等于 4k 个独立智能体的成功率，且该优势随 k 增大而增强，说明增益可随规模累积；更重要的是，存在单个智能体无法解决、但智能体团队能可靠完成的任务。在 polyomino packing 上，通信智能体优于 best@k 并超过此前已知最佳分数；在 MNIST 分类器压缩上，四个智能体产出一个 1,957 字节的分类器提交，测试准确率达 99.4%，小于已知最佳人类解与最佳单智能体结果。但增益并非无条件：在计算受限或缺乏明确进展度量时，独立智能体可能反而更优；只有计算充足且反馈清晰时，通信才稳定带来更强结果。

Conclusion: 论文主要贡献在于：证明测试时通信可大幅超越独立并行尝试，揭示了 team@k 相对 best@k/4k 独立智能体的扩展优势，并展示其在 ARC-AGI-3、组合优化（polyomino packing）与模型压缩（MNIST 分类器）任务上的迁移性与超越人类最佳解的能力。其意义在于为多智能体协作式推理提供了可量化的扩展证据，并刻画了增益成立的条件。局限在于结论仅在"计算充足 + 反馈明确"的场景下成立，计算受限或缺乏清晰进展度量时通信可能失效；同时评测主要集中在这几类任务，通用性仍待更广泛验证。

Abstract: Science advances not in isolation but through collaboration, yet existing agentic systems capture little of this. Whether communicating agents help remains an open question with mixed prior results. We show that test-time communication can substantially outperform independent parallel attempts on challenging tasks, where sharing a breakthrough can push the whole group forward. We first study the effect of scaling multi-agent test-time communication, where agents have no predefined roles and communicate via a shared directory, on ARC-AGI-3, a benchmark requiring novel problem solving. We find that a team of $k$ communicating agents, team@$k$, matches the success rate of $4k$ independent agents, and this advantage grows with $k$, suggesting gains compound with scale. The effect is not merely efficiency: a task that no single agent can solve, a team of agents can solve reliably. Furthermore, these gains transfer to research-oriented tasks, given sufficient compute. On polyomino packing, communicating agents outperform best@$k$ and exceed the prior best-known score. On MNIST classifier compression, communication surpasses the best-known human solution. A team of four agents produced a 1,957-byte classifier submission achieving 99.4% test accuracy, smaller than both the best-known human solution and the best single-agent result. These gains are not unconditional. Independent agents may outperform communication when compute is limited or when a clear measure of progress is absent. However, under sufficient compute and clear feedback, multi-agent communication consistently yields stronger results.

</details>


### [194] [Stiefel-AdamW: Geometry-Aware AdamW for Linear Factorization Blocks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21039)
*Emanuele Zangrando,Marco Sutti,Francesco Tudisco*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Stiefel-AdamW，一种针对深度学习中"线性因子分解块"（W=BA）的优化器，通过将一个因子约束在 Stiefel 流形上、另一个保持欧氏空间，把 GL(r) 规范对称性松弛为紧正交对称性，从而消除因子爆炸问题，同时保留 AdamW 的逐坐标对角预条件特性。在 LoRA 微调和全预训练上均取得稳定提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性因子分解块（两个参数矩阵直接相乘、无非线性间隔）广泛出现在 LoRA 适配器、低秩压缩层、自注意力的 query-key 乘积中。这类结构存在共性病症：因子分解是非唯一的（例如 BA 与 (BQ)(Q^{-1}A) 等价），导致优化景观存在大量等价解，容易引发"因子爆炸"（factor blow-up），使训练不稳定并限制可用学习率。然而现有做法通常沿用标准欧氏优化器（如 AdamW），完全忽略了该结构内在的几何性质。论文旨在不牺牲 AdamW 实用优势的前提下，引入流形几何来稳定这类块的训练。

Method: 核心思路：对线性因子块 W=BA，将其中一个因子约束在 Stiefel 流形（正交矩阵集合）上，另一个因子保持无约束的欧氏变量。这一约束把原本的完整 GL(R^r) 规范（gauge）对称性收缩为紧致的正交对称性，理论上排除了因子幅度无界增长的"blow-up"路径。算法层面采用"几何最小侵入"设计：一阶/二阶矩估计（Adam 的动量与方差）仍在环绕的欧氏空间中进行，几何信息仅通过两个操作进入——切空间投影（tangent-space projection）与流形回缩（manifold retraction）。因此 Stiefel-AdamW 可视为 AdamW 的近似即插即用替代，实现开销极小。论文进一步给出该优化器既继承 Riemannian 方法的稳定性优势，又保有标准收敛保证的理论分析。

Result: 实验覆盖两类场景：（1）LoRA 风格的微调，模型包括 GPT2、ViT 和 Mistral 7B；（2）GPT2 在 OpenWebText 上的全量预训练。评价以训练稳定性与性能指标为主。主要发现：Stiefel-AdamW 相对强基线（标准 AdamW）取得一致性的性能改进，且相比 AdamW 几乎不产生额外计算成本；同时缓解了因子爆炸、允许使用更稳定的学习率。

Conclusion: 贡献在于识别出线性因子分解块的"非唯一性/规范对称性"这一被忽视的结构性病症，并给出一个理论上有依据、工程上近零开销的优化器方案：以 Stiefel 流形约束换取紧对称性，兼顾 AdamW 的对角预条件强度与 Riemannian 优化的稳定性。意义在于可普遍用于 LoRA、低秩压缩、注意力 QK 乘积等广泛存在的结构。局限方面：论文未详细展开对超参（如学习率、流形步长/回缩策略）敏感性的分析，也未说明在更大规模模型或更长训练下的可扩展性，以及约束单一因子对表达能力是否带来潜在影响。

Abstract: A pervasive structural pattern in modern deep learning is the linear factorization block: a submodule of the form $W = BA$ in which two parameter matrices are multiplied directly, with no intervening nonlinearity. Such blocks appear in LoRA adapters, low-rank compressed layers, query-key products of self-attention, and share a common pathology: the factorization is non-unique, which can destabilize training and limit usable learning rates. Despite this, factorization blocks are typically optimized with standard Euclidean methods that ignore the underlying geometry. We introduce Stiefel-AdamW, a near drop-in replacement for AdamW for use wherever such blocks appear. By constraining one factor on the Stiefel manifold while leaving the other Euclidean, Stiefel-AdamW relaxes the full $\mathrm{GL}(\mathbb{R}^r)$ gauge symmetry to a compact orthogonal symmetry, ruling out factor blow-up while retaining the coordinate-wise diagonal preconditioning that gives AdamW its practical strength. Moment estimation is performed in the ambient Euclidean space, with geometry entering only through a tangent-space projection and a manifold retraction. The implementation overhead over AdamW is minimal, and we show that the resulting optimizer inherits both the stability benefits of Riemannian methods and standard convergence guarantees.
